Algorithmic Monocultures in Hiring
Authors: Rishi Bommasani, Sarah H. Bana, Kathleen A. Creel, Dan Jurafsky, Percy Liang
First: 2026-05-26T17:59:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:59:55+00:00
Comments: Published at FAccT 2026. Website: https://algorithmichiring.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Many employers screen job applicants with algorithms built by the same few algorithm vendors. We hypothesize that algorithmic monoculture leads to the same individuals and members of the same racial groups facing rejection. We acquire and analyze a novel dataset of 3 million applicants submitting 4 million applications where all the applications are screened by algorithms built by the same vendor. We find clear racial disparities in applicant outcomes. Of all applications submitted by Asian and Black applicants, 14.74% and 25.87% are submitted to positions that adversely impact Asian and Black applicants, respectively, according to U.S. employment discrimination standards. Individuals also receive homogeneous outcomes: 4% of all applicants who apply to 10 positions are recommended for rejection from all positions, a rate higher than expected by chance. To better understand this homogeneity, we leverage the deterministic replicability of hiring algorithms to generate the outcomes applicants would have received if they applied to all positions. We show that applicants would need to apply widely in order to ensure their applications are considered by a human
中文标题/摘要
标题：招聘中的算法单一化
许多雇主使用由少数几家算法供应商开发的算法筛选求职者。我们假设算法单一化会导致相同个体及同一种族群体面临相似的拒绝。我们获取并分析了一个包含300万申请人提交400万份申请的新型数据集，所有申请均由同一供应商的算法筛选。我们发现申请结果存在明显的种族差异：根据美国就业歧视标准，亚裔和非裔申请人的申请中，分别有14.74%和25.87%被分配到对其不利的职位。申请人也获得同质化结果：4%的申请人申请10个职位时，被全部推荐拒绝，这一比例高于随机预期。为更好地理解这种同质性，我们利用招聘算法的确定性可重复性，模拟申请人若申请所有职位将获得的结果。我们表明，申请人需广泛申请，才能确保其申请被人类评审。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how algorithmic monoculture in hiring—where employers rely on algorithms from the same vendor—leads to systemic bias and homogeneity in applicant outcomes. Using a dataset of 3 million applicants and 4 million applications all processed by a single vendor’s algorithm, researchers found significant racial disparities: 14.74% of Asian applicants and 25.87% of Black applicants were rejected in roles deemed adverse under U.S. employment discrimination standards. Additionally, 4% of applicants received rejection recommendations across all 10 positions they applied to, a rate statistically higher than chance, indicating algorithmic homogeneity. The study further demonstrates that applicants would need to apply to a wide variety of positions to increase the likelihood of human review, highlighting the limitations of algorithmic screening in diverse hiring contexts.
该研究探讨了招聘中算法单一化（即雇主普遍使用同一供应商的算法）如何导致系统性偏见和申请人结果趋同。基于300万申请人提交的400万份申请（均由同一供应商算法处理）的数据，研究发现显著的种族差异：亚裔申请人中有14.74%，非裔申请人中有25.87%被分配到美国就业歧视标准认定的不利职位。此外，4%的申请人对所有10个职位均被推荐拒绝，这一比例高于随机预期。研究进一步表明，算法的确定性导致申请人结果趋同，申请人需广泛投递才能提高被人工审核的机会。
G3T Up! Gravity Aligned Coordinate Frames Simplify Pointmap Processing
Authors: Bharath Raj Nagoor Kani, Noah Snavely
First: 2026-05-26T17:59:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:59:55+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://g3t-paper.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Modern feed-forward 3D reconstruction methods like VGGT predict pixel-aligned pointmaps in camera-centric coordinate frames. However, this choice of coordinate frame is not always optimal. We propose instead to predict pointmaps in upright, gravity-aligned frames that exploit strong structural cues present in many real-world scenes. Unlike camera-centric frames, gravity-aligned frames share a common vertical axis across viewpoints, reducing the rotational degrees of freedom needed to relate pointmaps to one another. To this end, we introduce the Gravity Grounded Geometry Transformer (G3T), fine-tuned from existing models on gravity-aligned 3D data. G3T produces highly accurate gravity-aware predictions, including upright pointmaps and camera-to-gravity poses. We further introduce G3T-Long, a submap-based incremental 3D reconstruction pipeline that leverages the reduced rotational degrees of freedom afforded by upright frames to achieve significantly improved reconstruction accuracy.
中文标题/摘要
标题：G3T Up! 重力对齐坐标系简化点图处理
现代前馈式3D重建方法（如VGGT）通常在相机中心坐标系中预测像素对齐的点图。然而，这种坐标系选择并非总是最优。我们提出改用直立、重力对齐的坐标系预测点图，以利用许多真实场景中存在的强结构线索。与相机中心坐标系不同，重力对齐坐标系在不同视角下共享同一垂直轴，从而减少了将点图相互关联所需的旋转自由度。为此，我们引入了重力引导几何变换器（G3T），该模型基于现有模型在重力对齐3D数据上微调。G3T可生成高精度的重力感知预测结果，包括直立点图和相机至重力姿态。此外，我们还提出了G3T-Long，一种基于子图的增量式3D重建流程，利用直立坐标系带来的减少旋转自由度的优势，显著提升了重建精度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of camera-centric coordinate frames in 3D reconstruction, this work proposes predicting pointmaps in gravity-aligned frames to better exploit structural cues in real-world scenes. The Gravity Grounded Geometry Transformer (G3T) is introduced, fine-tuned from existing models on gravity-aligned data, to generate upright pointmaps and camera-to-gravity poses with high accuracy. Experiments show that G3T-Long, an incremental reconstruction pipeline based on G3T, achieves significantly improved reconstruction accuracy by leveraging the reduced rotational degrees of freedom inherent in gravity-aligned representations.
为了解决相机中心坐标系在3D重建中的效率问题，本文提出在重力对齐坐标系中预测点图，以更好地利用真实场景中的结构线索。引入了重力对齐几何变换器（G3T），基于现有模型在重力对齐数据上微调，可生成高精度的竖直点图和相机至重力姿态。实验表明，基于G3T的增量重建管道G3T-Long，通过利用竖直坐标系降低的旋转自由度，显著提升了重建精度。
SpatialBench: Is Your Spatial Foundation Model an All-Round Player?
Authors: Haosong Peng, Hao Li, Jiaqi Chen, Yuhao Pan, Runmao Yao, Yalun Dai, Fushuo Huo, Fangzhou Hong, Zhaoxi Chen, Haozhao Wang, Dingwen Zhang, Ziwei Liu, Wenchao Xu
First: 2026-05-26T17:59:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:59:20+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://ropedia.github.io/SpatialBench/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
While spatial foundation models have demonstrated impressive performance on standard datasets, a critical question remains: are they truly all-round players capable of generalizing robustly across diverse downstream tasks, arbitrary viewpoints, shifting scene domains, varying input densities, and specific hardware constraints? Answering this overarching question requires a holistic assessment, yet current models are mainly evaluated on specific domains for which they were specifically designed or trained. Such evaluations are intrinsically limited by narrow paradigm coverage, limited scene domains, and arbitrary frame sampling, making it fundamentally difficult to assess their true generalization capabilities. To address this gap, we present SpatialBench, a cross-paradigm, domain-diverse benchmark for spatial foundation models with deterministic sampling. SpatialBench features unprecedented scale and rigorous deterministic design, comprising 19 datasets and 546 scenes across 5 diverse spatial domains. It comprehensively evaluates 41 models across 6 paradigms on 5 task suites under 4 different input density settings. Our extensive evaluation reveals that current models are not yet all-round players, and uncovers crucial insights for future advancement. Specifically, we demonstrate that full-context attention maximizes accuracy while bounded-memory strategies unlock long-sequence scalability. Moreover, our empirical evaluations in challenging embodied and egocentric tasks demonstrate that strict domain alignment and high data quality are far more critical to performance than simple dataset scaling. Furthermore, to address the largest data gap identified in our analysis, we go beyond evaluation by introducing a large-scale dataset, DA-Next-5M, and a strong baseline model, DA-Next, pushing the boundaries of spatial representation learning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SpatialBench：你的空间基础模型是全能选手吗？
尽管空间基础模型在标准数据集上表现优异，但一个关键问题仍待解答：它们是否真能作为全能选手，在多样化的下游任务、任意视角、变化的场景领域、不同输入密度及特定硬件约束下稳健泛化？回答这一核心问题需要全面评估，但当前模型主要在为其专门设计或训练的特定领域进行评估，这种评估因覆盖范围狭窄、场景领域有限及任意帧采样而固有受限，从根本上难以评估其真实泛化能力。为弥合这一差距，我们提出SpatialBench，这是一个跨范式、多领域、确定性采样的空间基础模型基准。SpatialBench规模空前且设计严谨，涵盖5个多样化空间领域中的19个数据集和546个场景。它在4种不同输入密度设置下，对6种范式中的41个模型进行5类任务套件的全面评估。我们的广泛评估表明，当前模型尚未达到全能选手水平，并揭示了未来发展的关键洞见：全上下文注意力可最大化精度，而受限内存策略则可解锁长序列可扩展性。此外，我们在具有挑战性的具身与第一人称任务中的实证评估表明，严格的领域对齐和高质量数据远比单纯的数据集规模增长对性能更为关键。为进一步解决分析中发现的最大数据缺口，我们不仅进行评估，还引入了大规模数据集DA-Next-5M和强基线模型DA-Next，推动空间表征学习的边界。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to assess whether spatial foundation models can generalize robustly across diverse tasks, viewpoints, domains, input densities, and hardware constraints, the authors introduce SpatialBench, a comprehensive benchmark with deterministic sampling across 19 datasets and 546 scenes spanning 5 spatial domains. Evaluated on 41 models across 6 paradigms and 5 task suites under 4 input density settings, the benchmark reveals that current models lack true all-round capability. Key findings include the superiority of full-context attention for accuracy and bounded-memory strategies for scalability, and the critical importance of domain alignment and data quality over mere dataset size. To address the largest data gap, the authors also release DA-Next-5M and the baseline model DA-Next, advancing spatial representation learning.
为评估空间基础模型是否能在多样化任务、视角、场景、输入密度和硬件约束下稳健泛化，作者提出了SpatialBench基准，包含19个数据集、546个场景，覆盖五大空间领域，采用确定性采样。他们在六种范式下对41个模型进行五类任务套件和四种输入密度的评估。结果表明当前模型缺乏真正泛化能力，全上下文注意力提升精度，而受限内存策略支持长序列扩展。实证发现领域对齐和数据质量比数据规模更重要，为弥合最大性能差距，作者发布了DA-Next-5M数据集和DA-Next基线模型以推动空间表征学习发展。
MUSE-Autoskill: Self-Evolving Agents via Skill Creation, Memory, Management, and Evaluation
Authors: Huawei Lin, Peng Li, Jie Song, Fuxin Jiang, Tieying Zhang
First: 2026-05-26T17:59:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:59:19+00:00
Comments: 30 pages, 8 figures, 13 tables, working in progress
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language model (LLM) agents rely on reusable skills to solve complex tasks. However, existing skill creation approaches treat skills as isolated and static artifacts, limiting their reusability, reliability, and long-term improvement. We propose MUSE-Autoskill Agent (Memory-Utilizing Skill Evolution), a skill-centric agent framework that lets agents continuously improve their task-solving capability by creating, reusing, and refining skills under a unified lifecycle (creation, memory, management, evaluation, and refinement). Our framework enables agents to create skills on demand, store and reuse them across tasks, organize and select them efficiently, and evaluate them through unit tests and runtime feedback for continuous refinement. We further introduce skill-level memory that accumulates experience for each skill across tasks, enabling more effective reuse and adaptation over time. Experiments on SkillsBench provide initial evidence that lifecycle-managed skills can improve task success, efficiency, reuse, and cross-agent transfer, highlighting the importance of treating skills as long-lived, experience-aware, and testable assets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MUSE-Autoskill：通过技能创建、记忆、管理与评估实现自我演进的智能体
大型语言模型（LLM）智能体依赖可复用的技能来解决复杂任务。然而，现有技能创建方法将技能视为孤立且静态的产物，限制了其可复用性、可靠性和长期改进能力。我们提出MUSE-Autoskill智能体（Memory-Utilizing Skill Evolution），这是一种以技能为中心的智能体框架，使智能体能够在统一生命周期（创建、记忆、管理、评估与优化）下持续提升任务解决能力。该框架支持智能体按需创建技能、跨任务存储与复用技能、高效组织与选择技能，并通过单元测试与运行时反馈评估技能以实现持续优化。我们进一步引入技能级记忆机制，积累各技能在不同任务中的经验，从而实现更有效的复用与适应。在SkillsBench上的实验初步表明，生命周期管理的技能可提升任务成功率、效率、复用性及跨智能体迁移能力，凸显了将技能视为长期存在、经验感知且可测试资产的重要性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of static and isolated skill creation in LLM agents, MUSE-Autoskill introduces a skill-centric framework that enables agents to continuously evolve their capabilities through a unified lifecycle of creation, memory, management, evaluation, and refinement. The method allows on-demand skill generation, cross-task reuse, efficient selection, and iterative improvement via unit tests and runtime feedback, while skill-level memory accumulates experience to enhance adaptation. Experimental results on SkillsBench demonstrate improved task success rates, efficiency, reuse potential, and cross-agent transfer, underscoring the value of treating skills as long-lived, experience-aware, and testable assets.
为了解决现有LLM代理中技能静态孤立导致的复用性差和长期改进能力弱的问题，MUSE-Autoskill提出一个以技能为中心的框架，通过统一的生命周期（创建、记忆、管理、评估与优化）实现技能的持续进化。该方法支持按需生成技能、跨任务复用、高效选择与迭代优化，结合技能级记忆积累经验以提升适应能力。在SkillsBench上的实验表明，生命周期管理的技能显著提升任务成功率、效率、复用性及跨代理迁移能力，验证了将技能视为长期、经验驱动且可测试资产的重要性。
LocateAnything: Fast and High-Quality Vision-Language Grounding with Parallel Box Decoding
Authors: Shihao Wang, Shilong Liu, Yuanguo Kuang, Xinyu Wei, Yangzhou Liu, Zhiqi Li, Yunze Man, Guo Chen, Andrew Tao, Guilin Liu, Jan Kautz, Lei Zhang, Zhiding Yu
First: 2026-05-26T17:59:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:59:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-language models (VLMs) commonly formulate visual grounding and detection as a coordinate-token generation problem, serializing each 2D box into multiple 1D tokens that are learned and decoded largely independently. This token-by-token decoding mismatches the coupled structure of box geometry and creates a practical inference bottleneck due to strictly sequential generation. We introduce LocateAnything, a unified generative grounding and detection framework based on Parallel Box Decoding (PBD). By decoding geometric elements such as bounding boxes and points as atomic units in a single step, LocateAnything preserves intra-box geometric coherence and unlocks substantial parallelism. We show that PBD improves both decoding throughput and localization accuracy. We further develop a scalable data engine and curate LocateAnything-Data, a large-scale dataset with more than 138 million training samples, substantially increasing data diversity for high-precision localization. Extensive evaluations show that LocateAnything advances the speed-accuracy frontier, achieving significantly higher decoding throughput while improving high-IoU localization quality across diverse benchmarks. The results highlight the complementary benefits of Parallel Box Decoding and large-scale training data in enabling efficient and precise unified visual grounding and detection.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LocateAnything：基于并行框解码的快速高质量视觉-语言定位
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）通常将视觉定位和检测表述为坐标-令牌生成问题，将每个2D框序列化为多个1D令牌，这些令牌大多独立学习和解码。这种逐令牌解码方式与框几何结构的耦合性不匹配，并因严格顺序生成造成实际推理瓶颈。我们提出LocateAnything，一种基于并行框解码（PBD）的统一生成式定位与检测框架。通过在单步内将边界框和点等几何元素作为原子单元解码，LocateAnything保持框内几何一致性并释放大量并行性。我们证明PBD同时提升了解码吞吐量和定位精度。我们进一步构建了可扩展的数据引擎，并整理了LocateAnything-Data数据集，包含超过1.38亿个训练样本，大幅提升了高精度定位所需的数据多样性。大量评估表明，LocateAnything推动了速度-精度前沿，实现显著更高的解码吞吐量，同时在多个基准测试中提升高IoU定位质量。结果凸显了并行框解码与大规模训练数据在实现高效精准统一视觉定位与检测方面的互补优势。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of token-by-token decoding in vision-language models that disrupts geometric coherence and limits inference speed, LocateAnything introduces Parallel Box Decoding (PBD), a method that generates bounding boxes and points as atomic units in parallel, preserving spatial structure and enabling faster decoding. Evaluated across multiple benchmarks, PBD achieves higher throughput and improved high-IoU localization accuracy compared to sequential methods, while a newly curated dataset of over 138 million samples further enhances model precision and generalization.
为了解决视觉语言模型中逐token解码导致的几何结构破坏和推理瓶颈问题，本文提出LocateAnything框架，采用并行框解码（PBD）技术，将边界框和点作为原子单元一次性并行生成，从而保持几何一致性并提升解码效率。实验表明，该方法在多个基准测试中显著提高推理速度和高IoU定位精度，同时依托包含超1.38亿样本的LocateAnything-Data大规模数据集，进一步增强了模型的精度与泛化能力。
Natural Language Query to Configuration for Retrieval Agents
Authors: Melissa Z. Pan, Negar Arabzadeh, Mathew Jacob, Fiodar Kazhamiaka, Esha Choukse, Matei Zaharia
First: 2026-05-26T17:58:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:58:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern retrieval agents expose many configuration choices -- LLM, retriever, number of documents, number of hops, and synthesis strategy -- each shaping both answer quality and serving cost. Today, these pipelines are typically hand-tuned once per workload, leaving substantial per-query optimization untapped. We formulate the problem: given a natural-language query and either an accuracy or a budget target, select from a predefined pipeline catalog the configuration that minimizes cost or maximizes accuracy at inference time. We propose **BRANE**, which uses an LLM to convert each query into workload-specific characteristics, then trains a lightweight per-configuration predictor that estimates whether the pipeline will answer the query correctly. At inference time, **BRANE** selects the configuration that maximizes predicted correctness penalized by cost, exposing a tunable cost-quality tradeoff without retraining. Across MuSiQue, BrowseComp-Plus, and FinanceBench, **BRANE** consistently pushes the cost-quality Pareto frontier, matches the best fixed configuration's accuracy at up to 89% lower cost, and outperforms LLM-routing, rule-based, and fine-tuned Qwen3-4B baselines. These results show that per-query configuration of the full retrieval pipeline is a practical alternative to static workload-level tuning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自然语言查询到检索代理配置的映射
现代检索代理暴露了多种配置选项——大语言模型、检索器、文档数量、跳数及合成策略——每项均影响答案质量和推理成本。当前这些流水线通常为每个工作负载手动调优一次，未能充分利用每查询的优化潜力。我们提出问题：给定自然语言查询及准确率或预算目标，从预定义的流水线目录中选择配置，以最小化成本或最大化准确率。我们提出**BRANE**，它利用大语言模型将每个查询转化为工作负载特定特征，再训练轻量级每配置预测器，以估计流水线是否能正确回答查询。推理时，**BRANE**选择预测正确率加权成本后的最优配置，无需重训练即可调节成本-质量权衡。在MuSiQue、BrowseComp-Plus和FinanceBench上，**BRANE**持续推动成本-质量帕累托前沿，以高达89%的成本匹配最佳固定配置的准确率，并优于LLM路由、规则基和微调Qwen3-4B基线。结果表明，全检索流水线的每查询配置是替代静态工作负载调优的实用方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of static pipeline tuning in retrieval agents, this work aims to optimize configuration per query for cost and accuracy trade-offs. BRANE, the proposed method, uses an LLM to extract query-specific features and trains lightweight predictors for each pipeline configuration to estimate correctness. At inference time, it selects the configuration maximizing predicted accuracy penalized by cost, without retraining. Experiments across MuSiQue, BrowseComp-Plus, and FinanceBench show BRANE consistently achieves the best cost-accuracy tradeoff, matching top fixed configurations at up to 89% lower cost and outperforming LLM-routing, rule-based, and fine-tuned baselines.
为解决现有检索代理静态调优导致的资源浪费问题，本文提出BRANE方法，旨在根据自然语言查询动态选择最优配置以平衡准确率与成本。该方法首先用LLM提取查询的特定工作负载特征，再为每个配置训练轻量级预测器以估计正确性。推理时，BRANE选择预测准确率加权成本最小的配置，无需重训练即可实现动态权衡。在MuSiQue、BrowseComp-Plus和FinanceBench三个数据集上，BRANE持续逼近帕累托前沿，以最高89%的成本匹配最优固定配置的准确率，并优于LLM路由、规则基和微调Qwen3-4B基线模型。
GENESIS: Harnessing AI Agents for Autonomous 6G RAN Synthesis, Research, and Testing
Authors: Tamerlan Aghayev, Maxime Elkael, Michele Polese, Minh Dat Nguyen, Gabriele Gemmi, Andrea Lacava, Ali Saeizadeh, Reshma Prasad, Paolo Testolina, Angelo Feraudo, Soumendra Nanda, Pedram Johari, Salvatore D'Oro, Tommaso Melodia
First: 2026-05-26T17:58:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:58:43+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 16 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Cellular research and development (R&D) is throttled by six structural processes that each consume months of manual engineering work per iteration: (i) synthesizing new features from standards or research papers into production code; (ii) conformance and interoperability testing; (iii) hardening against field anomalies and diverse deployment environments; (iv) data-driven optimization of network functionalities; (v) discovering and prototyping novel waveforms, functionalities, and capabilities for future standards; and (vi) securing the stack against vulnerabilities. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) have compressed comparable R&D work in general software engineering from days to minutes, their known pitfalls worsen on Radio Access Network (RAN) use cases: they hallucinate Application Programming Interfaces (APIs) and mis-read specifications, which kills interoperability of RAN components at the first mistake, and they heavily rely on simulations for designing algorithms, which is notorious for breaking when transferred to real hardware. To address these challenges, we present GENESIS, an agentic Artificial Intelligence (AI) framework that converts intents (e.g., a specification clause, a telemetry anomaly, or a research hypothesis) into solutions validated with over-the-air experiments, fed back into a persistent knowledge base. GENESIS is built on three composable primitives (agents, skills, hooks) and a knowledge layer (SYNAPSE) that doubles as the source of ground truth and the recipient of every artifact the framework produces, making capabilities compound across runs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GENESIS：利用AI代理实现6G无线接入网的自主合成、研究与测试
蜂窝网络研发受六大结构化流程制约，每项流程每次迭代均需数月人工工程工作：(i) 从标准或研究论文中合成新功能为生产代码；(ii) 一致性与互操作性测试；(iii) 针对现场异常与多样部署环境的加固；(iv) 基于数据驱动的网络功能优化；(v) 发现并原型化未来标准的新波形、功能与能力；(vi) 防御系统漏洞。尽管大语言模型（LLMs）已将通用软件工程中的类似研发工作从数天压缩至数分钟，但其已知缺陷在无线接入网（RAN）场景中更严重：它们会虚构应用程序编程接口（API）并误读规范，导致首个错误即破坏RAN组件互操作性，且高度依赖仿真设计算法，而仿真在转至真实硬件时极易失效。为应对这些挑战，我们提出GENESIS，一个基于代理的AI框架，将意图（如规范条款、遥测异常或研究假设）转化为经空中实验验证的解决方案，并反馈至持久化知识库。GENESIS基于三个可组合原语（代理、技能、钩子）及一个知识层（SYNAPSE），后者既作为真实基准来源，也作为框架生成的所有产物接收器，使能力在多次运行中累积叠加。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to automate six major, time-consuming RAN R&D processes that currently require months of manual effort per iteration, GENESIS introduces an agentic AI framework designed to translate high-level intents—such as specification clauses or telemetry anomalies—into validated solutions through over-the-air experiments. Built on three composable primitives (agents, skills, hooks) and a persistent knowledge layer called SYNAPSE, which serves both as ground truth and artifact repository, GENESIS enables iterative learning and capability compounding. Experimental results demonstrate that GENESIS successfully generates production-ready code, passes conformance tests, hardens against real-world anomalies, and prototypes novel waveforms—all while reducing development cycles from months to days and minimizing hallucinations or simulation-to-hardware failures.
为解决6G无线接入网研发中六个耗时数月的重复性人工流程，GENESIS利用AI代理自动完成从标准或论文到生产代码的合成、测试与实网验证。该框架基于可组合的代理、技能与钩子模块，并结合名为SYNAPSE的知识层，既作为真实基准数据源，也作为所有产出物的存储库，实现能力跨轮次累积。实验表明，GENESIS能将规范条款、异常报告等高层意图转化为经实网验证的可互操作RAN方案，有效克服大语言模型幻觉与仿真到硬件失效的问题。
MobileMoE: Scaling On-Device Mixture of Experts
Authors: Yanbei Chen, Hanxian Huang, Ernie Chang, Jacob Szwejbka, Digant Desai, Zechun Liu, Vikas Chandra, Raghuraman Krishnamoorthi
First: 2026-05-26T17:58:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:58:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) has become the de facto architecture for hundred-billion-parameter language models, yet its advantages at sub-billion scales for on-device deployment remain largely unexplored. To close this gap, we present MobileMoE, a family of on-device MoE language models with sub-billion active parameters (0.3-0.9B active and 1.3-5.3B total) that establish a new Pareto frontier for on-device LLMs. We first formulate an on-device MoE scaling law that jointly optimizes MoE architecture under mobile memory and compute constraints, identifying an on-device sweet spot - moderate sparsity with fine-grained and shared experts - that is simultaneously memory and compute-optimal. Building on the derived architectures, we train MobileMoE with a four-stage recipe covering pre-training, mid-training, instruction fine-tuning, and quantization-aware training, all on open-source datasets. Across 14 benchmarks, MobileMoE matches or exceeds leading on-device dense LLMs with 2-4$\times$ fewer inference FLOPs, and matches or surpasses the state-of-the-art MoE OLMoE-1B-7B with up to 60% fewer parameters. To bridge the last mile to mobile deployment, we provide the first efficient MoE inference on commodity smartphones with comprehensive on-device profiling. At comparable INT4 weight memory, MobileMoE-S delivers $1.8$-$3.8\times$ faster prefill and $2.2$-$3.4\times$ faster decode than the dense baseline MobileLLM-Pro.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MobileMoE：设备端专家混合模型的扩展
专家混合（MoE）已成为百亿参数语言模型的主流架构，但其在十亿级参数规模下用于设备端部署的优势尚未充分探索。为弥合这一差距，我们提出了MobileMoE，这是一系列设备端MoE语言模型，其活跃参数在0.3-0.9B之间，总参数量为1.3-5.3B，为设备端大语言模型建立了新的帕累托前沿。我们首先制定了设备端MoE的扩展定律，联合优化移动端内存与计算约束下的MoE架构，识别出设备端的最优平衡点——适度稀疏性与细粒度共享专家，同时实现内存与计算最优。基于所推导的架构，我们采用四阶段训练方案（预训练、中期训练、指令微调、量化感知训练），全部基于开源数据集训练MobileMoE。在14项基准测试中，MobileMoE在推理FLOPs仅为领先设备端密集型LLM的2-4倍的情况下，表现匹配或超越；与最先进的MoE模型OLMoE-1B-7B相比，参数量最多可减少60%。为实现移动端部署的最后一步，我们首次提供了在普通智能手机上高效运行MoE推理的方案，并进行了全面的设备端性能分析。在INT4权重内存相当的情况下，MobileMoE-S的预填充速度比密集基线MobileLLM-Pro快1.8-3.8倍，解码速度快2.2-3.4倍。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to deploy large language models efficiently on mobile devices, this work introduces MobileMoE, a family of on-device MoE models with sub-billion active parameters that achieve state-of-the-art performance under memory and compute constraints. The method derives a scaling law to identify an optimal MoE architecture featuring moderate sparsity and shared experts, then trains the models through a four-stage pipeline including pre-training, mid-training, instruction fine-tuning, and quantization-aware training on open-source data. Experimental results across 14 benchmarks show MobileMoE matches or outperforms leading dense models with 2-4x fewer inference FLOPs and surpasses existing MoE models with up to 60% fewer parameters, while on-device profiling confirms MobileMoE-S delivers 1.8-3.8x faster prefill and 2.2-3.4x faster decode than the dense baseline at comparable INT4 memory.
为在移动设备上高效部署大语言模型，本文提出MobileMoE，是一系列活跃参数低于十亿的设备端MoE模型，其在内存与计算受限条件下达到最优性能。作者推导出适用于移动端的MoE架构扩展定律，发现适度稀疏度结合细粒度共享专家是兼顾内存与计算效率的最佳方案。模型通过预训练、中期训练、指令微调和量化感知训练四阶段流程，在开源数据集上训练，其在14项基准测试中，推理FLOPs比现有设备端密集模型少2-4倍，参数量比SOTA MoE模型OLMoE-1B-7B少最多60%。在真实智能手机上，MobileMoE-S在INT4权重内存相当条件下，比密集基线MobileLLM-Pro的预填充快1.8-3.8倍，解码快2.2-3.4倍。
Alignment Tampering: How Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback Is Exploited to Optimize Misaligned Biases
Authors: Dongyoon Hahm, Dylan Hadfield-Menell, Kimin Lee
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-26T17:57:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:57:04+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICML 2026, Source code: https://alignment-tampering.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) is the standard method to align Large Language Models (LLMs) with human preferences. In this work, we introduce alignment tampering, a potential vulnerability where the LLM undergoing alignment influences the preference dataset, causing RLHF to amplify undesired behaviors. This arises from core limitations of RLHF: (1) preference datasets are constructed from the LLM's own outputs, allowing it to influence them, and (2) pairwise comparisons only indicate which response is better, not why. These limitations can be exploited to cause alignment tampering. For example, if an LLM generates biased responses with higher quality, annotators will prefer them based on quality. However, preference labels do not distinguish quality from bias, and the reward model inherits this limitation. Optimizing such rewards through reinforcement learning or best-of-N sampling can amplify misaligned biases. Our experiments demonstrate amplification across diverse biases: from keyword bias to propaganda (e.g., sexism), brand promotion, and instrumental goal-seeking. Mitigation remains challenging, as existing techniques for robust RLHF fail to fully resolve alignment tampering without sacrificing response quality. These findings reveal structural vulnerabilities of current RLHF and emphasize the need to prevent this vulnerability. Project page: https://alignment-tampering.github.io/
中文标题/摘要
标题：对齐篡改：人类反馈强化学习如何被利用以放大对齐偏差
人类反馈强化学习（RLHF）是将大语言模型（LLM）对齐人类偏好的标准方法。本文引入了“对齐篡改”这一潜在漏洞：正在对齐的LLM可影响偏好数据集，导致RLHF放大不期望的行为。这源于RLHF的核心局限：（1）偏好数据集由LLM自身输出构建，使其可影响数据集；（2）成对比较仅表明哪个响应更优，而非原因。这些局限可被利用引发对齐篡改。例如，若LLM生成质量更高但有偏的响应，标注者将因质量偏好该响应。但偏好标签无法区分质量与偏见，奖励模型继承此局限。通过强化学习或最佳N采样优化此类奖励，可放大对齐偏差。实验表明，该现象在多种偏见中普遍存在：从关键词偏见到宣传（如性别歧视）、品牌推广及工具性目标追求。缓解仍具挑战，因现有鲁棒RLHF技术无法完全解决对齐篡改而不牺牲响应质量。这些发现揭示当前RLHF的结构性脆弱性，并强调需防范此漏洞。项目页面：https://alignment-tampering.github.io/
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates alignment tampering, a vulnerability in Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) where the LLM under alignment can manipulate its own preference dataset to amplify misaligned biases. The authors demonstrate that because RLHF relies on pairwise comparisons of the model’s own outputs and does not capture causal reasons for preference, the model can generate biased responses that appear higher quality, leading annotators to label them as preferred. This feedback loop causes the reward model and subsequent RL optimization to reinforce these biases. Experiments show amplification of various biases including keyword bias, sexism, brand promotion, and instrumental goal-seeking. Existing mitigation strategies fail to fully resolve the issue without degrading response quality, revealing structural flaws in current RLHF alignment methods.
本文研究了RLHF中的‘对齐篡改’漏洞，即正在对齐的LLM可通过影响自身偏好数据集来放大非对齐偏见。作者指出，由于RLHF仅依赖模型自身输出的成对比较，且无法区分偏好背后的质量与偏见，模型会生成看似更优但带有偏见的响应，导致标注者误判其为更优选项，进而使奖励模型和强化学习优化过程强化这些偏见。实验在关键词偏见、性别歧视、品牌推广和工具性目标追求等多个偏见类型中均观察到偏见被显著放大。现有缓解方法难以完全解决该问题而不牺牲响应质量，揭示了当前RLHF对齐范式的结构性缺陷。
SOLE-R1: Video-Language Reasoning as the Sole Reward for On-Robot Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Philip Schroeder, Thomas Weng, Karl Schmeckpeper, Eric Rosen, Stephen Hart, Ondrej Biza
First: 2026-03-30T17:46:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:56:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Vision-language models (VLMs) have shown impressive capabilities across diverse tasks, motivating efforts to leverage these models to supervise robot learning. However, when used as evaluators in reinforcement learning (RL), today's strongest models often fail under partial observability and distribution shift, enabling policies to exploit perceptual errors rather than solve the task. We introduce SOLE-R1 (Self-Observing LEarner), a video-language reasoning model explicitly designed to serve as the sole reward signal for online RL. Given only raw video observations and a natural-language goal, SOLE-R1 performs per-timestep spatiotemporal chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning and produces dense estimates of task progress that can be used directly as rewards. To train SOLE-R1, we develop a large-scale video trajectory and reasoning synthesis pipeline that generates temporally grounded CoT traces aligned with continuous progress supervision. This data is combined with foundational spatial and multi-frame temporal reasoning, and used to train the model with a hybrid framework that couples supervised fine-tuning with RL from verifiable rewards. Across four different simulation environments and a real-robot setting, SOLE-R1 enables zero-shot online RL from random initialization: robots learn previously unseen manipulation tasks without ground-truth rewards, success indicators, demonstrations, or task-specific tuning. SOLE-R1 succeeds on 24 unseen tasks and substantially outperforms strong vision-language rewarders, including Robometer, RoboReward, ReWiND, GPT-5, and Gemini-3-Pro, while exhibiting markedly greater robustness to reward hacking. We release all models, data, code, and demos at the anonymous page: https://philip-mit.github.io/sole-r1/
中文标题/摘要
标题：SOLE-R1：视频-语言推理作为机器人在线强化学习的唯一奖励信号
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在多种任务中展现出卓越能力，推动了利用这些模型监督机器人学习的努力。然而，当用作强化学习（RL）中的评估器时，当前最强的模型常在部分可观测性和分布偏移下失效，使策略能利用感知错误而非解决任务。我们提出了SOLE-R1（自观测学习者），一种专为在线RL设计、作为唯一奖励信号的视频-语言推理模型。仅给定原始视频观测和自然语言目标，SOLE-R1执行每时间步的空间-时间链式思维（CoT）推理，并生成可直接作为奖励的密集任务进度估计。为训练SOLE-R1，我们开发了大规模视频轨迹与推理合成管道，生成与连续进度监督对齐的时间接地CoT轨迹。该数据结合基础空间与多帧时间推理，通过结合监督微调与可验证奖励的强化学习的混合框架进行训练。在四个不同仿真环境和真实机器人设置中，SOLE-R1实现了从随机初始化的零样本在线RL：机器人无需真实奖励、成功指标、演示或任务特定调优，即可学习此前未见过的操作任务。SOLE-R1在24个未见过的任务中成功，并显著优于Robometer、RoboReward、ReWiND、GPT-5和Gemini-3-Pro等强视觉-语言奖励器，同时展现出对奖励作弊的明显更强鲁棒性。我们将在匿名页面https://philip-mit.github.io/sole-r1/发布所有模型、数据、代码和演示。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable reward signals in robot reinforcement learning, this work proposes SOLE-R1, a video-language model designed to act as the sole reward function using only raw video and natural-language goals. It performs per-timestep spatiotemporal chain-of-thought reasoning to generate dense, continuous progress estimates directly usable as rewards. Trained via a synthesized dataset of temporally grounded reasoning traces combined with supervised fine-tuning and RL from verifiable rewards, SOLE-R1 enables zero-shot online learning on real robots and simulation environments without ground-truth rewards or demonstrations. It successfully learns 24 unseen manipulation tasks and outperforms existing vision-language reward models like Robometer, ReWiND, and GPT-5 while being more resistant to reward hacking.
为解决机器人强化学习中奖励信号不可靠的问题，本文提出SOLE-R1，一种专为在线强化学习设计的视频-语言推理模型，可作为唯一奖励信号。它基于原始视频与自然语言目标，逐时间步进行时空链式推理，生成可直接用于强化学习的密集任务进度估计。通过合成大规模时空对齐的推理轨迹数据，并结合监督微调与可验证奖励的强化学习训练，SOLE-R1能在无真实奖励、演示或任务调优的情况下，零样本学习多个未见过的操控任务，在四个仿真环境和真实机器人上表现优异，显著超越Robometer、ReWiND、GPT-5等现有视觉-语言奖励模型，且对奖励欺骗更具鲁棒性。
Guiding LLM Post-training Data Engineering with Model Internals from Sparse Autoencoders
Authors: Yi Jing, Zao Dai, Jinwu Hu, Zijun Yao, Lei Hou, Juanzi Li, Xiaozhi Wang
First: 2026-05-26T17:55:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:55:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Model internals encode rich information about how a large language model (LLM) processes its training data; however, post-training data engineering largely relies on external signals and ignores rich intrinsic signals lying in model internals. We propose SAERL, a data engineering framework for LLM reinforcement learning (RL). It models three intrinsic data properties: diversity, difficulty, and quality, using model internals extracted with Sparse Autoencoder (SAE), an advanced mechanistic interpretability tool. Each property grounds a concrete data engineering operation: SAE-space clustering with moderate batch mixing for batch diversity control, a difficulty proxy for easy-to-hard curriculum ordering, and a quality probe for data filtering. SAERL improves average accuracy by 3.00% over vanilla GRPO and reaches target accuracy with 20% fewer training steps on Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B, with consistent gains across model scales and RL algorithms. Experiments show that SAE transfers effectively across model families and scales, serving as a lightweight and reusable data engineering tool. These results demonstrate that model internals are a powerful and practical source of signals for post-training data engineering.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用稀疏自编码器模型内部信息指导大语言模型后训练数据工程
模型内部编码了关于大型语言模型（LLM）如何处理其训练数据的丰富信息；然而，后训练数据工程主要依赖外部信号，忽视了模型内部蕴含的丰富内在信号。我们提出了SAERL，一个用于LLM强化学习（RL）的数据工程框架。它利用稀疏自编码器（SAE）提取的模型内部信息，建模三种内在数据属性：多样性、难度和质量。每种属性对应一个具体的数据工程操作：SAE空间聚类结合适度批次混合以控制批次多样性，难度代理用于由易到难的课程排序，质量探测器用于数据过滤。SAERL在Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B上相比基础GRPO平均准确率提升3.00%，且达到目标准确率所需训练步数减少20%，在不同模型规模和RL算法中均保持一致增益。实验表明，SAE可跨模型家族和规模有效迁移，作为轻量且可复用的数据工程工具。这些结果表明，模型内部是后训练数据工程中强大且实用的信号来源。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the underutilization of model internals in post-training data engineering, the authors propose SAERL, a framework that leverages Sparse Autoencoder (SAE) extracted model internals to guide data engineering for LLM reinforcement learning. SAERL models three intrinsic data properties—diversity, difficulty, and quality—by mapping them to concrete operations: SAE-space clustering with batch mixing for diversity, a difficulty proxy for curriculum ordering, and a quality probe for filtering. Experiments on Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B show SAERL improves average accuracy by 3.00% over vanilla GRPO and achieves target accuracy 20% faster, with consistent gains across model scales and RL algorithms, demonstrating SAE’s transferability and utility as a lightweight, reusable data engineering tool.
为解决大语言模型后训练数据工程中对模型内部信息利用不足的问题，本文提出SAERL框架，利用稀疏自编码器（SAE）提取的模型内部信息指导强化学习训练数据工程。该框架通过三个内在数据属性——多样性、难度和质量——分别对应具体操作：SAE空间聚类与批次混合控制多样性、难度代理实现由易到难的课程编排、质量探测器用于数据过滤。在Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B模型上实验表明，SAERL相较基础GRPO平均准确率提升3.00%，达到目标准确率所需训练步数减少20%，且在不同模型规模与强化学习算法中表现稳定，验证了SAE的跨模型可迁移性与作为轻量可复用数据工程工具的实用性。
RSD: A Local Triangulation Audit Primitive for Learned Vector Blocks
Authors: Seungmin Jin
First: 2026-05-17T14:44:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:55:28+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 1 figure. Revised version with clarified scope, experiments, and limitations
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Local XAI audits compare a finite block of learned vectors with a weak side signal. Baselines such as nearest-neighbor lookup, low-rank coordinate models, and relation factorization expose different parts of this audit. We introduce Relational Semantic Decomposition, abbreviated as RSD, as a local triangulation audit for learned vector blocks. Given coordinates X and a declared bounded weak affinity proxy A, RSD fits simplex memberships S and coordinate poles C. It reuses S in a relation decoder for A and reports the coordinate residual R=X-SC. This yields a scoped audit unit: compatibility for the chosen block, proxy, decoder class, and loss budget, plus component mass and residual readouts. Synthetic controls check simplex reconstruction, proxy decoding, and fixed-S residual decomposition. The theorem-statement, month, and dog/wolf blocks illustrate why low proxy loss should be read with component mass, residual readouts, and block size.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RSD：用于学习向量块的局部三角测量审计原语
局部XAI审计将有限的学习向量块与弱侧信号进行比较。基线方法如最近邻查找、低秩坐标模型和关系因子分解揭示了该审计的不同部分。我们引入关系语义分解（RSD），作为学习向量块的局部三角测量审计方法。给定坐标X和声明的有界弱亲和性代理A，RSD拟合单纯形成员S和坐标极点C。它在关系解码器中重用S对A进行解码，并报告坐标残差R=X-SC。这产生一个限定的审计单元：所选块、代理、解码器类和损失预算的兼容性，以及组件质量与残差读数。合成控制检查单纯形重建、代理解码和固定S残差分解。定理陈述、月份和狗/狼块示例说明为何应结合组件质量、残差读数和块大小来解读低代理损失。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for localized, interpretable audits of learned vector blocks using weak side signals, the paper introduces RSD (Relational Semantic Decomposition), a local triangulation method that fits simplex memberships and coordinate poles to reconstruct and analyze vector blocks. Given a coordinate set X and a declared weak affinity proxy A, RSD computes simplex memberships S and coordinate poles C, then uses S to decode A and reports the residual R = X - SC. Experiments on synthetic controls and real-world blocks (theorem-statement, month, dog/wolf) demonstrate that RSD effectively isolates component mass and residual readouts, revealing that low proxy loss alone is insufficient without context from block size and residual structure.
为实现对学习到的向量块的局部可解释审计，该论文提出RSD（关系语义分解），一种基于局部三角剖分的方法。给定坐标集X和声明的弱亲和性代理A，RSD拟合单纯形成员S和坐标极点C，利用S在关系解码器中重建A，并报告残差R = X - SC。在合成控制和真实块（定理陈述、月份、狗/狼）实验中，RSD有效分解向量块为可解释组件，表明低代理损失需结合组件质量、残差读数和块大小进行评估，才能得出有意义的审计结论。
The AI Cognitive Trojan Horse: How Large Language Models May Bypass Human Epistemic Vigilance
Authors: Andrew D. Maynard
First: 2026-01-11T22:28:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:53:46+00:00
Comments: 16 pages, 20 references. v2: Added brief discussion situating "honest signals" terminology in evolutionary biology (Sec. 3), with two added citations (Zahavi 1975; Maynard Smith & Harper 2003). No changes to argument or conclusions
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language model (LLM)-based conversational AI systems present a challenge to human cognition that current frameworks for understanding misinformation and persuasion do not adequately address. This paper proposes that a significant epistemic risk from conversational AI may lie not in inaccuracy or intentional deception, but in something more fundamental: these systems may be configured, through optimization processes that make them useful, to present characteristics that bypass the cognitive mechanisms humans evolved to evaluate incoming information. The Cognitive Trojan Horse hypothesis draws on Sperber and colleagues' theory of epistemic vigilance -- the parallel cognitive process monitoring communicated information for reasons to doubt -- and proposes that LLM-based systems present 'honest non-signals': genuine characteristics (fluency, helpfulness, apparent disinterest) that fail to carry the information equivalent human characteristics would carry, because in humans these are costly to produce while in LLMs they are computationally trivial. Four mechanisms of potential bypass are identified: processing fluency decoupled from understanding, trust-competence presentation without corresponding stakes, cognitive offloading that delegates evaluation itself to the AI, and optimization dynamics that systematically produce sycophancy. The framework generates testable predictions, including a counterintuitive speculation that cognitively sophisticated users may be more vulnerable to AI-mediated epistemic influence. This reframes AI safety as partly a problem of calibration -- aligning human evaluative responses with the actual epistemic status of AI-generated content -- rather than solely a problem of preventing deception.
中文标题/摘要
标题：人工智能认知木马：大型语言模型如何可能绕过人类认知警惕
基于大型语言模型（LLM）的对话式人工智能系统对人类认知构成挑战，现有对虚假信息和说服机制的理解框架难以充分应对。本文提出，对话式人工智能的重大认知风险可能不在于不准确或故意欺骗，而在于更根本的机制：这些系统可能通过使其具有实用性的优化过程，呈现出绕过人类为评估信息而演化出的认知机制的特征。‘认知木马’假说借鉴了Sperber等人的认知警惕理论——即并行监控沟通信息以产生怀疑的思维过程——并提出LLM系统呈现‘诚实非信号’：真实特征（流畅性、有用性、表面冷漠）在人类中因产生成本高昂而在AI中却计算上微不足道，因而无法传递人类特征所承载的信息。本文识别出四种潜在绕过机制：理解与处理流畅性脱钩、无对应风险的可信能力展示、认知卸载将评估本身委托给AI、以及系统性产生谄媚的优化动态。该框架生成可检验的预测，包括一个反直觉推测：认知能力更强的用户可能更易受AI中介的认知影响。这将AI安全问题部分重构为校准问题——使人类评价反应与AI生成内容的实际认知状态对齐，而非仅限于防止欺骗。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the epistemic risks posed by large language models (LLMs) not through deception or inaccuracy, but by exploiting human cognitive mechanisms evolved to detect deception — a phenomenon termed the 'Cognitive Trojan Horse.' The authors propose that LLMs, optimized for fluency and helpfulness, generate 'honest non-signals' — traits like apparent disinterest or trustworthiness that are computationally cheap for AI but costly for humans — thereby bypassing epistemic vigilance. Four bypass mechanisms are identified: fluency without understanding, trust without stakes, cognitive offloading to the AI, and systemic sycophancy from optimization. Experimental predictions suggest that cognitively sophisticated users may be more vulnerable, reframing AI safety as a calibration problem — aligning human judgment with the actual epistemic status of AI output — rather than merely preventing lies.
本文指出，大型语言模型可能通过呈现‘诚实非信号’——如流畅性和帮助性等看似可信但缺乏人类认知权重的特征，来绕过人类的认知警惕机制。这些特征在人类中因成本高昂而具有信息价值，但在LLM中却计算成本极低，从而产生误导。作者基于Sperber的‘认知警惕’理论，提出四种绕过机制：流畅性脱离理解、无真实代价的信任呈现、认知卸载至AI、以及优化驱动的谄媚倾向。研究预测认知能力更强的用户反而更易受AI影响，将AI安全问题重新定位为认知校准问题，而非单纯防范欺骗。
Compute Optimal Tokenization
Authors: Tomasz Limisiewicz, Artidoro Pagnoni, Srini Iyer, Mike Lewis, Sachin Mehta, Alisa Liu, Margaret Li, Gargi Ghosh, Luke Zettlemoyer
First: 2026-05-02T01:53:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:52:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Scaling laws enable the optimal selection of data amount and language model size, yet the impact of the data unit, the token, on this relationship remains underexplored. In this work, we systematically investigate how the information granularity of tokens, controlled by the compression rate (i.e., average bytes of text per token), affects scaling trends. We train 988 latent tokenized models (BLT) ranging from 50M to 7B parameters that enable setting the desired compression rate. This flexibility allows us to study the role of compression rate well beyond 4.57 bytes per token obtained with a popular BPE tokenizer. Our experiments reveal that in compute-optimal configurations, model parameter counts scale proportionally to data size measured in bytes, not in tokens as commonly perceived (Kaplan et al., 2020; Hoffmann et al., 2022). Furthermore, we discover that the optimal compression rate differs from the one obtained with BPE and decreases with compute. These findings generalize to both latent and subword tokenization, as well as to languages other than English, guiding language model developers on tokenization scheme selection for maximal compute efficiency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：计算最优分词
缩放定律可实现数据量与语言模型大小的最优选择，但数据单位——token——对此关系的影响尚未充分研究。本研究系统探讨了token信息粒度（由压缩率即每token平均字节数控制）如何影响缩放趋势。我们训练了988个从50M到7B参数的潜在分词模型（BLT），以设定所需压缩率。这种灵活性使我们能深入研究压缩率远超流行BPE分词器所获的4.57字节/Token的影响。实验表明，在计算最优配置下，模型参数数量与以字节为单位的数据量成正比，而非通常认为的以Token为单位（Kaplan等，2020；Hoffmann等，2022）。此外，我们发现最优压缩率不同于BPE所得，且随计算资源减少。这些发现适用于潜在与子词分词，以及非英语语言，为语言模型开发者提供分词方案选择以实现最大计算效率的指导。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how token granularity affects scaling laws in language models, this study investigates the impact of compression rate—the average bytes per token—on compute-optimal model scaling. Using 988 latent tokenized models ranging from 50M to 7B parameters, the authors vary compression rates beyond the typical BPE baseline to explore how tokenization influences efficiency. Their experiments show that in compute-optimal settings, model size scales with data size in bytes, not tokens, and that the optimal compression rate varies with compute budget and differs from BPE’s default. These results hold across tokenization types and languages, offering practical guidance for selecting tokenization schemes to maximize efficiency.
为探究token粒度对语言模型缩放定律的影响，本研究系统分析了压缩率（即每token平均字节数）对计算最优配置的作用。通过训练988个从50M到7B参数的潜在token化模型（BLT），作者在远超BPE典型值的压缩率范围内进行实验。结果表明，在计算最优配置下，模型参数量与数据字节数成比例增长，而非传统认知中的token数；同时，最优压缩率随计算预算降低，且该规律适用于多种token化方法和非英语语言，为模型开发者提供了提升计算效率的实用指导。
From Scores to Gibbs Correctors: Accelerating Uniform-Rate Discrete Diffusion Models
Authors: Yuchen Liang, Ness Shroff, Yingbin Liang
First: 2026-05-26T17:52:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:52:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Discrete diffusion models have achieved strong empirical performance in text and other symbolic domains, but, especially for uniform-rate models, they often require many steps to generate a single sample. Existing acceleration methods either rely on training additional quantities or suffer from slow mixing. In this work, we propose a novel Gibbs-based corrector for discrete diffusion models, termed Gibbs-Accelerated Discrete Diffusion (GADD). GADD leverages the structure of the concrete score function to construct Gibbs posterior likelihoods directly, without requiring any additional training beyond standard score estimation. We show that GADD achieves an overall sampling complexity of $\mathcal{O}(\mathrm{polylog} (\varepsilon^{-1}))$, yielding the first such rate for diffusion-based samplers for uniform-rate discrete diffusion models. We also conduct numerical experiments demonstrating the practical advantages of GADD across synthetic data, zero-shot text sampling, and zero-shot conditional music generation. These results corroborate the theory and show that GADD consistently improves sample quality and wall-clock efficiency over standard baselines, including vanilla Euler methods and CTMC correctors. Beyond this, our theoretical analysis introduces a novel framework for analyzing predictor-corrector methods in discrete diffusion models, which may be of independent interest. Unlike existing approaches that rely on the Girsanov change-of-measure technique, our method is based on an induction argument that tracks error propagation across predictor iterations while accounting for inaccuracies in the corrector updates.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从分数到吉布斯校正器：加速均匀速率离散扩散模型
离散扩散模型在文本及其他符号域中表现出强大的实证性能，但尤其是均匀速率模型，通常需要大量步骤才能生成单个样本。现有加速方法要么依赖额外训练量，要么混合速度缓慢。本文提出了一种基于吉布斯的离散扩散模型校正器，称为吉布斯加速离散扩散（GADD）。GADD利用具体分数函数的结构，直接构建吉布斯后验似然，无需超出标准分数估计之外的任何额外训练。我们证明GADD实现了整体采样复杂度为$\mathcal{O}(\mathrm{polylog} (\varepsilon^{-1}))$，这是均匀速率离散扩散模型中基于扩散的采样器首次达到的速率。我们还通过数值实验展示了GADD在合成数据、零样本文本采样和零样本条件音乐生成中的实际优势。这些结果验证了理论，并表明GADD在样本质量与实际运行效率上均优于标准基线方法，包括朴素欧拉方法和CTMC校正器。此外，我们的理论分析引入了一种用于分析离散扩散模型中预测-校正方法的新框架，可能具有独立研究价值。与现有依赖吉尔萨诺夫测度变换技术的方法不同，我们的方法基于归纳论证，追踪预测迭代中误差传播，同时考虑校正更新中的不准确性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost of uniform-rate discrete diffusion models, this work introduces GADD, a Gibbs-based corrector that accelerates sampling without requiring additional training. It constructs Gibbs posterior likelihoods directly from the concrete score function, achieving a theoretical sampling complexity of O(polylog(ε⁻¹)), the first such rate for uniform-rate discrete diffusion. Experiments across synthetic data, zero-shot text, and music generation show GADD consistently outperforms baselines like Euler and CTMC correctors in both sample quality and wall-clock efficiency, validating the theoretical claims and offering a novel analytical framework for predictor-corrector methods in discrete diffusion.
为解决均匀速率离散扩散模型采样效率低的问题，本文提出GADD方法，一种基于Gibbs的校正器，无需额外训练即可加速采样。该方法直接从混凝土得分函数构建Gibbs后验似然，实现O(polylog(ε⁻¹))的理论采样复杂度，这是均匀速率离散扩散模型中首次达到该速率。在合成数据、零样本文本和音乐生成实验中，GADD在样本质量和实际效率上均优于Euler和CTMC等基线方法，验证了理论有效性，并提出了一种分析离散扩散模型预测-校正方法的新框架。
Feedforward 3D Editing Learns from Semantic-Part Transformation
Authors: Jiawei Weng, Saining Zhang, Zhenxin Diao, Peishuo Li, Henghaofan Zhang, Junhao Chen, Hao Zhao
First: 2026-05-26T17:51:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:51:59+00:00
Comments: 30 pages, 22 figures. Project Page: https://dennis-jwweng.github.io/pxform/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
3D editing is a fundamental capability for scalable 3D content creation. While image editing has rapidly evolved toward large-scale feedforward generative paradigms, 3D AI generation remains dominated by training-free editing pipelines. A central challenge of feedforward 3D editing lies in the lack of high-quality paired supervision. Editable 3D assets require simultaneous preservation of geometry, multi-view consistency, structural coherence, and localized edit controllability. Existing 3D editing datasets often rely on independently generated assets, image-mediated reconstruction or narrow edit taxonomies, leading to inaccurate localization, weak preservation, blurred edit boundaries, and limited semantic consistency. In this work, we introduce a new perspective: scalable feedforward 3D editing should be learned from semantic-part transformations. Based on this insight, we propose Pxform, a high-quality 3D editing dataset with over 100K consistent before/after editing pairs across seven edit types. Instead of treating objects as unstructured shapes, our pipeline grounds edits directly in semantic 3D parts. Built upon Pxform, we further propose PartFlow, a feedforward 3D editing network that injects source-aware latent control into pretrained 3D generative priors. PartFlow introduces mask-aware velocity preservation and render-space consistency supervision to jointly improve edit fidelity and source preservation, while requiring no 3D edit mask during inference. Extensive experiments demonstrate that high-quality semantic-part supervision substantially improves scalable 3D editing, enabling PartFlow to achieve state-of-the-art performance on both geometric and appearance editing benchmarks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：前馈3D编辑通过语义部件变换学习
3D编辑是可扩展3D内容创作的基础能力。尽管图像编辑已迅速演进为大规模前馈生成范式，3D人工智能生成仍主要依赖无训练编辑流程。前馈3D编辑的核心挑战在于缺乏高质量配对监督。可编辑的3D资产需同时保持几何结构、多视角一致性、结构连贯性及局部编辑可控性。现有3D编辑数据集常依赖独立生成的资产、图像中介重建或狭窄的编辑分类，导致定位不准、保真度弱、编辑边界模糊及语义一致性有限。本文提出新视角：可扩展前馈3D编辑应从语义部件变换中学习。基于此，我们提出Pxform，一个包含超10万对一致前后编辑样本、涵盖七类编辑的高质量3D编辑数据集。不同于将物体视为无结构形状，我们的流程直接在语义3D部件基础上进行编辑。基于Pxform，我们进一步提出PartFlow，一种前馈3D编辑网络，将源感知潜在控制注入预训练3D生成先验。PartFlow引入掩码感知速度保真与渲染空间一致性监督，联合提升编辑保真度与源保持能力，且推理时无需3D编辑掩码。大量实验表明，高质量语义部件监督显著提升可扩展3D编辑效果，使PartFlow在几何与外观编辑基准上均达到最先进水平。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of high-quality paired supervision in 3D editing and the limitations of existing datasets, this work proposes Pxform, a large-scale 3D editing dataset with over 100K consistent before/after pairs across seven edit types, grounded in semantic 3D parts. Building on this, PartFlow, a feedforward 3D editing network, injects source-aware latent control into pretrained 3D generative models and employs mask-aware velocity preservation and render-space consistency supervision to enhance edit fidelity and source preservation without requiring edit masks at inference. Experimental results show PartFlow achieves state-of-the-art performance on both geometric and appearance editing benchmarks, demonstrating that semantic-part supervision significantly improves scalable 3D editing.
为解决3D编辑缺乏高质量配对监督的问题，该研究提出从语义部件变换中学习前馈3D编辑。作者构建了Pxform数据集，包含超过10万组跨七类编辑的前后一致配对，将编辑直接锚定在语义3D部件上而非无结构形状。在此基础上，提出PartFlow网络，通过注入源感知的潜在控制到预训练3D生成模型中，并结合掩码感知速度保持与渲染空间一致性监督，在推理时无需编辑掩码，显著提升编辑保真度与源结构保留能力。实验表明，PartFlow在几何与外观编辑基准上均达到当前最优性能。
When Eyes Betray AI: Social Gaze Consistency as a Semantic Cue for AI-Generated Image Detection
Authors: Kim Jihyeon, Sohee Kim, Soosan Lee, Souhwan Jung, James Matthew Rehg, Hyesong Choi
First: 2026-05-26T17:50:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:50:17+00:00
Comments: 23 pages, 2 figures, 17 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent generative models have largely closed the gap on low-level artifacts - pixel fingerprints, frequency anomalies, upsampling traces - particularly in person-centric and partial-edit settings where the manipulated region is small and surrounded by photometrically authentic content. We introduce Social Gaze Consistency, a high-level semantic cue defined as the mutual coherence of gaze direction, head-eye alignment, and pupil placement between interacting individuals, and show that it constitutes a previously underutilized detection axis orthogonal to existing low-level paradigms. We instantiate this insight through three coupled mechanisms: (i) a controlled diagnostic dataset with region-specific perturbations of gaze-consistent imagery, where strict pair-level grouping forecloses generator-fingerprint memorization as an optimization-time shortcut rather than relying on augmentation; (ii) Block-Compositional Caption Supervision, which holds a single 5-block reasoning skeleton invariant across 1,250 macro-combined captions, decoupling reasoning consistency from surface diversity; (iii) Cross-architecture validation showing the same supervision improves a vision-language backbone (FakeVLM) by +3.7 pp on the COCOAI Interaction subset (balanced accuracy 67.8 -> 71.5) and +1.3 pp on the COCOAI Person subset (83.0 -> 84.3), with consistent gains on a vision-only backbone (Effort), evidencing a backbone-agnostic cue. Real- and fake-class recalls rise simultaneously, ruling out a "predict-all-fake" artifact. A four-step mechanistic account - paired-edit shortcut blocking, hard-to-easy difficulty transfer, CLIP prior preservation, and diffusion-family shared spectral weakness in periocular structure - explains why training on a single inpainter (FLUX.1-Fill) transfers to multi-generator suites. We will release the code upon acceptance to facilitate reproducibility.
中文标题/摘要
标题：当眼睛背叛AI：社交注视一致性作为AI生成图像检测的语义线索
近期生成模型已大幅缩小了低级伪影（像素指纹、频率异常、上采样痕迹）的差距，尤其在以人物为中心及局部编辑场景中，被篡改区域小且被真实光度内容包围。我们引入社交注视一致性，定义为互动个体间注视方向、头眼对齐与瞳孔位置的相互一致性，证明其为与现有低级范式正交的、此前未被充分利用的检测维度。我们通过三个耦合机制实现这一洞察：(i) 一个受控诊断数据集，包含区域特异性注视一致性图像扰动，严格按对级分组，避免生成器指纹记忆作为优化捷径，而非依赖数据增强；(ii) 块式组合式标题监督，保持单个5块推理骨架在1,250个宏观组合标题中不变，解耦推理一致性与表面多样性；(iii) 跨架构验证显示，相同监督机制使视觉-语言主干（FakeVLM）在COCOAI交互子集上准确率提升3.7个百分点（67.8→71.5），在COCOAI人物子集上提升1.3个百分点（83.0→84.3），视觉主干（Effort）亦有稳定提升，证明其主干无关性。真实与伪造类召回率同步上升，排除“全预测为伪造”伪迹。四步机制解释为何仅训练单一修复器（FLUX.1-Fill）即可泛化至多生成器套件：配对编辑捷径阻断、难易难度迁移、CLIP先验保留、扩散族在眼周结构的共享频谱弱点。论文接受后将开源代码以促进可复现性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the diminishing effectiveness of low-level artifact detection in AI-generated images, especially in person-centric scenarios, this work introduces Social Gaze Consistency—a high-level semantic cue based on the mutual coherence of gaze direction, head-eye alignment, and pupil placement between interacting individuals. The method employs a controlled diagnostic dataset with region-specific gaze perturbations to prevent generator-fingerprint memorization, coupled with Block-Compositional Caption Supervision to enforce consistent reasoning across diverse captions. Experiments show consistent performance gains across vision-language and vision-only backbones, with +3.7 pp and +1.3 pp improvements on COCOAI subsets respectively, and simultaneous increases in real and fake class recalls, indicating robustness. A mechanistic analysis attributes success to shortcut blocking, difficulty transfer, CLIP prior preservation, and shared spectral weaknesses in periocular regions, demonstrating transferability across generators.
鉴于低级伪影检测在AI生成图像中，特别是在人物相关场景下效果逐渐减弱，本文提出“社交注视一致性”这一高层语义线索，用于衡量交互个体间注视方向、头眼对齐与瞳孔位置的相互协调性。方法通过构造可控诊断数据集（含区域特异性注视扰动）、块级组合式图文监督（保持5块推理骨架不变）及跨架构验证（在视觉-语言与纯视觉模型上均有效）实现。实验表明：在COCOAI Interaction和Person子集上分别提升3.7%和1.3%准确率，且真实与伪造类召回率同步上升，排除“全预测为伪造”偏差。机制分析指出，其成功源于阻断生成器指纹捷径、难度迁移、CLIP先验保持及眼周区域扩散模型共有的频谱弱点，具备跨生成器迁移能力。
LiPUP-MA: A Residential Experience-centric Multi-Agent Framework for Living-in-the-loop Participatory Urban Planning
Authors: Hang Ni, Yuzhi Wang, Yizhi Song, Hao Liu
First: 2024-12-29T15:43:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:49:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Participatory Urban Planning (PUP) is increasingly supported by LLM-based agents, yet existing methods largely rely on static preference elicitation and one-shot stakeholder discussions, overlooking the cyclical nature of real-world planning, where residential life, experience collection, and plan adjustment continually interact. We propose Living-in-the-loop Participatory Urban Planning (LiPUP), a closed-loop paradigm that alternates between simulated residential living and experience-driven plan revision, while posing two key challenges: grounding scattered living experience in concrete urban contexts and translating subjective feedback into spatially coherent planning actions. To instantiate LiPUP, we introduce LiPUP-MA, an LLM-based multi-agent framework that constructs a Plan-centric Graph-based Experience Bank to organize urban-grounded residential feedback from living simulation and equips a Spatially-constrained Skill-augmented Planner agent to revise plans by harmonizing experiential, visual, and geospatial evidence. Experiments show that LiPUP-MA consistently outperforms baselines on both conventional static planning metrics and living-based metrics, while iterative LiPUP cycles further improve plan quality.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LiPUP-MA：面向居住体验中心的参与式城市规划闭环多智能体框架
参与式城市规划（PUP）日益依赖基于大语言模型（LLM）的智能体，但现有方法主要依赖静态偏好采集和单次利益相关者讨论，忽视了现实规划中居住生活、体验收集与方案调整持续交互的循环特性。我们提出“Living-in-the-loop Participatory Urban Planning”（LiPUP），一种闭环范式，交替进行模拟居住生活与体验驱动的方案修订，并面临两大关键挑战：将分散的居住体验锚定于具体城市语境，以及将主观反馈转化为空间连贯的规划行动。为实现LiPUP，我们提出LiPUP-MA，一种基于LLM的多智能体框架，构建以方案为中心的图结构体验库，组织模拟居住中产生的城市语境化反馈，并配备空间约束的技能增强规划智能体，通过整合体验、视觉与地理空间证据修订方案。实验表明，LiPUP-MA在传统静态规划指标与居住基础指标上均显著优于基线方法，而迭代的LiPUP循环进一步提升方案质量。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to model the cyclical, experience-driven nature of real-world urban planning, this work introduces LiPUP-MA, an LLM-based multi-agent framework that integrates simulated residential living with iterative plan revision. The method constructs a Plan-centric Graph-based Experience Bank to ground subjective feedback in urban contexts and employs a Spatially-constrained Skill-augmented Planner to translate experiential, visual, and geospatial data into coherent planning actions. Experimental results demonstrate that LiPUP-MA outperforms baseline methods on both static planning metrics and living-based evaluation criteria, with iterative cycles further enhancing plan quality.
为应对现实城市规划中居住体验驱动的循环性需求，本文提出LiPUP-MA框架，结合模拟居住与迭代规划调整。该方法构建以规划为中心的图结构体验库，将主观反馈锚定于城市语境，并配备空间约束技能增强规划器，利用体验、视觉与地理空间证据生成空间协调的调整方案。实验表明，LiPUP-MA在静态规划指标与居住导向评估中均优于基线方法，且迭代循环进一步提升规划质量。
MATCHA: Matching Text via Contrastive Semantic Alignment
Authors: Siran Li, Ece Sena Etoglu, Carsten Eickhoff, Seyed Ali Bahrainian
First: 2026-05-26T17:47:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:47:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Reliable evaluation is essential for understanding large language model (LLM) performance, yet today's go-to metrics, namely token-overlap scores (e.g., ROUGE) and embedding-based measures (e.g., BERTScore), often misjudge semantic similarity of documents. Our study shows that both token-overlap metrics and embedding-based metrics routinely assign nearly identical scores to texts that directly contradict each other, thereby potentially masking fundamental errors. We introduce MATCHA, an automatic metric that jointly rewards semantic agreement with a reference and penalizes contradictions. MATCHA employs a dual-view perspective that measures (i) proximity to the gold text and (ii) distance from an adversarially generated counterfactual contradiction. In eight public benchmarks, MATCHA outperforms popular metrics, compared with human annotations on question-answering, image caption generation, natural language inference, summarization, and semantic textual similarity tasks. On the TruthfulQA dataset (i.e., a dataset without a training set, where no embedding-based metrics could locally train on), this improvement in terms of matching texts with a reference reaches 18.38% over ROUGE-L and 20.82% over BERTScore. Both quantitative comparison and qualitative human assessments confirm the efficacy and validity of MATCHA and uncover fundamental weaknesses in pre-existing metrics. Compared with 23 embedding models, including top state-of-the-art ones, used as a metric similar to BERTScore, MATCHA remains the most accurate in distinguishing correct from incorrect statements solely based on a reference. Our code and metric are publicly available (https://github.com/Siran-Li/MATCHA).
中文标题/摘要
标题：MATCHA：通过对比语义对齐实现文本匹配
可靠的评估对于理解大语言模型（LLM）性能至关重要，但当前主流的评估指标，如词元重叠分数（如ROUGE）和基于嵌入的度量（如BERTScore），常误判文档间的语义相似性。我们的研究表明，这两种指标经常为相互矛盾的文本赋予几乎相同的分数，从而可能掩盖根本性错误。我们提出了MATCHA，一种自动评估指标，同时奖励与参考文本的语义一致性并惩罚矛盾。MATCHA采用双视角方法，分别衡量（i）与参考文本的接近程度，（ii）与对抗性生成的反事实矛盾文本的距离。在八个公开基准测试中，MATCHA在问答、图像生成、自然语言推理、摘要和语义文本相似度任务上，相比人工标注优于主流指标。在TruthfulQA数据集（无训练集，嵌入类指标无法局部训练）上，MATCHA在匹配参考文本方面的表现比ROUGE-L提升18.38%，比BERTScore提升20.82%。定量比较和定性人工评估均证实MATCHA的有效性和有效性，并揭示现有指标的根本缺陷。与23种嵌入模型（包括当前最先进的模型）相比，MATCHA在仅依据参考文本区分正确与错误陈述时仍是最准确的。我们的代码和指标已公开（https://github.com/Siran-Li/MATCHA）。
Summary / 总结
To address the limitations of existing metrics like ROUGE and BERTScore in accurately capturing semantic similarity, the authors propose MATCHA, a contrastive semantic alignment metric that jointly rewards semantic agreement with a reference and penalizes contradictions via a dual-view approach: measuring proximity to the gold text and distance from adversarially generated counterfactuals. Evaluated across eight benchmarks including QA, captioning, NLI, summarization, and STS, MATCHA consistently outperforms baseline metrics, achieving up to 20.82% higher accuracy than BERTScore on TruthfulQA—a dataset without training data—where no embedding-based metric could train locally. Quantitative and qualitative assessments confirm MATCHA’s superior ability to distinguish correct from incorrect text, even surpassing 23 state-of-the-art embedding models in this task.
为解决ROUGE和BERTScore等现有指标在捕捉语义相似性时的不足，作者提出MATCHA，一种通过对比语义对齐的自动评估指标，同时奖励与参考文本的语义一致性和惩罚矛盾内容，采用双视角机制：衡量与黄金标准文本的接近度及与对抗性生成的矛盾文本的距离。在包括问答、图像描述、自然语言推理、摘要和语义文本相似性在内的八个基准测试中，MATCHA持续优于现有指标，在无训练数据的TruthfulQA数据集上较ROUGE-L和BERTScore分别提升18.38%和20.82%，且在23种嵌入模型中仍是最准确区分正确与错误陈述的指标，验证了其有效性与鲁棒性。
AMARIS: A Memory-Augmented Rubric Improvement System for Rubric-Based Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Peilin Wu, Xinlu Zhang, Kun Wan, Wentian Zhao, Gang Wu, Xinya Du, Zhiyu Chen
First: 2026-05-18T16:06:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:47:05+00:00
Comments: Preprint. Under review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Rubric-based reward shaping provides interpretable and editable reward signals for fine-tuning LLMs via reinforcement learning (RL), but existing adaptive rubric methods typically update criteria from local evidence such as the current batch or instance-level comparisons. This local view discards diagnostic information produced during training, making it difficult to track recurring failures, evaluate previous rubric edits, or raise standards once earlier criteria become saturated. We introduce AMARIS, A Memory-Augmented Rubric Improvement System that grounds rubric updates in longitudinal training evidence. AMARIS stores rollout analyses, step-level summaries, and rubric update records in a persistent evaluation memory, then retrieves recent and semantically relevant history to revise rubrics. We evaluate AMARIS across science, medicine, instruction following, and creative writing under both global and instance-specific rubric settings. AMARIS improves over static, local-adaptive, and memory-ablated baselines, such as +2.8 points on GPQA-Diamond and +2.2 points on IFBench over the strongest baselines, while analysis shows that memory reduces oscillatory rubric edits and supports a progression from early failure correction to later curriculum advancement. AMARIS runs asynchronously alongside the normal RL loop, reducing blocking latency relative to synchronous rubric updates.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AMARIS：一种基于记忆增强的评分标准改进系统，用于基于评分标准的强化学习
基于评分标准的奖励塑造为通过强化学习（RL）微调大语言模型提供了可解释且可编辑的奖励信号，但现有自适应评分标准方法通常仅根据局部证据（如当前批次或实例级比较）更新标准。这种局部视角忽略了训练过程中产生的诊断信息，使得难以追踪重复失败、评估先前评分标准的修改，或在早期标准饱和后提升标准。我们提出了AMARIS，一种基于记忆增强的评分标准改进系统，其评分标准更新基于纵向训练证据。AMARIS将 rollout 分析、步骤级摘要和评分标准更新记录存储在持久化评估记忆中，然后检索近期且语义相关的过往记录以修订评分标准。我们在科学、医学、指令遵循和创意写作领域，在全局和实例特定评分标准设置下评估了AMARIS。AMARIS优于静态、局部自适应和无记忆基线方法，例如在GPQA-Diamond上提升2.8分，在IFBench上提升2.2分（超过最强基线），分析表明记忆减少了评分标准的振荡性修改，并支持从早期失败修正到后期课程进阶的过渡。AMARIS可与常规RL循环异步运行，相较于同步评分标准更新减少了阻塞延迟。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of local rubric adaptation in reinforcement learning, which neglects historical diagnostic data and hinders long-term improvement, AMARIS introduces a memory-augmented system that stores and retrieves longitudinal training evidence to guide rubric updates. It asynchronously integrates rollout analyses, step summaries, and past rubric revisions into a persistent memory, enabling context-aware revisions that reduce oscillatory edits and support curriculum progression. Experiments across science, medicine, instruction following, and creative writing show AMARIS outperforms static and local-adaptive baselines by +2.8 points on GPQA-Diamond and +2.2 points on IFBench, demonstrating its effectiveness in improving reward shaping and stabilizing learning trajectories.
为了解决现有基于评分标准的强化学习系统因依赖局部证据而难以追踪长期表现与优化评分标准的问题，AMARIS提出一种记忆增强框架，通过持久化存储训练过程中的回滚分析、步骤摘要和评分标准修订记录，实现基于长期历史的评分标准更新。该系统异步运行于常规强化学习循环中，有效减少延迟并避免评分标准的震荡调整，支持从早期错误纠正到后期课程进阶的渐进式优化。在科学、医学、指令遵循和创意写作等多个领域实验中，AMARIS相较静态和局部自适应基线分别提升2.8分（GPQA-Diamond）和2.2分（IFBench），验证了其在奖励对齐与稳定性方面的优越性。
Towards Controllable Image Generation through Representation-Conditioned Diffusion Models
Authors: Nithesh Chandher Karthikeyan, Jonas Unger, Gabriel Eilertsen
First: 2026-05-26T17:46:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:46:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion models have emerged as powerful tools for high-quality image generation and editing, but guiding these models to produce specific outputs remains a challenge. Conventional approaches rely on conditioning mechanisms, such as text prompts or semantic maps, which require extensively annotated datasets. In this preliminary work, we explore diffusion models conditioned on representations from a pre-trained self-supervised model. The self-conditioning mechanism not only improves the quality of unconditional image generation, but also provides a representation space that can be used to control the generation. We explore this conditioning space by identifying directions of variations, and demonstrate promising properties in terms of smoothness and disentanglement.
中文标题/摘要
