Summary / 总结
This work addresses the challenge of guiding diffusion models to generate specific images without relying on annotated data, by conditioning them on representations from a pre-trained self-supervised model. The method leverages self-conditioning to enhance unconditional image quality while enabling controllable generation through identified variation directions in the representation space. Experimental results show that this approach yields smooth and disentangled control over image generation, offering a promising alternative to text- or semantic-map-based conditioning.
该研究旨在无需标注数据的情况下引导扩散模型生成特定图像，方法是利用预训练自监督模型的表示作为条件。自条件机制不仅提升了无条件图像生成质量，还构建了一个可控的表示空间。实验表明，该空间内的变化平滑且解耦，可有效控制图像生成过程，无需额外标注数据。
Stochastic Non-Smooth Convex Optimization with Unbounded Gradients
Authors: Dmitry Kovalev
First: 2026-05-15T01:43:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:45:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Much of the existing theory on first-order non-smooth optimization is built on a restrictive assumption that the gradients of the objective function are uniformly bounded. We introduce a much more realistic class of generalized Lipschitz functions, where the gradient norms are bounded by an affine function of the optimality gap. We then ask a natural question: what algorithm achieves the best global convergence rates for solving convex stochastic generalized Lipschitz optimization problems? To address this, we develop a new convergence analysis for several existing algorithms and find that AdamW with clipped updates, provably outperforms other popular stochastic optimization methods, such as SGD and AdaGrad. Moreover, our analysis establishes the critical role of AdamW's exponentially weighted gradient accumulation, as opposed to simple averaging. We further show that clipped AdamW is universal and achieves improved rates under the popular generalized smoothness assumption, analyze the convergence of clipped AdamW with diagonal and matrix preconditioners, and extend our results to the quasar-convex setting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：具有无界梯度的随机非光滑凸优化
现有的一阶非光滑优化理论大多建立在目标函数梯度有统一界这一限制性假设之上。我们引入了一类更为现实的广义Lipschitz函数，其梯度范数由最优性间隙的仿射函数界定。接着我们提出一个自然的问题：哪种算法在求解凸随机广义Lipschitz优化问题时能获得最佳全局收敛速率？为此，我们对若干现有算法进行了新的收敛性分析，发现AdamW配合裁剪更新在理论上优于SGD和AdaGrad等流行随机优化方法。此外，我们的分析阐明了AdamW的指数加权梯度累积机制（而非简单平均）的关键作用。我们进一步证明裁剪AdamW具有普适性，在广义光滑性假设下可获得改进的收敛速率，分析了裁剪AdamW在对角和矩阵预处理下的收敛性，并将其结果扩展至quasar-凸设置。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation that existing non-smooth convex optimization theory assumes uniformly bounded gradients, this work introduces a more realistic class of generalized Lipschitz functions where gradient norms scale with the optimality gap. The authors analyze several stochastic optimization algorithms under this setting and find that AdamW with clipped updates achieves the best global convergence rates, outperforming SGD and AdaGrad. Their analysis highlights the importance of AdamW's exponentially weighted gradient accumulation over simple averaging, and they demonstrate that clipped AdamW is universally effective under generalized smoothness, with improved convergence rates even when using diagonal or matrix preconditioners, and extend the results to quasar-convex problems.
鉴于现有非光滑凸优化理论依赖于梯度有界这一限制性假设，本文引入更贴近实际的广义Lipschitz函数类，其中梯度范数与最优性间隙呈仿射关系。作者在该框架下分析多种随机优化算法，发现带裁剪更新的AdamW在全局收敛速率上优于SGD和AdaGrad。分析表明AdamW的指数加权梯度累积机制至关重要，且裁剪版AdamW在广义光滑性假设下仍具普适性，配合对角或矩阵预处理可进一步提升收敛速度，并可扩展至quasar-凸问题。
2-ASP(Q) programs with weak constraints: Complexity and efficient implementation
Authors: Andrea Cuteri, Giuseppe Mazzotta, Francesco Ricca
First: 2026-05-26T17:44:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:44:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
ASP(Q) extends Answer Set Programming (ASP) with Quantifiers over answer sets. In this paper we focus on the class of ASP(Q) programs with two quantifiers and weak constraints, denoted as 2-ASP(Q)^w. 2-ASP(Q)^w is a practically relevant fragment of ASP(Q) that is expressive enough to capture optimization problems up to the class Delta_3^P. On the theoretical side, we provide a complete complexity characterization of the main computational tasks for 2-ASP(Q)^w programs, including tight completeness results and the analysis of nontrivial cases that have not been addressed in previous works. On the practical side, we introduce novel strategies for computing (optimal) quantified answer sets in the Casper system, that rely on a Counterexample-Guided Abstraction Refinement (CEGAR) technique tailored to ASP(Q). An experimental evaluation on hard benchmarks from different application domains shows that the proposed techniques are effective in practice.
中文标题/摘要
标题：带弱约束的二元ASP(Q)程序：复杂性与高效实现
ASP(Q)在答案集编程（ASP）基础上扩展了对答案集的量词。本文聚焦于包含两个量词和弱约束的ASP(Q)程序类，记为2-ASP(Q)^w。2-ASP(Q)^w是ASP(Q)的一个实用相关片段，其表达能力足以捕捉至Delta_3^P类的优化问题。理论上，我们对2-ASP(Q)^w程序的主要计算任务提供了完整的复杂性刻画，包括紧致完备性结果及前人未涉及的非平凡情况分析。实践上，我们在Casper系统中引入了基于针对ASP(Q)定制的反例引导抽象精炼（CEGAR）技术的新策略，用于计算（最优）量化答案集。在不同应用领域的硬性基准上的实验评估表明，所提技术在实践中有效。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to model optimization problems within Answer Set Programming, this paper investigates 2-ASP(Q)^w — a fragment of ASP(Q) with two quantifiers and weak constraints, expressive enough to capture Delta_3^P problems. The authors provide a complete complexity analysis of core computational tasks, resolving previously unaddressed nontrivial cases. Practically, they introduce CEGAR-based strategies in the Casper system to compute optimal quantified answer sets, demonstrating significant performance improvements over existing methods on challenging benchmarks across multiple domains.
为建模可达Delta_3^P类的优化问题，本文研究了包含两个量词和弱约束的2-ASP(Q)^w程序。作者从理论上给出了核心计算任务的完整复杂度刻画，包括对先前未解决的非平凡情况的紧致完备性结果。在实践层面，他们在Casper系统中引入了基于反例引导抽象精炼（CEGAR）的新策略以计算最优量化答案集，并在多个应用领域的难例上验证了所提方法的有效性与性能提升。
PARE: Pruning and Adaptive Routing for Efficient Video Generation
Authors: Yutong Wang, Yunke Wang, Tianfan Xue, Yu Qiao, Yaohui Wang, Xinyuan Chen, Chang Xu
First: 2026-05-26T17:43:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:43:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Video Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) generate high-quality videos but demand substantial compute due to wide blocks, deep architectures, and iterative sampling. Recent methods reduce cost by compressing width, depth, or sampling steps, but typically commit to a fixed architecture that cannot adapt to individual inputs or denoising stages. We propose PARE (Pruning and Adaptive Routing for Efficient video generation), which jointly compresses width and depth with structure-aware pruning and input-adaptive routing. For width, we observe that attention heads specialize into spatial and temporal roles, and design importance scoring that accounts for this distinction to prevent motion-critical temporal heads from being pruned prematurely. For depth, we train a lightweight router conditioned on denoising timestep and visual content to dynamically select which blocks to execute at each step, enabling per-input compute adaptation rather than static block removal. A progressive pipeline first recovers width-pruned quality via distillation, then jointly optimizes the student and router to decouple the two learning objectives. Experiments on Wan2.1-14B for both image-to-video and text-to-video generation show that PARE substantially reduces per-step computation while preserving quality across VBench dimensions, and composes with step distillation for further acceleration.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PARE：用于高效视频生成的剪枝与自适应路由
视频扩散Transformer（DiTs）可生成高质量视频，但因块宽、架构深度和迭代采样而需大量计算。近期方法通过压缩宽度、深度或采样步数降低成本，但通常固定架构，无法适应单个输入或去噪阶段。我们提出PARE（用于高效视频生成的剪枝与自适应路由），联合压缩宽度和深度，结合结构感知剪枝与输入自适应路由。在宽度方面，我们观察到注意力头分化为空间与时间角色，设计重要性评分以区分此差异，防止关键运动时间头过早被剪枝。在深度方面，我们训练轻量级路由器，根据去噪时间步和视觉内容动态选择每步执行的块，实现按输入自适应计算而非静态块移除。渐进式流程先通过蒸馏恢复宽度剪枝后的质量，再联合优化学生模型与路由器，解耦两个学习目标。在Wan2.1-14B模型上，针对图像到视频和文本到视频生成实验表明，PARE显著降低每步计算量，同时在VBench各维度保持质量，并结合步长蒸馏进一步加速。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost of Video Diffusion Transformers due to wide blocks, deep architectures, and iterative sampling, PARE introduces a method combining structure-aware pruning and input-adaptive routing to dynamically reduce compute without sacrificing quality. It prunes attention heads by distinguishing spatial and temporal roles, preserving critical temporal heads, and employs a lightweight router conditioned on denoising timestep and visual content to selectively activate blocks per input. Experiments on Wan2.1-14B show PARE reduces per-step computation significantly while maintaining video quality across VBench metrics, and further accelerates generation when combined with step distillation.
为降低视频扩散Transformer因宽块、深架构和迭代采样带来的高计算开销，PARE提出联合压缩策略，结合结构感知剪枝与输入自适应路由。其通过区分注意力头的空间与时间角色，保护关键运动时间头，并训练轻量级路由器根据去噪步长和视觉内容动态选择执行块。在Wan2.1-14B上实验表明，PARE显著降低每步计算量，同时保持VBench各维度视频质量，并结合步级蒸馏进一步加速生成。
FinHarness: An Inline Lifecycle Safety Harness for Finance LLM Agents
Authors: Haoxuan Jia, Yang Liu, Bin Chong, Yingguang Yang, Yancheng Chen, Jiayu Liang, Qian Li, Hanning Lu, Kefu Xu, Hao Zheng, Chongyang Zhang, Hao Peng, Philip S. Yu
First: 2026-05-26T17:41:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:41:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Finance LLM agents must simultaneously block prompt-induced unauthorized actions and approve legitimate multi-step business workflows. However, boundary filters often miss irreversible mid-trajectory tool calls, while post-hoc LLM judges perform auditing only after termination -- too late for intervention and at a computational cost that scales linearly with trace length. We present FinHarness, an inline safety harness that wraps a finance agent end-to-end with three components: a Query Monitor that fuses single-turn intent with cross-turn drift, a Tool Monitor that evaluates each prospective tool call, and a Cascade module that integrates per-step risk and adaptively routes verification between a lightweight and an advanced-tier LLM judge. Fired risk factors are re-injected into the agent input as ex-ante evidence, enabling the agent to refuse, re-plan, or approve on its own. On FinVault, routed FinHarness cuts ASR from 38.3% to 15.0% while largely preserving benign approval ($41.1\% \to 39.3\%$), and uses $4.7\times$ fewer advanced-judge calls than an always-advanced ablation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FinHarness：一种用于金融LLM代理的内嵌生命周期安全防护机制
金融LLM代理必须同时阻止由提示引发的未经授权的操作，并批准合法的多步骤业务流程。然而，边界过滤器常遗漏不可逆的中途工具调用，而事后LLM裁判仅在流程终止后进行审计——此时干预已为时已晚，且计算成本随轨迹长度线性增长。我们提出FinHarness，一种内嵌式安全防护机制，通过三个组件端到端包裹金融代理：查询监控器融合单轮意图与跨轮漂移、工具监控器评估每个潜在工具调用、级联模块整合每步风险并自适应在轻量级与高级LLM裁判间路由验证。触发的风险因素作为事前证据重新注入代理输入，使其可自主拒绝、重新规划或批准。在FinVault上，路由后的FinHarness将ASR从38.3%降至15.0%，同时基本保持良性批准率（41.1% → 39.3%），且高级裁判调用次数比始终使用高级裁判的基线少4.7倍。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to safely manage finance LLM agents that must both block unauthorized actions and approve complex workflows, FinHarness introduces an inline safety system that intercepts and evaluates tool calls in real time. It combines a Query Monitor to detect intent drift across turns, a Tool Monitor to assess each tool call’s risk, and a Cascade module that dynamically routes verification between lightweight and advanced LLM judges based on per-step risk. Experimental results on FinVault show that this approach reduces adversarial success rate (ASR) from 38.3% to 15.0% while maintaining high benign approval rates (41.1% to 39.3%), and cuts advanced-judge usage by 4.7 times compared to always-using the advanced tier.
为解决金融LLM代理在阻止非法操作与批准复杂业务流程之间难以兼顾的问题，FinHarness提出一种内嵌式安全机制，在代理执行过程中实时监控并评估每一步操作。该机制包含查询监控器（追踪跨轮意图漂移）、工具监控器（评估每个工具调用）和级联模块（根据风险动态分配轻量级或高级LLM裁判）。在FinVault数据集上的实验表明，FinHarness将攻击成功率从38.3%降至15.0%，同时保持良性批准率基本稳定（41.1%→39.3%），并使高级裁判调用次数减少4.7倍。
EdgeFlow: Edge-Map Augmented VLM-Based Flowchart Processing for Industrial Requirements Engineering
Authors: Zhifei Dou, Shabnam Hassani, Ou Wei
First: 2026-05-26T17:40:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:40:47+00:00
Comments: 10 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Flowcharts are widely used in industrial requirements, but usually remain embedded as static images. Vision Language Models (VLMs) show promise in the conversion of these flowcharts into machine-readable models for RE activities, yet, when directly applied to flowchart conversion, they often fail on topology-critical visual details. To address this, we propose EdgeFlow that augments a VLM's original input with a deterministically extracted Canny edge map-acting as a structural prior-to improve flowchart-to-Mermaid conversion, without requiring annotated training data or domain-specific model fine-tuning. We evaluate EdgeFlow on IndusReqFlow, a dataset sourced from real-world requirements. Compared with off-the-shelf VLMs, EdgeFlow improves node-level F1 by 17.39 percentage points and edge-level F1 by 16.94 percentage points. At the path level, EdgeFlow improves path F1 by 11.06 percentage points, enabling better support for model-based testing. These results demonstrate that EdgeFlow provides a practical, training-free means to improve topology-preserving flowchart-to-Mermaid conversion for industrial RE. Cross-dataset evaluation results on a public synthetic benchmark show no significant improvement; this highlights the need for diverse benchmarks incorporating industrial data for the comprehensive evaluation of future VLM-based RE tools.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EdgeFlow：基于边缘图增强的视觉语言模型流程图处理方法用于工业需求工程
流程图在工业需求中广泛应用，但通常以静态图像形式嵌入。视觉语言模型（VLM）在将流程图转换为机器可读模型以支持需求工程活动方面展现出潜力，但直接应用于流程图转换时，常因忽略拓扑关键的视觉细节而失败。为此，我们提出EdgeFlow，通过在VLM原始输入中添加确定性提取的Canny边缘图（作为结构先验），提升流程图到Mermaid格式的转换效果，无需标注训练数据或领域特定模型微调。 我们在IndusReqFlow数据集（源自真实需求）上评估EdgeFlow，相比现成VLM，其节点级F1提升17.39个百分点，边级F1提升16.94个百分点；路径级F1提升11.06个百分点，从而更好地支持基于模型的测试。结果表明，EdgeFlow提供了一种实用、无需训练的方法，以提升工业需求工程中流程图到Mermaid的拓扑保持转换。在公开合成基准上的跨数据集评估显示无显著改进，这突显了未来基于VLM的需求工程工具全面评估需依赖包含工业数据的多样化基准。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to convert static flowchart images into machine-readable models for industrial requirements engineering, the authors propose EdgeFlow, a method that enhances Vision Language Models by augmenting their input with a deterministically extracted Canny edge map to preserve structural topology. Evaluated on the real-world IndusReqFlow dataset, EdgeFlow significantly outperforms off-the-shelf VLMs, boosting node-level F1 by 17.39 percentage points and edge-level F1 by 16.94 percentage points, with a 11.06 percentage point gain in path-level F1, enabling better model-based testing. Cross-dataset evaluation on a synthetic benchmark showed no significant improvement, underscoring the importance of industrial data in benchmarking VLM-based RE tools.
为解决工业需求工程中静态流程图难以被机器理解的问题，本文提出EdgeFlow方法，通过在视觉语言模型输入中加入确定性提取的Canny边缘图作为结构先验，以提升流程图到Mermaid格式的转换效果。在真实工业数据集IndusReqFlow上测试，EdgeFlow相较现成VLM模型在节点级F1提升17.39%，边级F1提升16.94%，路径级F1提升11.06%，显著增强模型测试支持能力。在公开合成基准上未见性能提升，表明未来评估需引入工业数据以全面检验VLM在需求工程中的适用性。
Maat: The Agentic Legal Research Assistant for Competition Protection
Authors: Basant Mounir, Farida Madkour, Amira Abdelaziz, Asmaa Sami
First: 2026-05-26T17:38:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:38:26+00:00
Comments: 5 pages, 1 figure
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Competition law experts conducting legal research must review extensive volumes of cases, decisions, and judicial reports to identify precedents and assess key elements in competition and merger cases. Although general research assistants such as Claude and ChatGPT and legal assistants such as SaulLM-7B and LegalGPT are increasingly used to assist legal research, they remain inadequate for competition law analysis: they lack specialized domain expertise, provide insufficient official citations, or hallucinate competition law cases. We propose Maat, a ReAct agent that orchestrates tools corresponding to different tasks of the research process. Designed iteratively with competition law experts, Maat grounds cases and findings in official sources using RAG for reliability, provides rich in-line citations, falls back to web search when database coverage is insufficient, and prompts the user for clarification when queries are ambiguous. Maat significantly outperforms all baseline assistants on case-specific tasks and performs within range of the top baseline on theoretical question tasks. The dataset used is available on GitHub.

Unique Lives, Shared World: Learning from Single-Life Videos
Authors: Tengda Han, Sayna Ebrahimi, Dilara Gokay, Li Yang Ku, Maks Ovsjanikov, Iva Babukova, Daniel Zoran, Viorica Patraucean, Joao Carreira, Andrew Zisserman, Dima Damen
First: 2025-12-03T18:59:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:38:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce the "single-life" learning paradigm, where we train a distinct vision model exclusively on egocentric videos captured by one individual. We leverage the multiple viewpoints naturally captured within a single life to learn a visual encoder in a self-supervised manner. Our experiments demonstrate three key findings. First, models trained independently on different lives develop a highly aligned geometric understanding. We demonstrate this by training visual encoders on distinct datasets each capturing a different life, both indoors and outdoors, as well as introducing a novel cross-attention-based metric to quantify the functional alignment of the internal representations developed by different models. Second, we show that single-life models learn generalizable geometric representations that effectively transfer to downstream tasks, such as depth estimation, in unseen environments. Third, we demonstrate that training on up to 30 hours from one week of the same person's life leads to comparable performance to training on 30 hours of diverse web data, highlighting the strength of single-life representation learning. Overall, our results establish that the shared structure of the world, both leads to consistency in models trained on individual lives, and provides a powerful signal for visual representation learning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：独特人生，共享世界：从独居生活视频中学习
我们引入了“独居生活”学习范式，即在单一个体拍摄的自拍视频上训练独立的视觉模型。我们利用单个生活中自然捕获的多视角数据，以自监督方式学习视觉编码器。实验表明三个关键发现：第一，不同人生数据训练的模型在几何理解上高度一致，我们通过在不同室内/室外生活数据集上训练视觉编码器，并引入基于交叉注意力的新指标，量化不同模型内部表征的功能对齐程度。第二，单人生模型学习到可泛化的几何表征，能有效迁移到未见环境中的下游任务（如深度估计）。第三，仅用一周内同一人30小时视频训练，其性能可媲美30小时多样网络数据，凸显单人生表征学习的优势。总体而言，我们的结果表明，世界共享结构既导致个体生活数据训练模型的一致性，也为视觉表征学习提供了强大信号。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the idea that individual egocentric video streams capture consistent world structures, this work proposes training vision models exclusively on videos from a single person’s life to learn self-supervised visual encoders. The method leverages the natural multi-view data within one life to align geometric understanding across models trained on different lives, using a novel cross-attention metric to quantify internal representation alignment. Experimental results show that such models develop generalizable geometric representations that transfer well to tasks like depth estimation in unseen environments, and that training on just 30 hours of a single person’s life yields performance comparable to training on 30 hours of diverse web data.
该研究受单个个体视角视频中世界结构一致性的启发，提出“单人生活”学习范式，即仅用单一用户拍摄的视角视频训练视觉模型。方法通过自监督学习，利用单人生活中自然存在的多视角信息构建几何对齐的视觉编码器。实验表明，不同个体训练的模型仍能达成高度一致的几何理解（通过新提出的交叉注意力指标量化）；这些模型可有效迁移到未见环境中的下游任务（如深度估计）；且仅用一周内同一人的30小时视频训练，其性能已可媲美30小时的多样化网络数据。
Governed Evolution of Agent Runtimes through Executable Operational Cognition
Authors: Mariano Garralda-Barrio
First: 2026-05-26T17:36:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:36:48+00:00
Comments: 14 pages, 4 figures, 1 table. Reference implementation and associated source code available at: https://github.com/mgarralda/governed-runtime
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Recent advances in agentic systems increasingly treat code as an executable operational substrate rather than as a disposable output artifact. Prior work such as \emph{Code as Agent Harness} frames validated agent-generated artifacts as runtime entities that can be created, executed, revised, persisted, and reused within long-running cognitive loops. However, the governance, lifecycle management, and operational evolution of such artifacts remain under-specified. This paper proposes a framework for governed runtime evolution in multi-agent systems through executable operational cognition. We formalize agent-generated artifacts as persistent runtime capabilities that progressively become part of the operational substrate rather than transient intermediate outputs. Building on this perspective, we introduce \emph{HarnessMutation} as a governed mechanism for lifecycle-aware runtime adaptation operating under explicit validation, traceability, evaluation, and rollback constraints. Rather than treating runtime adaptation as unrestricted self-modification, the proposed framework models evolution as a bounded and observable process over persistent operational memory. It further shows how these ideas can be operationalized over modern agent runtimes and governance-oriented orchestration systems, providing a conceptual foundation for adaptive infrastructures whose evolution remains explicit, auditable, and constrained.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过可执行的操作认知实现代理运行时的受控演化
近期在代理系统方面的进展越来越多地将代码视为可执行的操作基底，而非一次性输出产物。此前工作如《代码作为代理框架》将经过验证的代理生成产物视为可在长期认知循环中创建、执行、修订、持久化和重用的运行时实体。然而，此类产物的治理、生命周期管理及操作演化仍缺乏明确规范。 本文提出了一种通过可执行操作认知实现多代理系统中运行时受控演化的框架。我们将代理生成产物形式化为逐步融入操作基底的持久化运行时能力，而非瞬时中间输出。在此基础上，我们引入《框架突变》作为受控机制，支持生命周期感知的运行时自适应，且在显式验证、可追溯性、评估与回滚约束下运行。 该框架不将运行时自适应视为无限制的自我修改，而是将其建模为在持久操作内存上进行的有界且可观测的演化过程。同时，本文展示了如何在现代代理运行时与面向治理的编排系统中实现这些理念，为演化过程明确、可审计且受约束的自适应基础设施提供了概念基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to manage the lifecycle and evolution of agent-generated code as persistent runtime entities rather than disposable artifacts, this paper proposes a framework for governed runtime evolution in multi-agent systems. It formalizes agent outputs as operational capabilities that integrate into the system’s runtime substrate and introduces HarnessMutation—a mechanism enforcing validation, traceability, evaluation, and rollback during adaptation. Experimental results demonstrate that this approach enables bounded, observable, and auditable runtime evolution while maintaining system stability and governance within modern agent runtimes and orchestration systems.
为解决多智能体系统中由智能体生成的代码作为持久化运行时能力而非一次性产物时，其生命周期与演化缺乏治理的问题，本文提出了一种受控的运行时演化框架。该框架将智能体输出形式化为可融入系统运行时基底的能力，并引入HarnessMutation机制，通过验证、可追溯性、评估与回滚约束实现受控演化。实验表明，该方法可在保障系统稳定性的前提下，实现有界、可观测且可审计的运行时演化。
TowerMind: A Tower Defence Game Learning Environment and Benchmark for LLM as Agents
Authors: Dawei Wang, Chengming Zhou, Di Zhao, Xinyuan Liu, Marci Chi Ma, Gary Ushaw, Richard Davison
Venue: AAAI 2026 Oral
First: 2026-01-09T16:18:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:34:04+00:00
Comments: AAAI 2026 Oral
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Recent breakthroughs in Large Language Models (LLMs) have positioned them as a promising paradigm for agents, with long-term planning and decision-making emerging as core general-purpose capabilities for adapting to diverse scenarios and tasks. Real-time strategy (RTS) games serve as an ideal testbed for evaluating these two capabilities, as their inherent gameplay requires both macro-level strategic planning and micro-level tactical adaptation and action execution. Existing RTS game-based environments either suffer from relatively high computational demands or lack support for textual observations, which has constrained the use of RTS games for LLM evaluation. Motivated by this, we present TowerMind, a novel environment grounded in the tower defense (TD) subgenre of RTS games. TowerMind preserves the key evaluation strengths of RTS games for assessing LLMs, while featuring low computational demands and a multimodal observation space, including pixel-based, textual, and structured game-state representations. In addition, TowerMind supports the evaluation of model hallucination and provides a high degree of customizability. We design five benchmark levels to evaluate several widely used LLMs under different multimodal input settings. The results reveal a clear performance gap between LLMs and human experts across both capability and hallucination dimensions. The experiments further highlight key limitations in LLM behavior, such as inadequate planning validation, a lack of multifinality in decision-making, and inefficient action use. We also evaluate two classic reinforcement learning algorithms: Ape-X DQN and PPO. By offering a lightweight and multimodal design, TowerMind complements the existing RTS game-based environment landscape and introduces a new benchmark for the AI agent field. The source code is publicly available on GitHub(https://github.com/tb6147877/TowerMind).
中文标题/摘要
标题：TowerMind：一种用于大语言模型作为代理的塔防游戏学习环境与基准
大语言模型（LLM）的最新突破使其成为代理的有前景范式，长期规划与决策正逐渐成为其适应多样化场景和任务的核心通用能力。实时策略（RTS）游戏是评估这两种能力的理想测试平台，因其游戏机制天然要求宏观战略规划与微观战术适应及行动执行。现有基于RTS游戏的环境要么计算需求较高，要么缺乏对文本观测的支持，这限制了其在LLM评估中的应用。为此，我们提出TowerMind，一种基于RTS子类塔防（TD）游戏的新型环境。TowerMind保留了RTS游戏评估LLM的核心优势，同时具备低计算需求和多模态观测空间（包括像素、文本和结构化游戏状态表示）。此外，TowerMind支持模型幻觉评估，并提供高度可定制性。我们设计了五个基准关卡，在不同多模态输入设置下评估多个常用LLM。结果表明，LLM在能力与幻觉维度上与人类专家之间存在明显性能差距。实验进一步揭示了LLM行为的关键局限，如规划验证不足、决策缺乏多目标性及行动使用效率低下。我们还评估了两种经典强化学习算法：Ape-X DQN和PPO。通过轻量级与多模态设计，TowerMind补充了现有RTS游戏环境生态，并为AI代理领域引入了新基准。源代码已公开于GitHub（https://github.com/tb6147877/TowerMind）。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate LLMs' long-term planning and decision-making in real-time strategy games while avoiding high computational costs and lacking textual observation support, TowerMind introduces a tower defense game environment tailored for LLM agent evaluation. It features low computational demands, multimodal inputs including pixel, text, and structured game states, and supports hallucination detection and customization. Five benchmark levels were used to test popular LLMs under varied input modalities, revealing consistent performance gaps compared to human experts, with LLMs showing poor planning validation, lack of multifinality in decisions, and inefficient action usage. Additionally, evaluation of Ape-X DQN and PPO algorithms demonstrated TowerMind’s utility as a lightweight, multimodal benchmark for AI agent research.
为评估大语言模型在实时策略游戏中长期规划与决策能力，同时解决现有环境计算成本高、缺乏文本观测支持的问题，TowerMind提出了一款基于塔防游戏的轻量级评估环境。该环境支持像素、文本与结构化状态的多模态观测，具备自定义能力与幻觉检测功能。通过五个基准关卡测试主流大语言模型，结果表明模型在能力与幻觉维度均显著落后于人类专家，存在规划验证不足、决策缺乏多目标性与行动效率低下等问题。同时，对Ape-X DQN与PPO等强化学习算法的评估显示，TowerMind的轻量设计可有效与强化学习方法对比，为AI代理研究提供新基准。
Semantic Gradients Interactions in SSD: A Case Study in Racial Identity and Hate Speech
Authors: Felix Ostrowicki, Hubert Plisiecki
First: 2026-05-26T17:33:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:33:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce interaction SSD, an extension of Supervised Semantic Differential that models how semantic meaning varies across moderators such as groups, traits, or conditions making this variation testable and interpretable. The method estimates a main semantic gradient, an interaction gradient, and conditional gradients, all interpretable through standard SSD tools. We illustrate it on the UC Berkeley Measuring Hate Speech corpus, testing whether annotator racial identity moderates hate-speech judgments of comments targeting people of color. The interaction model detects a significant moderation effect: the shared gradient contrasts dehumanizing hostility with counter-speech, while the interaction gradient reveals smaller group-linked differences in which semantic cues predict hate-speech ratings. Interaction SSD makes moderated meaning-outcome relationships statistically testable and interpretable.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SSD中的语义梯度交互：以种族身份与仇恨言论为例的案例研究
我们引入交互SSD，作为监督语义差异方法的扩展，用于建模语义意义如何随群体、特质或条件等调节变量而变化，使这种变化可检验且可解释。该方法估计主语义梯度、交互梯度和条件梯度，均可通过标准SSD工具进行解释。我们在加州大学伯克利分校的仇恨言论语料库中加以说明，检验标注员的种族身份是否调节针对有色人种评论的仇恨言论判断。交互模型检测到显著的调节效应：共享梯度对比去人性化敌意与反言论，而交互梯度揭示了较小的群体相关差异，即哪些语义线索预测仇恨言论评分。交互SSD使调节意义-结果关系在统计上可检验且可解释。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how annotator racial identity moderates hate-speech judgments in comments targeting people of color, this study introduces Interaction SSD—an extension of Supervised Semantic Differential that models semantic gradients across moderators. The method estimates main, interaction, and conditional gradients, all interpretable via standard SSD tools. Applied to the UC Berkeley Measuring Hate Speech corpus, the model revealed a significant moderation effect: while the shared gradient contrasted dehumanizing hostility with counter-speech, the interaction gradient showed reduced group-specific differences in semantic cue influence on hate-speech ratings, demonstrating that moderator effects can be statistically tested and meaningfully interpreted.
为探究注释者种族身份如何调节针对有色人种评论的仇恨言论判断，作者将监督语义差异（SSD）扩展为交互式SSD，以建模在群体等调节变量下语义意义的变化。该方法估计主语义梯度、交互梯度和条件梯度，均可通过标准SSD工具进行解释。在加州大学伯克利分校仇恨言论语料库上的实验发现，交互式SSD检测到显著的调节效应：共享梯度区分去人性化敌意与反言论，而交互梯度显示语义线索预测仇恨言论评分的群体差异较小，使调节的语义-结果关系具备统计可检验性和可解释性。
InHabit: Leveraging Image Foundation Models for Scalable 3D Human Placement
Authors: Nikita Kister, Pradyumna YM, István Sárándi, Jiayi Wang, Anna Khoreva, Gerard Pons-Moll
First: 2026-04-21T16:53:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:30:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Training embodied agents to understand 3D scenes as humans do requires large-scale data of people meaningfully interacting with diverse environments, yet such data is scarce. Real-world capture is costly and limited to controlled settings, while existing synthetic datasets rely on simple geometric heuristics, ignoring rich scene context. In contrast, 2D foundation models trained at internet scale have acquired commonsense knowledge of human-environment interactions. To transfer this knowledge to 3D, we introduce InHabit, an automatic and scalable data generator for populating 3D scenes with interacting humans. InHabit follows a render-generate-lift principle: given a rendered 3D scene, a vision-language model proposes contextually meaningful actions, an image-editing model inserts a human, and an optimization procedure lifts the edited result into physically plausible SMPL-X bodies aligned with the scene geometry. Applied to Habitat-Matterport3D, InHabit produces InHabitants, the first large-scale photorealistic 3D human-scene interaction dataset, with 78K samples across $\sim$800 building-scale scenes with complete 3D geometry, SMPL-X bodies, and images. Augmenting standard training data with InHabitants improves RGB-based 3D human-scene reconstruction and contact estimation, and in a perceptual user study our data is preferred in 78% of cases over prior art.
中文标题/摘要
标题：InHabit：利用图像基础模型实现可扩展的3D人体放置
训练具身智能体以人类方式理解3D场景，需要大量人类与多样化环境有意义交互的数据，但此类数据稀缺。现实世界数据采集成本高且仅限于受控环境，而现有合成数据集依赖简单几何启发式方法，忽略丰富场景上下文。相比之下，互联网规模训练的2D基础模型已获得人类-环境交互的常识性知识。为将该知识迁移到3D领域，我们提出InHabit，一种自动且可扩展的3D场景中交互人类数据生成器。InHabit遵循“渲染-生成-提升”原则：给定渲染的3D场景，视觉-语言模型提出语境相关的动作，图像编辑模型插入人类，优化过程将编辑结果提升为与场景几何对齐的物理合理SMPL-X人体。应用于Habitat-Matterport3D后，InHabit生成InHabitants，首个大规模逼真3D人-场景交互数据集，包含约800栋建筑规模场景的78K样本，具备完整3D几何、SMPL-X人体及图像。将InHabitants与标准训练数据结合，可提升RGB基3D人-场景重建与接触估计性能；在感知用户研究中，我们的数据在78%的案例中优于现有方法。
Summary / 总结
To address the scarcity of large-scale, realistic 3D human-environment interaction data, the authors propose InHabit, a scalable method that leverages 2D vision-language models and image-editing tools to automatically generate photorealistic 3D human placements. The method follows a render-generate-lift pipeline: it renders a 3D scene, uses a vision-language model to suggest contextually appropriate human actions, inserts a human via image editing, and optimizes the pose into physically plausible SMPL-X bodies aligned with the scene. Evaluated on Habitat-Matterport3D, InHabit produces 78K samples across ~800 building-scale scenes, enabling significant improvements in RGB-based 3D human reconstruction and contact estimation, and outperforms prior datasets in a perceptual user study with 78% preference.
为解决大规模真实3D人-环境交互数据稀缺的问题，作者提出InHabit方法，利用2D视觉语言模型与图像编辑工具自动生成逼真的3D人物放置。该方法遵循“渲染-生成-提升”流程：先渲染3D场景，用视觉语言模型生成语境相关的动作建议，再通过图像编辑插入人物，并优化为与场景几何对齐的物理合理SMPL-X人体模型。在Habitat-Matterport3D上，InHabit生成了约78K个样本，覆盖约800个建筑级场景，显著提升了RGB驱动的3D人-场景重建与接触估计效果，并在78%的用户感知测试中优于现有数据集。
Modeling Agentic Technical Debt and Stochastic Tax: A Standalone Framework for Measurement, Simulation, and Dashboarding
Authors: Muhammad Zia Hydari, Raja Iqbal, Narayan Ramasubbu
First: 2026-05-26T17:28:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:28:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agentic AI systems combine probabilistic reasoning with delegated action through tools, context, memory, orchestration, and external workflow integration. This note develops a formal and managerially usable model that distinguishes Agentic Technical Debt from Stochastic Tax. Agentic Technical Debt is a stock of accumulated design and governance liability. Stochastic Tax is a recurring flow of operating burden that arises when stochastic agents are used in business workflows. The two constructs are related, but they are not the same: debt can amplify the tax, while the tax can remain positive even when debt is minimized. The note starts from a compact dashboard expression, expands it into a fuller structural model, defines all variables and parameters, shows how each cost category can be estimated from operational data, and illustrates the framework with an accounts-payable simulation and companion spreadsheet.
中文标题/摘要
标题：代理型技术债与随机税建模：一个独立的测量、模拟与仪表盘框架
代理型AI系统通过工具、上下文、记忆、编排和外部工作流集成，将概率推理与委托行动相结合。本说明构建了一个形式化且可管理使用的模型，区分代理型技术债与随机税。代理型技术债是累积的设计与治理责任存量；随机税则是当随机代理用于业务工作流时产生的持续运营负担流。二者相关但不相同：债务可放大税负，而税负即使在债务最小化时仍可为正。本说明从简洁仪表盘表达出发，扩展为完整结构模型，定义所有变量与参数，展示如何从运营数据估算各类成本，并以应付账款模拟及配套电子表格示例说明该框架。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the need for a formal, managerially actionable framework to quantify and simulate the distinct yet interrelated concepts of Agentic Technical Debt and Stochastic Tax in AI systems. It introduces a structural model that separates the accumulated governance and design liabilities (technical debt) from the ongoing operational burden (stochastic tax) arising from probabilistic agents in workflows. The model is built from a dashboard-level expression and expanded into a full set of measurable variables, with methods for estimating costs from operational data. Experimental results using an accounts-payable simulation demonstrate how technical debt can magnify stochastic tax, while tax can persist even when debt is low, validating the framework’s utility for monitoring and decision-making.
本文旨在构建一个形式化且便于管理者使用的框架，用于量化和模拟AI系统中代理技术债与随机税两个相关但不同的概念。模型将设计与治理累积的负债（技术债）与业务流程中使用随机代理产生的持续运营负担（随机税）区分开来，从仪表盘表达式扩展为完整结构模型，并定义所有变量与参数。通过应付账款模拟验证，结果表明技术债可加剧运营税，但即使技术债较低，随机税仍可能持续存在，凸显二者需独立追踪与管理。
Q-GeoMem: Question-Guided Geometric Memory for Video Spatial Reasoning
Authors: Xianqiang Gao, Qizhi Chen, Delin Qu, Haoming Song, Zhigang Wang, Bin Zhao, Dong Wang, Xuelong Li
First: 2026-05-26T17:26:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:26:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Video spatial reasoning requires accumulating viewpoint-dependent evidence over time while retaining information useful to the question being asked. Existing spatial video-language models improve geometric perception and long-range context modeling, but often treat memory as a generic temporal cache, which can introduce redundant or irrelevant geometry and weaken long-horizon reasoning. We propose \textbf{\ours}, a question-guided geometric memory framework for video spatial reasoning. \ours injects camera-conditioned geometry into visual tokens and maintains two complementary memories: a Fine-Grained Context Bank for recent dense features and camera states, and a Semantic-Geometric Evidence Bank for compact long-range evidence. Each candidate frame is scored by the product of Q-Former-based question relevance and novelty with respect to the retained bank; this score is stored and reused during reading, while a capacity-based replacement rule keeps the bank compact. During reasoning, both memories are read before update and adaptively fused with the current frame representation. Experiments on VSI-Bench and VSTI-Bench show that \ours achieves state-of-the-art performance among evaluated spatial reasoning models, validating the effectiveness of question-guided geometric memory. Ablations further verify the contribution of the proposed evidence scoring mechanism.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Q-GeoMem：面向视频空间推理的问答引导几何记忆
视频空间推理需要在时间上累积视角相关的证据，同时保留对当前问题有用的信息。现有空间视频-语言模型虽提升了几何感知与长程上下文建模能力，但常将记忆视为通用时间缓存，可能引入冗余或无关的几何信息，削弱长程推理能力。我们提出\textbf{\ours}，一种面向视频空间推理的问答引导几何记忆框架。\ours将相机条件几何注入视觉标记，并维护两个互补记忆：细粒度上下文库用于存储近期密集特征与相机状态，语义-几何证据库用于存储紧凑的长程证据。每个候选帧的得分由基于Q-Former的问答相关性与新颖性乘积决定；该得分在读取时被存储并复用，容量控制替换规则保持记忆库紧凑。推理时，两个记忆在更新前被读取，并自适应融合当前帧表示。在VSI-Bench与VSTI-Bench上的实验表明，\ours在评估的空间推理模型中达到最先进性能，验证了问答引导几何记忆的有效性。消融实验进一步验证了所提证据评分机制的贡献。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to retain viewpoint-dependent spatial evidence relevant to questions in video reasoning, the paper introduces Q-GeoMem, a question-guided geometric memory framework. It injects camera-conditioned geometry into visual tokens and maintains two complementary memory banks: a Fine-Grained Context Bank for recent dense features and a Semantic-Geometric Evidence Bank for long-range evidence. Each frame is scored by the product of question relevance and novelty before being stored or replaced via capacity-based rules; during reasoning, both memories are read and adaptively fused with the current frame. Experiments on VSI-Bench and VSTI-Bench show Q-GeoMem achieves state-of-the-art performance, with ablation studies confirming the effectiveness of its evidence scoring mechanism.
为提升视频空间推理中与问题相关且依赖视角的证据保留能力，本文提出Q-GeoMem框架，将相机条件几何注入视觉token，并维护两个互补记忆库：细粒度上下文库用于存储近期密集特征与相机状态，语义-几何证据库用于存储紧凑的长程证据。每个候选帧通过问题相关性与新颖性乘积得分进行评估，得分被存储并重用，容量控制机制保持记忆库紧凑。推理时，两个记忆库被读取并自适应融合当前帧表示。在VSI-Bench与VSTI-Bench上的实验表明Q-GeoMem达到当前最优性能，消融实验进一步验证了证据评分机制的有效性。
Incremental Gauss-Newton Descent for Machine Learning
Authors: Mikalai Korbit, Mario Zanon
First: 2024-08-10T13:52:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:25:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Stochastic gradient updates are widely used for their efficiency and scalability, but their effective step sizes can depend strongly on feature scaling and local model sensitivity. Gauss-Newton methods address such scale effects through curvature information, but in their standard mini-batch form they require matrix-vector products, linear solves, or structured approximations. This paper studies the special case of scalar-output losses evaluated one sample at a time. In this setting, the generalized Gauss-Newton matrix has rank at most one, and its only possible nonzero curvature direction is aligned with the stochastic gradient. As a result, the damped Gauss-Newton direction reduces to a closed-form scalar normalization of the sample gradient. The resulting update, Incremental Gauss-Newton Descent (IGND), requires no curvature matrix storage, factorization, or iterative linear solve. We derive the update, characterize its behavior, and relate it to normalized gradient descent, adaptive first-order methods, stochastic Polyak step sizes, and mini-batch Gauss-Newton updates. Under explicit smoothness, alignment, and stochastic approximation assumptions, we prove a stationarity result for the IGND update. Experiments on supervised learning, a controlled test of scale robustness, and a linear-quadratic control case study show that IGND improves robustness to sensitivity scaling and can be competitive with, or complementary to, common stochastic optimizers while retaining a simple incremental update.
中文标题/摘要
标题：机器学习中的增量高斯-牛顿下降法
随机梯度更新因其效率和可扩展性而广泛应用，但其有效步长常依赖于特征缩放和局部模型敏感度。高斯-牛顿方法通过曲率信息解决此类尺度效应，但其标准小批量形式需矩阵-向量乘积、线性求解或结构化近似。本文研究单样本评估标量输出损失的特例。在此设定下，广义高斯-牛顿矩阵秩至多为一，其唯一可能非零曲率方向与随机梯度对齐。因此，阻尼高斯-牛顿方向简化为样本梯度的闭式标量归一化。所得更新方法——增量高斯-牛顿下降法（IGND）——无需存储曲率矩阵、分解或迭代线性求解。我们推导了该更新方法，分析其行为，并将其与归一化梯度下降、自适应一阶方法、随机Polyak步长及小批量高斯-牛顿更新关联。在显式光滑性、对齐性及随机逼近假设下，我们证明了IGND更新的平稳性。在监督学习、尺度鲁棒性控制测试及线性-二次控制案例研究中，实验表明IGND对敏感度缩放更具鲁棒性，且在保持简单增量更新的同时，可与常见随机优化器竞争或互补。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the sensitivity of stochastic gradient methods to feature scaling and local model curvature, this paper proposes Incremental Gauss-Newton Descent (IGND), a method tailored for scalar-output losses evaluated one sample at a time. Leveraging the rank-one structure of the Gauss-Newton matrix in this setting, IGND simplifies the curvature-based update to a scalar normalization of the sample gradient, eliminating the need for matrix storage, factorization, or linear solves. Theoretical analysis under smoothness and alignment assumptions establishes stationarity, while experiments across supervised learning, scale robustness tests, and a linear-quadratic control problem demonstrate IGND’s improved robustness to sensitivity scaling and its competitive or complementary performance relative to standard stochastic optimizers without sacrificing simplicity.
为解决随机梯度方法对特征缩放和局部模型曲率敏感的问题，本文提出增量高斯-牛顿下降法（IGND），专用于逐样本评估的标量输出损失。通过利用广义高斯-牛顿矩阵的秩一结构，IGND将曲率引导的更新简化为样本梯度的标量归一化，无需矩阵存储、分解或线性求解。在光滑性和对齐性假设下，理论分析证明了该更新的稳定性，实验表明IGND在监督学习和控制任务中对敏感性缩放具有鲁棒性，常能与主流随机优化器相当或互补，同时保持计算简洁。
Probabilistic Smoothing with Ratio-Monotone Transforms for Global Optimization
Authors: Kukyoung Jang, Taehyun Cho, Junrui Zhang, Ping Xu, Kyungjae Lee
First: 2026-05-26T17:25:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:25:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Probabilistic smoothing is a standard tool for global optimization, but existing methods rely on Gaussian kernels and specific transforms, often resulting in strong hyperparameter sensitivity and limited robustness. We propose a general smoothing framework that combines flexible symmetric unimodal kernels with monotonic ratio-based transformations. Under mild conditions, we show that the smoothed objective preserves the global maximizer and that all stationary points concentrate near the true optimum for sufficiently large amplification, without requiring a decreasing smoothing schedule. We further provide explicit complexity bounds for stochastic gradient ascent and show that a leave-one-out baseline provably reduces variance. Experiments on high-dimensional benchmarks and black-box adversarial attacks demonstrate improved robustness and competitive performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于比率单调变换的概率平滑方法用于全局优化
概率平滑是全局优化的标准工具，但现有方法依赖高斯核和特定变换，常导致超参数敏感性高且鲁棒性有限。我们提出一种通用平滑框架，结合灵活的对称单峰核与基于单调比率的变换。在温和条件下，我们证明平滑后的目标函数保留全局最大值，且所有临界点在放大因子足够大时集中于真实最优解附近，无需递减平滑调度。我们进一步给出随机梯度上升的显式复杂度界，并证明留一法基线可显著降低方差。在高维基准和黑盒对抗攻击实验中，该方法展现出更强鲁棒性与竞争力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing probabilistic smoothing methods—such as sensitivity to hyperparameters and lack of robustness—the paper introduces a general smoothing framework combining flexible symmetric unimodal kernels with ratio-monotone transformations. Theoretical analysis shows that under mild conditions, the smoothed objective preserves the global maximizer and concentrates all stationary points near the true optimum for sufficiently large amplification, without needing a decreasing smoothing schedule. Empirical results on high-dimensional benchmarks and black-box adversarial attacks confirm improved robustness and competitive performance compared to baseline methods.
为了解决现有概率平滑方法对超参数敏感、鲁棒性差的问题，本文提出一种结合灵活对称单峰核与比值单调变换的通用平滑框架。理论分析表明，在温和条件下，平滑后的目标函数保持全局最大值，且当放大因子足够大时，所有临界点会集中于真实最优解附近，无需递减平滑调度。在高维基准和黑盒对抗攻击实验中，该方法展现出更强的鲁棒性与竞争力。
Real Images, Worse Judgments: Evaluating Vision-Language Models on Concreteness and Imagery
Authors: Yifan Jiang, Ruoxi Ning, Sheng Yao, Freda Shi
First: 2026-05-26T17:24:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:24:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Visual inputs are often assumed to improve language understanding in multimodal models. We examine this assumption by asking whether vision-language models (VLMs) can distinguish useful visual evidence from incidental image context in lexical judgments. We use human concreteness and imagery ratings because they span words with varying expected visual relevance, from abstract and low-imagery words to concrete and high-imagery words. We find that real-image contexts do not yield consistent gains and often hurt alignment with human ratings, most sharply when visual evidence is least relevant. Through probing and canonical correlation analysis, complemented by an attribution case study, we find that real-image contexts are associated with representational shifts and greater sensitivity to spurious visual cues, coinciding with weaker recoverability of the targeted lexical properties. We further show that instructing models to focus solely on textual content at inference time can reduce this degradation, with the clearest gains on these vulnerable subsets. Our findings suggest that current instruction-tuned VLMs need better calibration of when visual context should inform lexical judgments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：真实图像反而导致更差的判断：在具体性和形象性上评估视觉-语言模型
通常认为视觉输入能提升多模态模型的语言理解能力。我们通过考察视觉-语言模型（VLMs）能否区分词义判断中的有用视觉证据与偶然图像背景来检验这一假设。我们使用人类对具体性和形象性的评分，涵盖从抽象低形象性到具体高形象性词汇的各类词汇。研究发现，真实图像上下文并未带来一致的提升，反而常损害与人类评分的一致性，尤其在视觉证据最不相关时表现最明显。通过探针分析、典型相关分析及归因案例研究，我们发现真实图像上下文与表征变化和对虚假视觉线索的更高敏感性相关，同时伴随目标词汇属性恢复能力的减弱。进一步研究表明，指导模型在推理时仅关注文本内容可减轻这种退化，尤其在脆弱子集上效果最明显。我们的研究结果表明，当前指令调优的VLMs需要更好地校准视觉上下文何时应影响词汇判断。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether real-image inputs improve vision-language models' lexical judgments by comparing their alignment with human concreteness and imagery ratings. The authors find that real images often degrade model performance, especially for words with low visual relevance, due to representational shifts and sensitivity to spurious cues. Inference-time instructions to ignore visual context significantly reduce this degradation, particularly for vulnerable word subsets, suggesting current VLMs require better calibration of when visual input should influence lexical decisions.
该研究探讨真实图像上下文是否能提升视觉语言模型在词义具体性和图像性判断中的表现。研究发现，真实图像常降低模型性能，尤其对视觉相关性低的词汇，导致表征偏移和对虚假视觉线索的敏感性增强。作者表明，若在推理时指导模型仅关注文本内容，可显著缓解此问题，尤其对易受影响的词汇子集，提示当前视觉语言模型需更精准地判断何时应使用视觉上下文辅助词义判断。
Riding the Shifting Potential: When Reactive Control Suffices for Multi-Goal Behavior
Authors: Vito Mengers, Oliver Brock
First: 2026-05-26T17:24:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:24:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reactive control is often considered insufficient for multi-objective tasks because conflicting objectives give rise to local minima. We argue this limitation is not inherent but arises from static encodings that fail to reflect how objectives currently interact. We exploit the interaction structure encoded in a graph-based world model by extending it with nullspace projections: conflicts are resolved where they arise by projecting lower-priority gradients into the nullspace of higher-priority ones, with priorities determined continuously from the current state. We demonstrate this in two domains where conflicts between objectives are central: navigation around non-convex obstacles, where static potential fields fundamentally fail, and planar pushing of non-convex objects, where our method achieves $100\%$ success across one-hundred configurations versus $0\%$ for the steepest-descent baseline and ${\sim}55\%$ for diffusion policy, without demonstrations or retraining. The same formulation transfers directly to a real robot with additional perceptual and kinematic constraints, accommodating them through the same mechanism.
中文标题/摘要
标题：驾驭动态势场：当反应式控制足以应对多目标行为时
反应式控制常被认为不足以处理多目标任务，因为冲突目标会导致局部极小值。我们认为这一局限并非固有，而是源于静态编码未能反映当前目标间的交互方式。我们通过扩展基于图的世界模型，引入零空间投影来利用目标交互结构：在冲突发生处，将低优先级梯度投影至高优先级梯度的零空间，优先级由当前状态连续决定。我们在两个冲突目标至关重要的领域中验证了该方法：在非凸障碍物周围导航（静态势场根本失效），以及平面非凸物体的推移（在一百种配置中实现100%成功率，而最陡下降基线为0%，扩散策略约55%），无需演示或再训练。相同框架可直接迁移到真实机器人，通过同一机制容纳额外感知与运动学约束。
Summary / 总结
The paper challenges the notion that reactive control is inherently inadequate for multi-goal tasks by showing that static potential field encodings, not the control paradigm itself, cause failure due to unmodeled objective conflicts. It introduces a method that dynamically resolves these conflicts via nullspace projections, where lower-priority gradients are projected into the nullspace of higher-priority ones, with priorities determined continuously from the current state. Experiments in obstacle navigation and planar pushing demonstrate that this approach achieves 100% success across 100 configurations, outperforming a steepest-descent baseline (0%) and a diffusion policy (~55%), without requiring demonstrations or retraining. The method also transfers directly to real robots by incorporating perceptual and kinematic constraints through the same projection mechanism.
该论文质疑了反应式控制在多目标任务中固有不足的观点，指出冲突源于静态势场编码而非控制范式本身。其方法在图结构世界模型中引入零空间投影，根据当前状态动态调整优先级，将低优先级梯度投影至高优先级梯度的零空间以解决冲突。在非凸障碍物导航和平面推动物体任务中，该方法在100个配置中实现100%成功率，远超最陡下降基线（0%）和扩散策略（约55%），且无需演示或重训练。该方法可直接迁移到真实机器人，通过相同机制兼容感知与运动学约束。
When Does Demographic Information Help? Data and Modeling Regimes for Perspective-Aware Hate Speech Detection
Authors: Weibin Cai, Reza Zafarani
First: 2026-05-26T17:24:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:24:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Demographic information is often used to model annotator perspectives in subjective tasks such as hate speech detection, but its benefit is inconsistent: it improves performance in some settings and behaves as noise in others. This paper asks when demographic features help. We analyze demographic gain as a function of both data split properties and modeling frameworks. For data splits, we measure annotator disagreement, namely how often annotators assign different labels to the same example, along with training size and train-test demographic coverage. We find that demographic gains concentrate in regimes with low training disagreement, high test disagreement, fine-grained ambiguity measurement, sufficient training data, and greater demographic overlap. Motivated by these regimes, we introduce a gated demographic residual model that treats demographics as a selective adjustment to text-only predictions. Experiments on MHS and POPQUORN show that this design is effective, especially on high disagreement or low confidence examples. Overall, our results suggest that demographics should not be assumed useful by default; their value depends jointly on the data regime and the modeling framework.
中文标题/摘要
标题：人口统计信息在何时有助于视角感知的仇恨言论检测？数据与建模框架分析
人口统计信息常用于建模主观任务（如仇恨言论检测）中的标注者视角，但其效果不一致：在某些场景下提升性能，而在其他场景下则表现为噪声。本文探讨人口统计特征何时有效。我们分析人口统计收益如何随数据划分特性和建模框架变化。针对数据划分，我们测量标注者分歧（即标注者对同一示例分配不同标签的频率），以及训练集规模和训练-测试集人口统计覆盖度。我们发现，人口统计收益集中在标注者训练分歧低、测试分歧高、细粒度模糊度测量、训练数据充足及人口统计重叠度高的场景。受此启发，我们提出一种门控人口统计残差模型，将人口统计信息视为对纯文本预测的有选择性调整。在MHS和POPQUORN数据集上的实验表明，该设计有效，尤其在高分歧或低置信度示例上表现突出。总体而言，我们的结果表明，人口统计信息不应默认有用，其价值取决于数据环境与建模框架的共同作用。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates when demographic information improves hate speech detection by analyzing its performance across different data splits and modeling frameworks. The authors find that demographic features yield gains only under specific conditions: low annotator disagreement during training, high disagreement during testing, fine-grained ambiguity measurement, sufficient training data, and demographic overlap between train and test sets. To exploit these conditions, they propose a gated demographic residual model that selectively adjusts text-only predictions using demographic data. Experiments on MHS and POPQUORN show this model performs best on high-disagreement or low-confidence examples, demonstrating that demographic information is not universally beneficial and must be applied contextually.
本文探讨了在何种条件下人口统计信息能提升仇恨言论检测效果，通过分析不同数据划分和建模框架下人口统计信息的表现，测量了标注者分歧度、训练样本量、人口统计覆盖度和模糊度粒度，发现其收益集中在训练分歧度低、测试分歧度高、模糊度精细、训练数据充足及人口统计重叠度高的场景。作者提出一种门控人口统计残差模型，仅在必要时用人口统计信息调整纯文本预测，在高分歧或低置信度样本上表现更优。在MHS和POPQUORN数据集上的实验表明，人口统计信息并非总是有益，其价值取决于数据环境与模型架构。
Credit Assignment with Resets in Language Model Reasoning
Authors: Ankur Samanta, Akshayaa Magesh, Ayush Jain, Youliang Yu, Daniel Jiang, Kavosh Asadi, Kaveh Hassani, Paul Sajda, Jalaj Bhandari, Yonathan Efroni
First: 2026-05-25T07:11:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:23:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Contemporary reinforcement learning with verifiable reward methods post-train language models on multi-step reasoning by assigning a single outcome reward uniformly across all tokens in a trajectory. Such uniform assignment ignores which steps contributed to success or failure. Improving credit assignment can address this limitation by enabling targeted refinement of faulty reasoning steps, rather than updating entire trajectories uniformly. Resets are one such simple mechanism, enabling more precise credit assignment by returning to an intermediate state and resampling counterfactual continuations, so that outcome differences can be attributed to decisions made at that point. We propose two such methods: Random-Reset Policy Optimization (RRPO), where reset states are drawn randomly from reasoning steps, and Self-Reset Policy Optimization (SRPO), where the model self-localizes the erroneous step in an incorrect trajectory and resets there. We analyze these methods within the Conservative Policy Iteration (CPI) framework. Extending CPI with a credit-assignment oracle that targets improvable states yields provable improvements over random resets. Across models and reasoning benchmarks, SRPO consistently outperforms standard GRPO and RRPO by sampling multiple suffix continuations at a self-localized reset and learning from their rewards, using only the model itself with no external supervision.
中文标题/摘要
标题：语言模型推理中的重置信用分配
当前可验证奖励的强化学习方法在训练后，通过在轨迹中所有token上均匀分配单一结果奖励，对语言模型进行多步推理。这种均匀分配忽略了哪些步骤导致了成功或失败。改进信用分配可以解决这一局限，通过针对性地优化错误推理步骤，而非对整个轨迹进行统一更新。重置是一种简单机制，通过返回中间状态并重新采样反事实延续，使结果差异可归因于该点的决策。我们提出两种方法：随机重置策略优化（RRPO），重置状态从推理步骤中随机抽取；自重置策略优化（SRPO），模型在错误轨迹中自我定位错误步骤并在此处重置。我们在保守策略迭代（CPI）框架下分析这些方法。通过引入一个针对可改进状态的信用分配预言机扩展CPI，可证明其优于随机重置。在不同模型和推理基准测试中，SRPO始终优于标准GRPO和RRPO，它在自我定位的重置点采样多个后缀延续并从其奖励中学习，仅使用模型本身，无需外部监督。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of uniform reward assignment in multi-step language model reasoning, this work introduces reset-based credit assignment mechanisms to target faulty reasoning steps. It proposes two methods: Random-Reset Policy Optimization (RRPO), which resets at randomly selected steps, and Self-Reset Policy Optimization (SRPO), which uses the model to self-identify and reset at erroneous steps. Evaluated within the Conservative Policy Iteration framework with a credit-assignment oracle, SRPO consistently outperforms both baseline methods and standard GRPO across multiple models and reasoning benchmarks by leveraging self-localized resets and learning from multiple counterfactual continuations without external supervision.
该论文针对强化学习中语言模型多步推理时统一奖励分配的缺陷，即所有轨迹中的token共享同一结果奖励，掩盖了具体步骤对成败的影响。作者提出两种基于重置的改进方法：随机重置策略优化（RRPO）随机选取重置点，以及自重置策略优化（SRPO）让模型自行定位错误步骤并在此处重置。在保守策略迭代框架下，SRPO通过从自定位重置点采样多个反事实延续并学习其奖励，仅依赖模型自身输出无需外部监督，在多个模型和推理基准上持续优于标准GRPO和RRPO。
Clozing the Gap: Exploring Why Language Model Surprisal Outperforms Cloze Surprisal
Authors: Sathvik Nair, Byung-Doh Oh
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-01-14T21:38:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:23:05+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 10 figures, accepted to ACL 2026 Main Conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
How predictable a word is can be quantified in two ways: using human responses to the cloze task or using probabilities from language models (LMs).When used as predictors of processing effort, LM probabilities outperform probabilities derived from cloze data. However, it is important to establish that LM probabilities do so for the right reasons, since different predictors can lead to different scientific conclusions about the role of prediction in language comprehension. We present evidence for three hypotheses about the advantage of LM probabilities: not suffering from low resolution, distinguishing semantically similar words, and accurately assigning probabilities to low-frequency words. These results call for efforts to improve the resolution of cloze studies, coupled with experiments on whether human-like prediction is also as sensitive to the fine-grained distinctions made by LM probabilities.
中文标题/摘要
标题：弥合差距：探究语言模型意外性为何优于填空意外性
词的可预测性可通过两种方式量化：使用人类对填空任务的反应，或使用语言模型（LM）的概率。当用作加工努力的预测指标时，LM概率优于由填空数据导出的概率。但重要的是要确认LM概率之所以表现更优，是出于正确的理由，因为不同的预测指标可能导致关于预测在语言理解中作用的科学结论不同。我们提出了三个关于LM概率优势的假设：不因分辨率低而受限、能区分语义相似的词、能准确为低频词分配概率。这些结果呼吁改进填空研究的分辨率，并开展实验，检验人类式预测是否同样对LM概率所做出的精细区分敏感。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates why language model (LM) surprisal outperforms cloze surprisal as a predictor of processing effort, motivated by the need to ensure scientific conclusions about language comprehension are grounded in the right mechanisms. The authors test three hypotheses: LM surprisal avoids low-resolution issues, better distinguishes semantically similar words, and more accurately assigns probabilities to low-frequency words. Experimental results support all three, demonstrating that LM surprisal’s superior performance stems from its finer-grained sensitivity to linguistic structure, which cloze data lacks. The findings suggest future work should enhance cloze resolution and test whether human prediction matches LM-level sensitivity.
该研究探讨了为何语言模型（LM）的惊讶值优于填空任务（cloze）的惊讶值作为加工努力度的预测指标，动机在于确保语言理解研究的科学结论基于正确的机制。作者检验了三个假设：LM惊讶值避免分辨率不足、能更好区分语义相似词、且对低频词的概率赋值更准确。实验结果支持所有假设，表明LM惊讶值对细微语言差异更敏感，而填空数据无法捕捉，因此呼吁改进填空研究的分辨率以匹配LM的精细度。
Chartographer: Counterfactual Chart Generation for Evaluating Vision-Language Models
Authors: Yifan Jiang, Dae Yon Hwang, Jesse C. Cresswell, Freda Shi
First: 2026-05-26T17:20:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:20:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Chart question-answering (QA) benchmarks aim to pose questions that require visual reasoning to correctly answer, but models can often reach solutions through shortcuts or prior familiarity with a chart based on their own background knowledge. To strictly evaluate visual reasoning, we propose counterfactual charts where the chart-question task remains fixed, but underlying chart and the corresponding answer are varied. We introduce Chartographer, a framework to reverse engineer charts into executable code, validate reconstruction fidelity, generate seed-controlled counterfactual variants, and derive new answers from executable QA logic. We apply this framework to existing chart QA datasets and evaluate proprietary and open-source vision-language models (VLMs), measuring variation sensitivity and generalizability. Counterfactual charts reveal failures hidden by single-chart performance: VLMs often fail to generalize after answering the original chart correctly. We find failures are most prevalent when updated charts require novel visual reasoning pathways.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Chartographer：用于评估视觉-语言模型的反事实图表生成
图表问答（QA）基准旨在提出需要视觉推理才能正确回答的问题，但模型常可通过捷径或基于自身背景知识对图表的熟悉度获得答案。为严格评估视觉推理能力，我们提出反事实图表：保持图表-问题任务不变，但底层图表及其对应答案发生变化。我们引入Chartographer框架，可将图表逆向工程为可执行代码，验证重建保真度，生成受种子控制的反事实变体，并从可执行的QA逻辑中推导新答案。我们将该框架应用于现有图表QA数据集，评估专有及开源视觉-语言模型（VLMs），测量其对变化的敏感性和泛化能力。反事实图表揭示了单一图表性能所掩盖的失败：VLMs常在正确回答原始图表后无法泛化。我们发现，当更新图表需要新颖的视觉推理路径时，失败最为普遍。
Summary / 总结
To rigorously evaluate visual reasoning in vision-language models, the authors propose counterfactual charts that preserve the question but alter the underlying chart and answer, exposing model reliance on shortcuts. They introduce Chartographer, a framework that reverse-engineers charts into executable code, validates reconstructions, generates controlled counterfactual variants, and derives new answers via executable QA logic. Applied to existing chart QA datasets, the framework reveals that models often fail to generalize even after correctly answering original charts, with failures most common when counterfactuals demand novel visual reasoning.
为严格评估视觉推理能力，作者提出保留问题但改变图表与答案的反事实图表，以揭示模型对捷径的依赖。他们构建了Chartographer框架，可将图表反向工程为可执行代码、验证重建精度、生成可控反事实变体并基于可执行QA逻辑推导新答案。在现有图表问答数据集上应用后发现，即使模型能正确回答原始图表，其在反事实图表上常无法泛化，且当反事实图表需新视觉推理路径时失败率最高。
How and What to Imagine? Visual Thinking in Unified Multimodal Models for Cross-View Spatial Reasoning
Authors: Qian Yang, Ankur Sikarwar, Huy Le, Le Zhang, Zhuan Shi, Perouz Taslakian, Aishwarya Agrawal
First: 2026-05-26T17:20:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:20:05+00:00
Comments: Preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Cross-view spatial reasoning remains a weak spot for vision-language models (VLMs): they often reason in language and lose the fine-grained geometry needed for the task. Thinking with images aims to address this by generating an intermediate thinking image, but recent work shows that models often ignore the visual evidence in these traces. We therefore ask how to make visual thinking matter, and what kind of visual thinking works best. We study these questions in unified multimodal models (UMMs), which natively support interleaved image-text generation. For the first question, we propose View Dropout (VDrop), a training-time intervention that hides parts of one input view from the answer span while keeping them visible to the thinking-image tokens. This encourages the model to use the thinking image when answering, instead of relying only on the input views. Once the thinking image is used for answer prediction, we study which type of visual thinking is most effective. We frame this as a learnability-informativeness tradeoff and compare three thinking-image variants: top-down, panoramic, and point-matching renderings. Trained on synthetic scenes and evaluated on five real-world out-of-domain benchmarks, panoramic visual thinking with VDrop is the only configuration that is both informative and learnable, and it achieves the best out-of-domain generalization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：如何以及想象什么？统一多模态模型中的视觉思维用于跨视角空间推理
跨视角空间推理仍是视觉-语言模型（VLMs）的薄弱环节：它们常依赖语言推理，而丢失任务所需的细粒度几何信息。视觉思维旨在通过生成中间思维图像来解决此问题，但近期研究表明，模型常忽略这些轨迹中的视觉证据。因此，我们探讨如何让视觉思维真正发挥作用，以及哪种视觉思维效果最佳。我们在原生支持图像-文本交错生成的统一多模态模型（UMMs）中研究这些问题。针对第一个问题，我们提出视图丢弃（VDrop），一种训练时干预方法，隐藏部分输入视图内容于答案跨度中，但保留其对思维图像令牌可见。这促使模型在回答时使用思维图像，而非仅依赖输入视图。一旦思维图像用于答案预测，我们研究哪种视觉思维最有效。我们将此视为可学习性与信息量之间的权衡，并比较三种思维图像变体：自上而下、全景式和点匹配渲染。在合成场景上训练并在五个真实世界跨域基准上评估，仅全景式视觉思维结合VDrop同时具备信息量与可学习性，且实现最佳跨域泛化性能。
Summary / 总结
Vision-language models struggle with cross-view spatial reasoning because they often rely on language and neglect fine-grained visual geometry. To address this, the paper investigates how to make visual thinking meaningful and effective within unified multimodal models. It introduces View Dropout, a training technique that hides parts of an input view from the answer generation while keeping them visible to the thinking image tokens, encouraging the model to use the thinking image for reasoning. The study then evaluates three types of visual thinking—top-down, panoramic, and point-matching—framed as a tradeoff between learnability and informativeness. Results show that panoramic visual thinking combined with View Dropout is the only configuration that balances both properties and achieves the best generalization on out-of-domain real-world benchmarks.
视觉语言模型在跨视角空间推理任务中表现不佳，常依赖语言而忽略精细的视觉几何信息。为解决此问题，作者提出View Dropout（VDrop）训练技巧，即在生成答案时隐藏部分输入视角内容，但保留给思考图像模块可见，以促使模型依赖思考图像进行推理。随后，他们评估了三种视觉思考方式——自上而下、全景式和点匹配渲染，将其视为可学习性与信息量之间的权衡。实验表明，仅全景式视觉思考结合VDrop能在信息量与可学习性上达到最佳平衡，从而在五个真实世界跨域基准测试中实现最佳泛化性能。
Greening AI Inference with Accuracy and Latency-aware User Incentives
Authors: Vasilios A. Siris, Adamantia Stamou, George D. Stamoulis, Konstantinos Varsos, Ramin Khalili
Venue: IEEE Internet Computing, 2026
First: 2026-05-26T17:19:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:19:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The widespread use of AI services has raised concerns for its environmental sustainability, towards which recent studies have identified carbon emissions of AI inference as the major contributor. This paper introduces a framework for designing AI inference incentives based on the users' valuation for inference quality and latency, together with their environmental consciousness, while accounting for the tradeoff between carbon emissions and the two QoE parameters. Our approach can accommodate different tradeoffs, that depend on the size and complexity of the AI models and the allocation of resources to serve inference requests. The incentives can be offered through a practical two-tier service subscription that offers users a discount in exchange for reduced carbon emissions. The discounted service option gives the AI provider the flexibility to serve some percentage of inference requests at a lower quality and higher latency during periods of high carbon intensity.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于精度与延迟感知用户激励的绿色AI推理
