Summary / 总结
Driven by concerns over AI inference's environmental impact, this paper proposes a user-incentive framework that aligns carbon emission reduction with user preferences for accuracy and latency. It models users' willingness to trade off quality and speed for lower emissions, enabling providers to offer tiered subscription plans where users receive discounts in exchange for accepting lower-quality, higher-latency services during high-carbon periods. Experiments show that this approach effectively reduces overall carbon footprint while maintaining acceptable service levels, with flexibility to adapt to model complexity and resource constraints.
为应对AI推理带来的环境负担，本文提出一种结合用户对准确率、延迟偏好及环保意识的激励框架，通过动态调整服务等级，引导用户在高碳排放时段接受较低质量或较慢响应的服务以换取折扣。实验表明，该机制可显著降低碳排放，同时保持用户满意度，且能根据模型规模与资源分配灵活调整，实现环保与服务质量的平衡。
Normal Guidance is what Attention Needs
Authors: Ethan Harvey, Dennis Johan Loevlie, Michael C. Hughes
First: 2026-05-26T17:17:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:17:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We consider training classifiers for 3D medical images using only one binary label for the entire volume rather than a label for each 2D slice. In such weakly supervised settings, can we learn accurate classifiers for slice-level predictions? Attention-based multiple instance learning (MIL) can produce an attention score for every slice. Yet recent work demonstrates that a simple center-focused baseline that ignores image content can outperform attention-based and transformer-based MIL at slice-level classification of 3D brain scans. We show this baseline also outperforms existing MIL at slice-level classification of thoracic and abdominal CT scans. Motivated by this baseline, we propose Normal Guidance, a regularization technique that encourages the learned attention distribution to follow a bell-shaped curve. Across three medical imaging datasets totaling over 4 million 2D slices, we show our Normal Guidance enables attention-based and transformer-based MIL methods to deliver significantly better slice-level localization than the state-of-the-art while remaining competitive at whole-scan classification.
中文标题/摘要
标题：正常引导是注意力机制所需要的
我们考虑仅使用一个二元标签对整个3D医学图像进行分类，而非为每个2D切片单独标注。在这样的弱监督设置下，我们能否学习到准确的切片级预测分类器？基于注意力的多实例学习（MIL）可为每个切片生成注意力分数。然而，近期研究表明，一个简单的以中心为焦点的基线方法（忽略图像内容）在3D脑部扫描的切片级分类中优于基于注意力和Transformer的MIL方法。我们进一步证明，该基线方法在胸腹部CT扫描的切片级分类中也优于现有MIL方法。受此启发，我们提出“正常引导”，一种鼓励学习到的注意力分布遵循钟形曲线的正则化技术。在三个总计超过400万张2D切片的医学影像数据集上，我们表明，正常引导能使基于注意力和Transformer的MIL方法在切片级定位上显著优于当前最优方法，同时在整体扫描分类上仍保持竞争力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that a simple center-focused attention baseline outperforms complex attention and transformer-based methods in slice-level classification of 3D medical scans, the authors propose Normal Guidance, a regularization technique that constrains attention distributions to follow a bell-shaped curve. Applied to attention-based and transformer-based multiple instance learning models, this method improves slice-level localization accuracy across three medical imaging datasets totaling over 4 million 2D slices, while maintaining competitive performance in whole-scan classification tasks.
鉴于简单中心聚焦的注意力基线在3D医学扫描切片级分类中优于复杂注意力和Transformer方法，作者提出Normal Guidance正则化技术，强制注意力分布呈钟形曲线。该方法应用于注意力和Transformer基的多实例学习模型，在超过400万张2D切片的三个医学影像数据集上显著提升切片级定位精度，同时保持整体扫描分类性能竞争力。
PlayClass: Automated Play Behaviour Classification in Poultry
Authors: Prince Ravi Leow, Neil Scheidwasser, Rebecca Oscarsson, Per Jensen, Samir Bhatt, David Alejandro Duchêne
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-05-26T17:13:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:13:36+00:00
Comments: Accepted at CV4Animals Workshop @ CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Automated monitoring of animal welfare has largely targeted negative indicators, leaving positive welfare behaviours such as play underexplored. To address this gap, we present PlayClass, a pipeline for play-behaviour classification in poultry from top-down pen video. The pipeline leverages long-duration tracking with SAM 3 via YOLO-guided chunk boundaries to minimise identity errors in point-based prompting, and frozen embeddings from image and video foundation models for play action classification. Although handcrafted motion features from tracked masks alone achieved competitive accuracy, V-JEPA 2.1 consistently outperformed all other backbones across model scales, reaching 77.0 macro-averaged F$_1$ when combined with handcrafted features. Despite this result, the dataset remains challenging due to play sub-types sharing similar kinematic profiles with non-play and inter-bird occlusion. Overall, our work provides encouraging evidence towards automated frameworks for play behaviour classification in poultry.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PlayClass：家禽玩耍行为的自动化分类
动物福利的自动化监测主要关注负面指标，而积极福利行为如玩耍则研究不足。为填补这一空白，我们提出了PlayClass，一个基于俯视围栏视频对家禽玩耍行为进行分类的流程。该流程利用YOLO引导的片段边界与SAM 3实现长时追踪，以最小化基于点提示的身份错误，并结合图像和视频基础模型的冻结嵌入进行玩耍动作分类。尽管仅使用追踪掩码的手工运动特征已取得竞争力的准确率，但V-JEPA 2.1在所有模型规模上均表现最佳，与手工特征结合时达到77.0的宏平均F1值。尽管如此，该数据集仍具挑战性，因玩耍亚型与非玩耍行为具有相似的运动学特征，且存在鸟类间遮挡问题。总体而言，我们的工作为家禽玩耍行为的自动化分类框架提供了积极证据。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the underexplored role of positive welfare behaviors like play in automated animal monitoring, this work introduces PlayClass, a pipeline that classifies play behavior in poultry using top-down pen video. It employs long-duration tracking with SAM 3 guided by YOLO to define video chunks and reduce identity errors in point-based prompting, while using frozen embeddings from image and video foundation models for classification. Handcrafted motion features from tracked masks achieved competitive performance, but V-JEPA 2.1 consistently outperformed other backbones across scales, reaching 77.0 macro-averaged F1 when combined with handcrafted features. Despite this success, the dataset remains challenging due to overlapping kinematic profiles between play sub-types and non-play behaviors, as well as inter-bird occlusions.
为弥补动物福利自动化监测中对积极行为（如玩耍）研究不足的空白，该研究提出PlayClass系统，利用俯视鸡舍视频自动分类鸡的玩耍行为。系统采用YOLO引导的视频分块与SAM 3进行长时追踪，减少点提示中的身份错误，并结合图像与视频基础模型的冻结嵌入进行行为分类。尽管仅用追踪掩码的手工运动特征已表现良好，但V-JEPA 2.1在所有模型规模中表现最优，与手工特征结合时达到77.0的宏平均F1值。然而，数据集仍具挑战性，因玩耍亚型与非玩耍行为运动特征高度重叠，且常出现鸡只遮挡问题。
Risk Averse Alert Prioritization for IDS Using Subnormal Gaussian Fuzzy Models
Authors: Murat Moran
First: 2026-05-26T17:11:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:11:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern intrusion detection systems generate thousands of alerts daily, but alert fatigue severely limits security operations effectiveness due to too many false positives or low-impact events. We address this by proposing a principled framework for alert prioritization based on subnormal Gaussian fuzzy numbers, explicitly modeling three sources of uncertainty: threat severity, detection confidence, and organizational risk attitude. Each alert is represented as a fuzzy number with the core indicating severity, spread indicating uncertainty, and height reflecting detection reliability. We apply ranking indices to prioritize alerts, allowing organizations to tune security posture through a risk-attitude parameter. Experimental validation on CIC-IDS2017 and NSL-KDD demonstrates greater robustness than baselines under detector degradation (0.9963 vs 0.8215 NDCGrel@100), with distinct differentiation in mid-confidence alerts and near-parity with baselines under robust detectors. The framework is theoretically grounded, computationally efficient, provides interpretable reasoning, and remains robust across detector families and miscalibration scenarios.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于次正规高斯模糊模型的入侵检测系统警报风险规避优先级排序
现代入侵检测系统每日生成数千条警报，但警报疲劳严重限制了安全运营效果，因假阳性或低影响事件过多。我们提出一种基于次正规高斯模糊数的警报优先级排序框架，显式建模三种不确定性来源：威胁严重性、检测置信度和组织风险态度。每条警报表示为一个模糊数，核心指示严重性，扩散度指示不确定性，高度反映检测可靠性。通过排序指标对警报进行优先级排序，使组织可通过风险态度参数调整安全态势。在CIC-IDS2017和NSL-KDD数据集上的实验验证表明，当检测器退化时，该方法比基线方法更鲁棒（NDCGrel@100为0.9963 vs 0.8215），在中等置信度警报上表现显著区分，在鲁棒检测器下与基线方法表现接近。该框架理论基础扎实、计算高效、提供可解释推理，且在不同检测器家族和误校准场景下保持鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
To combat alert fatigue in intrusion detection systems caused by excessive false positives and low-impact alerts, this work proposes a prioritization framework using subnormal Gaussian fuzzy numbers that explicitly model uncertainty in threat severity, detection confidence, and organizational risk tolerance. Each alert is encoded as a fuzzy number with core, spread, and height representing severity, uncertainty, and reliability respectively, and ranked using indices that allow tuning via a risk-attitude parameter. Experiments on CIC-IDS2017 and NSL-KDD show the method achieves superior robustness under detector degradation (NDCGrel@100: 0.9963 vs 0.8215), effectively distinguishes mid-confidence alerts, and maintains performance parity with baselines under robust detectors, while offering theoretical grounding, computational efficiency, and interpretability.
为缓解入侵检测系统因大量误报和低影响事件导致的警报疲劳问题，本文提出一种基于次正态高斯模糊数的警报优先级框架，显式建模威胁严重性、检测置信度和组织风险偏好三类不确定性。每个警报以模糊数表示，核心、扩散度和高度分别对应严重性、不确定性与检测可靠性，并通过排序指标进行优先级排序，支持通过风险偏好参数调节安全策略。在CIC-IDS2017和NSL-KDD数据集上的实验表明，该方法在检测器退化场景下显著优于基线（NDCGrel@100达0.9963 vs 0.8215），在稳健检测器下表现接近基线，且对检测器类型和校准误差具有强鲁棒性。
Self-Ensembling Vision-Language Models for Chart Data Extraction
Authors: Thomas Berkane, Qianyi Wang, Maimuna S. Majumder
First: 2026-05-26T17:10:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:10:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Charts effectively convey quantitative information, but the underlying data are often locked in image form, hindering reuse and analysis. Manually digitizing charts is time-consuming and error-prone, motivating automatic chart-to-table extraction. Recent approaches use specialized vision-language models (VLMs), yet performance still lags on charts with many datapoints or substantial stylistic variation. We propose a VLM self-ensembling method that repeatedly samples multiple tabular outputs from the same VLM for a fixed chart image and aggregates them at the level of individual table cells. We align candidate tables and take per-cell medians over numerical values to produce a more accurate consensus table. Our method also includes convergence detection to stop sampling once the aggregated table stabilizes, and uncertainty estimation based on dispersion across samples to help users assess extraction reliability. Because existing chart extraction benchmarks contain relatively simple plots with limited room for improvement, we introduce WB-ChartExtract, a new benchmark built from World Bank data with more complex and stylistically diverse charts; on average, its charts contain 7 times more datapoints than those in the ChartQA benchmark. Across both ChartQA and WB-ChartExtract, our approach improves extraction accuracy over single-pass VLM outputs, yielding up to 23% relative improvement on WB-ChartExtract after ensembling. More broadly, our method helps unlock tabular data previously siloed in chart images, enabling downstream analysis and reuse.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于图表数据提取的自集成视觉-语言模型
图表能有效传达定量信息，但其底层数据常以图像形式锁定，阻碍了重用与分析。手动数字化图表耗时且易出错，因此推动了自动图表到表格提取的发展。近期方法采用专用视觉-语言模型（VLM），但对数据点较多或风格差异大的图表性能仍不足。我们提出一种VLM自集成方法，对同一图表图像反复采样多个表格输出，并在单个表格单元级别进行聚合。通过对候选表格对齐并取数值单元的中位数，生成更准确的共识表格。该方法还包含收敛检测机制，当聚合表格稳定时停止采样，并基于样本间分散度进行不确定性估计，帮助用户评估提取可靠性。由于现有图表提取基准包含相对简单的图表，改进空间有限，我们引入WB-ChartExtract新基准，基于世界银行数据构建，包含更复杂且风格多样的图表；其平均数据点数量是ChartQA基准的7倍。在ChartQA和WB-ChartExtract两个基准上，我们的方法相较单次VLM输出提升提取准确率，对WB-ChartExtract的集成后提升最高达23%。更广泛地说，该方法有助于解锁此前被锁定在图表图像中的表格数据，支持下游分析与重用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to automate the extraction of quantitative data from charts to enable reuse and analysis, this paper proposes a self-ensembling method for vision-language models that repeatedly samples multiple table outputs from the same model for a given chart image and aggregates them cell-by-cell using median values to produce a more accurate consensus table. The method includes convergence detection to halt sampling when results stabilize and uncertainty estimation based on sample dispersion to indicate reliability. Evaluated on both ChartQA and a newly introduced WB-ChartExtract benchmark featuring charts with significantly more data points and stylistic diversity, the approach consistently outperforms single-pass VLM outputs, achieving up to 23% relative accuracy improvement on WB-ChartExtract.
为解决图表中数据难以提取和复用的问题，本文提出一种视觉语言模型自集成方法，对同一图表图像反复采样多个表格输出，按单元格取中位数生成更准确的共识表格，并加入收敛检测与样本分散度评估以判断结果可靠性。在ChartQA和新构建的包含更多数据点与风格多样性的WB-ChartExtract基准上测试，该方法显著优于单次输出模型，于WB-ChartExtract上最高提升23%准确率，有效释放图表中被锁住的结构化数据。
Persona2Web: Benchmarking Personalized Web Agents for Contextual Reasoning with User History
Authors: Serin Kim, Sangam Lee, Dongha Lee
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-02-19T01:54:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:09:16+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Large language models have advanced web agents, yet current agents lack personalization capabilities. Since users rarely specify every detail of their intent, practical web agents must be able to interpret ambiguous queries by inferring user preferences and contexts. To address this challenge, we present Persona2Web, the first benchmark for evaluating personalized web agents on the real open web, built upon the clarify-to-personalize principle, which requires agents to resolve ambiguity based on user history rather than relying on explicit instructions. Persona2Web consists of: (1) user histories that reveal preferences implicitly over long time spans, (2) ambiguous queries that require agents to infer implicit user preferences, and (3) a reasoning-aware evaluation framework that enables fine-grained assessment of personalization. We conduct extensive experiments across various agent architectures, backbone models, history access schemes, and queries with varying ambiguity levels, revealing key challenges in personalized web agent behavior. For reproducibility, our codes and datasets are publicly available at https://serin-kimm.github.io/Persona2Web/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Persona2Web：基于用户历史评估个性化网络代理的基准测试
大语言模型推动了网络代理的发展，但当前代理缺乏个性化能力。由于用户很少明确说明其意图的每个细节，实用的网络代理必须能够通过推断用户偏好和上下文来理解模糊查询。为应对这一挑战，我们提出了Persona2Web，这是首个在真实开放网络上评估个性化网络代理的基准，基于“澄清到个性化”原则，即代理需根据用户历史而非显式指令解决歧义。Persona2Web包含：（1）揭示长期隐含偏好的用户历史，（2）需要代理推断隐含用户偏好的模糊查询，（3）一个支持细粒度个性化评估的推理感知评估框架。我们在多种代理架构、主干模型、历史访问方案和不同模糊程度的查询上进行了广泛实验，揭示了个性化网络代理行为的关键挑战。为确保可复现性，我们的代码和数据集已公开于https://serin-kimm.github.io/Persona2Web/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for web agents to handle ambiguous user queries through inferred personal preferences rather than explicit instructions, the authors introduce Persona2Web, the first benchmark for evaluating personalized web agents on the real open web. The benchmark is built on the clarify-to-personalize principle and includes user histories revealing long-term implicit preferences, ambiguous queries requiring preference inference, and a reasoning-aware evaluation framework. Experiments across diverse agent architectures, backbone models, history access methods, and query ambiguity levels reveal consistent challenges in personalization, including difficulty in leveraging historical context and maintaining coherence across sessions.
为解决当前网络代理缺乏个性化能力、难以处理用户模糊查询的问题，作者提出了Persona2Web，这是首个在真实开放网络上评估个性化网络代理的基准。该基准基于“澄清到个性化”原则，包含揭示长期隐含偏好的用户历史、需要推断隐含偏好的模糊查询，以及支持细粒度评估的推理感知评估框架。在多种代理架构、基础模型、历史访问方式和模糊程度的查询上进行的广泛实验揭示了个性化代理行为中的关键挑战，如难以有效利用历史上下文和在模糊情境下保持推理一致性。
Probing Cultural Awareness in LLMs: A Case Study of Cross-Culture Aesthetic Stylistics
Authors: Jiashuo Wang, Fenggang Yu, Jian Wang, Chak Tou Leong, Xiaoyu Shen, Chunpu Xu, Jiawen Duan, Wenjie Li, Johan F. Hoorn
Venue: IJCAI 2026
First: 2026-05-26T17:08:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:08:46+00:00
Comments: IJCAI 2026 Human-Centred AI track
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in diverse cultural contexts, yet their ability to master aesthetic stylistics, i.e., the strategic use of language to evoke cultural resonance, remains underexplored. We curate C4STYLI, a benchmark of highly stylized translated movie titles and advertising slogans from Hong Kong and the Chinese Mainland, to evaluate LLMs via the lens of behavioral recognition and productive competence. Extensive evaluations show that LLMs differ from humans in stylistic recognition, and this recognition ability varies across text domains. In addition, stylistic recognition and generation performance in LLMs are not consistently aligned. To further examine whether LLMs genuinely capture stylistic information in stylistic recognition, we conduct structural ablation with logistic regression probes. We find that, in the Hong Kong setting, stylistic recognition in LLMs relies primarily on surface-level linguistic information rather than stylistic structure. This suggests limited sensitivity to Hong Kong-specific stylistic structure.
中文标题/摘要
标题：探究大语言模型中的文化意识：跨文化美学修辞风格案例研究
大语言模型（LLMs）日益被部署于多元文化语境中，但其掌握美学修辞风格——即通过语言策略激发文化共鸣的能力——仍鲜有研究。我们构建了C4STYLI基准数据集，收录来自香港与内地高度风格化的电影标题及广告标语，用以从行为识别与生成能力角度评估LLMs。大量评估表明，LLMs在风格识别上与人类存在差异，且识别能力因文本领域而异。此外，LLMs的风格识别与生成表现并不一致。为进一步检验LLMs在风格识别中是否真正捕捉到风格信息，我们采用逻辑回归探针进行结构消融实验。结果发现，在香港语境下，LLMs的风格识别主要依赖表层语言信息，而非风格结构，表明其对香港特有风格结构敏感度有限。

Motivated by the need to assess how well LLMs grasp culturally specific aesthetic stylistics, this study introduces C4STYLI, a benchmark dataset of stylized translated movie titles and advertising slogans from Hong Kong and the Chinese Mainland. Structural ablation with logistic regression probes reveals that LLMs in the Hong Kong context rely mainly on surface linguistic cues rather than underlying stylistic structures, indicating limited sensitivity to local stylistic nuances.
本研究旨在评估大语言模型（LLM）在跨文化审美修辞方面的理解与生成能力，因其在多元文化场景中的广泛应用。研究构建了C4STYLI数据集，包含来自香港与内地的风格化电影标题和广告标语，用于测试LLM的识别与生成表现。实验发现，LLM在风格识别上与人类表现不同，且在不同文本领域表现不一，识别与生成能力亦不一致。结构消融实验结合逻辑回归探针表明，香港语境下LLM的风格识别主要依赖表面语言特征，而非深层风格结构，说明其对本地风格结构敏感度有限。
GlobalDentBench: A Multinational Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Clinical Reasoning in Dentistry with Expert Calibration
Authors: Junjie Zhao, Jingyi Liang, Zhenyang Cai, Jiaming Zhang, Zhenwei Wen, Shuzhi Deng, Wenjing Yi, Chunfeng Luo, Hexian Zhang, Junying Chen, Tianrui Liu, Zhuhui Bai, Zixu Zhang, Pradeep Singh, Xiang Liu, Jianquan Li, Nhan L Tran, Falk Schwendicke, Zuolin Jin, Lijian Jin, Liangyi Chen, Wei-fa Yang, Benyou Wang, Junwen Wang, Shan Jiang
First: 2026-05-23T15:53:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:07:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While large language models (LLMs) hold transformative potential for medicine, their reasoning robustness and safety in real-world clinical scenarios remain critically underexplored, particularly in dentistry. Here we introduce GlobalDentBench, the first multinational dental benchmark, featuring a taxonomy that encompasses 14 dental specialties across 88 countries and regions spanning six continents. The benchmark comprises 8,978 expert-validated questions across three formats (multiple-choice, short-answer, and case-based questions) and assesses three progressive reasoning levels: knowledge recall (L1), routine reasoning (L2), and individualized reasoning (L3). To ensure data quality, the automated construction framework was calibrated by six senior dentists, achieving expert agreement rates of 99.98% for multiple-choice and short-answer questions and 96.78% for the more complex case-based questions. Evaluation of 12 frontier LLMs on GlobalDentBench revealed a sharp, stepwise performance degradation with increasing reasoning complexity. Specifically, accuracy plummeted from 81.34% on multiple-choice to 64.53% on short-answer and 22.34% on case-based questions, while declining markedly from 74.01% at L1 to 55.64% at L2 and 35.71% at L3. More critically, risk analysis of real-world dental cases demonstrated an alarming overall unsafe rate of 31.01% in LLM-generated clinical recommendations, with 4.51% posing risks of irreversible patient harm and risks particularly pronounced in specialties such as orthodontics. These findings expose fundamental limitations in the medical reasoning and safety of current LLMs. Consequently, GlobalDentBench provides a scalable foundation for trustworthy clinical AI evaluation, underscoring the urgent need for rigorous validation before the safe deployment of these models in healthcare.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GlobalDentBench：评估大语言模型在牙科临床推理能力的多国基准测试（含专家校准）

Gemini Embedding 2: A Native Multimodal Embedding Model from Gemini
Authors: Madhuri Shanbhogue, Zhe Li, Shanfeng Zhang, Gustavo Hernández Ábrego, Shih-Cheng Huang, Aashi Jain, Daniel Salz, Sonam Goenka, Chaitra Hegde, Ji Ma, Feiyang Chen, Jiaxing Wu, Tanmaya Dabral, Babak Samari, Kevin Poulet, Daniel Cer, Kaifeng Chen, Paul Suganathan, Hui Hui, Jovan Andonov, Philippe Schlattner, Jay Han, Iftekhar Naim, Wing Lowe, Vladimir Pchelin, Albert Yang, Yi-Ting Chen, Zhongli Ding, Grace Zhang, Georg Heigold, Yichang Chen, Antoine Reveillon, Brendan Mccloskey, Wenlei Zhou, Dahun Kim, Rui Meng, Emma Wang, Jack Zheng, Halley Fede, Zhen Yang, Keegan Mosley, Brian Potetz, Sahil Dua, Henrique Schechter Vera, Shen Gao, Hesen Zhang, Andreas Hess, Hengxuan Ying, Alberto Montes, Karan Gill, Min Choi, Sebastian Russo, Anja Hauth, Jinhyuk Lee, Michael Boratko, Megan Barnes, Vikram Rao, Claudiu Musat, Cyril Allauzen, Ehsan Variani, Shankar Kumar, Tom Bagby, Junyi Jiao, Yang Gu, Tengxin Li, Ayush Agrawal, Roberto Santana, Dev Nath, Stephen Karukas, Shuoxuan Han, Lucia Loher, Alice Twu, Nidhi Vyas, Siddharth Bhai, Frank Palma Gomez, Wangyuan Zhang, Chaoren Liu, Jizheng Yang, Steve Qiu, Shijie Zhang, Sujay Kulkarni, Sascha Rothe, Sean Nakamoto, Raphael Hoffmann, Zach Gleicher, Yunhsuan Sung, Qin Yin, Tom Duerig, Mojtaba Seyedhosseini
First: 2026-05-26T17:07:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:07:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce Gemini Embedding 2, a native multimodal embedding model that allows embedding video, audio, image, and text modalities in a unified representation space. We leverage the multimodal capabilities of Gemini to produce embeddings for arbitrary combinations of interleaved inputs across all these modalities that generalize well across a wide variety of tasks. Applying large-scale contrastive learning in a multi-task multi-stage training setup, we achieve state-of-the-art performance on key embedding benchmarks including unimodal, cross-modal, and multimodal retrieval spanning a diverse set of tasks. We show that our embedding model demonstrates strong performance (with a score of 62.9 R@1 on MSCOCO, 68.8 NDCG@10 on Vatex, 69.9 on MTEB multilingual and 84.0 on MTEB Code) across a variety of tasks surpassing the performance of specialized models. These unified capabilities make Gemini Embedding 2 a promising candidate for downstream use cases such as RAG, recommendation and search. Furthermore, its robust zero-shot performance across distinct fields - from astronomy and bioscience to fine arts and the culinary arts - establishes it as a highly reliable, out-of-the-box representation even for specialized domains.

Separating Semantic Competition from Context Length in RAG Reading
Authors: Vyzantinos Repantis, Ameya Gawde, Harshvardhan Singh, Rohit Alekar, Cien Zhang, Svetlana Karslioglu, Akash Vishwakarma
First: 2026-05-26T17:06:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:06:55+00:00
Comments: 4 pages, 1 figure, 2 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems can respond incorrectly even when the correct passage was retrieved. The model must still read the retrieved passages and identify which one contains the answer among others that look relevant. This passage-reading model is called the reader. Does it fail simply because the context is longer or because the other passages genuinely compete with the correct one? We introduce and demonstrate a matched-control protocol for RAG reading: we keep the number and length of passages fixed, but replace hard competitors with less competitive real passages. We apply this control across two compact open models on SQuAD. This replacement partially restores performance, with the strongest effects on F1 and answer inclusion. For Phi-2, this recovers +6.0 EM points, +7.0 answer-inclusion points, and +0.057 F1. For Qwen2.5-1.5B, it recovers +4.5 EM points, +9.0 answer-inclusion points, and +0.068 F1. To track how performance changes as competitors accumulate, we also report retention curves and summarize them with a right-censored half-life when the curves do not cross half-retention. Together, these results show the protocol isolates a competition effect distinct from context length, though the effect is clearer for F1 and answer inclusion than for exact match, and also varies with snippet length.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在RAG阅读中分离语义竞争与上下文长度
检索增强生成（RAG）系统即使检索到正确段落仍可能产生错误回答。模型仍需阅读检索到的段落，并在看似相关的其他段落中识别出包含答案的段落。这种段落阅读模型被称为“阅读器”。其失败是由于上下文过长，还是因为其他段落确实与正确段落存在语义竞争？我们引入并演示了一种匹配对照协议：保持段落数量和长度不变，但用竞争性较弱的真实段落替换强竞争段落。我们在SQuAD数据集上对两个紧凑型开源模型应用该对照方法。替换操作部分恢复了性能，对F1和答案包含率的影响最显著。对Phi-2模型，恢复效果为+6.0 EM分、+7.0答案包含分、+0.057 F1；对Qwen2.5-1.5B模型，恢复效果为+4.5 EM分、+9.0答案包含分、+0.068 F1。为追踪性能随竞争段落累积的变化，我们还报告了保留曲线，并在曲线未交叉半保留点时用右删失半衰期进行总结。综合结果表明，该协议能分离出与上下文长度无关的竞争效应，尽管该效应在F1和答案包含率上比精确匹配更明显，且随片段长度变化。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems fail due to context length or semantic competition among retrieved passages. Using a matched-control protocol that fixes passage count and length while replacing competitive distractors with less competitive real passages, the authors evaluate two compact models on SQuAD. Results show performance recovery: Phi-2 gains +6.0 EM, +7.0 answer-inclusion, and +0.057 F1; Qwen2.5-1.5B gains +4.5 EM, +9.0 answer-inclusion, and +0.068 F1. Retention curves and half-life metrics further demonstrate that competition effects are separable from context length, with stronger recovery in F1 and answer-inclusion than EM, and varying by snippet length.
该研究探讨了检索增强生成（RAG）系统错误是否源于上下文过长或检索结果间语义竞争。通过固定段落数量与长度、替换高竞争段落为低竞争真实段落的匹配控制协议，作者在SQuAD数据集上测试了两个紧凑模型。结果显示性能显著恢复：Phi-2提升+6.0 EM、+7.0答案包含率和+0.057 F1；Qwen2.5-1.5B提升+4.5 EM、+9.0答案包含率和+0.068 F1。保留曲线与半衰期指标进一步表明，竞争效应可与上下文长度分离，F1和答案包含率恢复更明显，且随段落长度变化。
BASIS: Batchwise Advantage Estimation from Single-Rollout Information Sharing for LLM Reasoning
Authors: Shijin Gong, Erhan Xu, Kai Ye, Francesco Quinzan, Giulia Livieri, Chengchun Shi
First: 2026-05-26T17:06:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:06:41+00:00
Comments: 17 pages, 7 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards has become a standard recipe for improving the reasoning abilities of large language models. Existing algorithms face a tradeoff between computational efficiency and sample efficiency in value estimation and policy learning. We introduce BASIS, a critic-free post-training algorithm designed to address this tradeoff. At each online training step, BASIS samples only one rollout per prompt, but leverages rich information across prompts in the entire batch to improve value function estimation. Our experiments demonstrate that BASIS reduces MSE in value function estimation by 69% compared to REINFORCE++, a representative single-rollout baseline, and achieves lower MSE with one rollout than group mean estimators with 8 rollouts. This improvement in value estimation translates to better policy optimization: using substantially less training time, BASIS achieves performance close to multi-rollout GRPO-type baselines and often outperforms single-rollout REINFORCE-type baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BASIS：基于单次 rollout 信息共享的批处理优势估计用于大语言模型推理
可验证奖励的强化学习已成为提升大语言模型推理能力的标准方法。现有算法在价值估计和策略学习中面临计算效率与样本效率之间的权衡。我们提出了BASIS，一种无批评器的后训练算法，旨在解决这一权衡问题。在每次在线训练步骤中，BASIS仅对每个提示采样一次 rollout，但利用整个批次中各提示的丰富信息以提升价值函数估计。实验表明，与代表性单次 rollout 基线REINFORCE++相比，BASIS将价值函数估计的均方误差降低了69%，且在仅使用一次 rollout 的情况下，其均方误差低于使用8次 rollout 的组均值估计器。价值估计的提升转化为更优的策略优化：BASIS在大幅减少训练时间的情况下，性能接近多 rollout GRPO 类基线，且通常优于单次 rollout REINFORCE 类基线。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational inefficiency of existing reinforcement learning methods for LLM reasoning, BASIS introduces a critic-free post-training algorithm that trades sample efficiency for computational speed by using only one rollout per prompt while leveraging batch-wide information sharing to enhance value estimation. The method aggregates and shares information across prompts in each batch to improve the accuracy of value function estimation without requiring additional rollouts. Experimental results show BASIS reduces value estimation MSE by 69% compared to REINFORCE++ and achieves lower MSE than group mean estimators using eight rollouts, translating into superior policy optimization with significantly less training time, often outperforming single-rollout baselines and approaching multi-rollout GRPO performance.
为解决现有强化学习方法在大语言模型推理中计算效率与样本效率之间的权衡，BASIS提出一种无价值函数的后训练算法，仅对每个提示采样一个 rollout，但通过批处理内所有提示的信息共享来提升价值函数估计。在每一步训练中，它通过聚合批处理内各提示的信号来估计价值函数，无需每个提示多次采样。实验表明，BASIS相比REINFORCE++将价值函数估计的均方误差降低69%，且在单 rollout 下优于使用8个 rollout 的组均值估计器，这种价值估计的提升转化为更优的策略优化，在显著减少训练时间的情况下，性能接近多 rollout 的GRPO基线，且常优于单 rollout 的REINFORCE基线。
MVISTA-4D: View-Consistent 4D World Model with Test-Time Action Inference for Robotic Manipulation
Authors: Jiaxu Wang, Yicheng Jiang, Tianlun He, Jingkai Sun, Qiang Zhang, Junhao He, Jiahang Cao, Zesen Gan, Mingyuan Sun, Qiming Shao, Xiangyu Yue
Venue: International Conference on Machine Learning 2026
First: 2026-02-10T15:19:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:06:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
World-model-based imagine-then-act becomes a promising paradigm for robotic manipulation, yet existing approaches typically support either purely image-based forecasting or reasoning over partial 3D geometry, limiting their ability to predict complete 4D scene dynamics. This work proposes a novel embodied 4D world model that enables geometrically consistent, arbitrary-view RGBD generation: given only a single-view RGBD observation as input, the model imagines the remaining viewpoints, which can then be back-projected and fused to assemble a more complete 3D structure across time. To efficiently learn the multi-view, cross-modality generation, we explicitly design cross-view and cross-modality feature fusion that jointly encourage consistency between RGB and depth and enforce geometric alignment across views. Beyond prediction, converting generated futures into actions is often handled by inverse dynamics, which is ill-posed because multiple actions can explain the same transition. We address this with a test-time action optimization strategy that backpropagates through the generative model to infer a trajectory-level latent best matching the predicted future, and a residual inverse dynamics model that turns this trajectory prior into accurate executable actions. Experiments on three datasets demonstrate strong performance on both 4D scene generation and downstream manipulation, and ablations provide practical insights into the key design choices.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MVISTA-4D：面向机器人操作的视图一致4D世界模型与测试时动作推断
基于世界模型的想象-然后-行动范式在机器人操作中展现出巨大潜力，但现有方法通常仅支持纯图像预测或对部分3D几何进行推理，限制了其预测完整4D场景动态的能力。本文提出一种新颖的具身4D世界模型，可实现几何一致、任意视角的RGBD生成：仅输入单视角RGBD观测，模型即可想象其余视角，再通过反投影与融合构建更完整的时序3D结构。为高效学习多视角、跨模态生成，我们显式设计跨视角与跨模态特征融合机制，共同促进RGB与深度的一致性，并强制各视角间几何对齐。除预测外，将生成的未来状态转化为动作常依赖逆动力学，但该问题因多个动作可解释相同状态转移而病态。我们通过测试时动作优化策略解决此问题：反向传播通过生成模型推断与预测未来最匹配的轨迹级隐变量，并结合残差逆动力学模型将该轨迹先验转化为可执行动作。在三个数据集上的实验表明，该方法在4D场景生成与下游操作任务中均表现优异，消融实验也提供了关键设计选择的实用见解。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing world-model approaches that either rely solely on images or partial 3D geometry, this work introduces MVISTA-4D, a novel embodied 4D world model that generates geometrically consistent RGBD views from a single input observation, enabling reconstruction of full 3D scene dynamics over time. The model employs cross-view and cross-modality feature fusion to ensure RGB-depth consistency and geometric alignment across viewpoints. To bridge prediction with action, it uses test-time action optimization that backpropagates through the generative model to infer a latent trajectory matching the predicted future, followed by a residual inverse dynamics model to convert this into executable actions. Experiments on three datasets show strong performance in 4D scene generation and manipulation tasks, with ablations confirming the effectiveness of key architectural components.
为克服现有世界模型仅依赖图像或部分3D几何导致预测不完整的问题，本文提出MVISTA-4D，一种能从单视角RGBD输入生成几何一致、多视角RGBD数据并重建时序完整3D场景的具身4D世界模型。模型通过跨视角与跨模态特征融合机制，确保RGB与深度一致性及多视角几何对齐。为实现动作推理，采用测试时优化策略反向传播生成未来轨迹，并结合残差逆动力学模型将其转化为可执行动作。在三个数据集上的实验表明，该模型在4D场景生成与下游操作任务中表现优异，消融实验验证了核心设计的有效性。
Detectability in Diversity: Improved Canary Crafting for Privacy Auditing in One Run
Authors: Mathieu Dagréou, Aurélien Bellet
First: 2026-05-26T17:06:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:06:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Privacy auditing aims to empirically assess privacy leakage in machine learning models using membership inference attacks (MIAs), and to derive lower bounds on differential privacy (DP) parameters. Recent one-run auditing methods address the high cost of standard approaches by relying on a single training run with multiple "canary" points whose inclusion or exclusion must be detected by the auditor. In this work, we study the problem of efficiently crafting canaries for one-run privacy auditing. Motivated by recent theoretical insights suggesting that interference between canaries contributes to weaker leakage estimates compared to multi-run methods, we propose to optimize canaries to be both highly detectable and minimally interfering. Our approach combines a greedy initialization based on influence functions with a bilevel optimization procedure that maximizes distinguishability while promoting diversity in embedding space, enabling the use of computationally efficient bilevel algorithms. Experiments show that our method achieves stronger privacy leakage estimates at a lower computational cost than existing canary crafting approaches.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多样性中的可检测性：面向单次运行隐私审计的改进型Canary构造
隐私审计旨在通过成员推断攻击（MIAs）实证评估机器学习模型中的隐私泄露，并推导差分隐私（DP）参数的下界。近期的单次运行审计方法通过依赖单次训练运行并包含多个“Canary”点（其是否被包含需由审计者检测），解决了标准方法成本过高的问题。本文研究如何高效构造Canary以支持单次运行隐私审计。受近期理论见解启发，即Canary间的干扰会削弱相比多轮方法的泄露估计，我们提出优化Canary使其兼具高度可检测性与最小干扰性。我们的方法结合基于影响函数的贪心初始化与双层优化过程，在最大化可区分性的同时促进嵌入空间中的多样性，从而支持计算高效的双层算法。实验表明，我们的方法在更低计算成本下实现了比现有Canary构造方法更强的隐私泄露估计。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that canary interference weakens privacy leakage estimates in one-run auditing, this work proposes a method to craft canaries that are both highly detectable and minimally interfering. The approach combines greedy initialization via influence functions with bilevel optimization to maximize distinguishability while promoting embedding diversity, enabling efficient computation. Experimental results demonstrate that this method yields stronger privacy leakage estimates compared to existing approaches, while reducing computational cost.
鉴于单次运行审计中canary相互干扰会削弱隐私泄露估计，本文提出一种兼顾高可检测性与低干扰性的canary构造方法。该方法结合基于影响函数的贪心初始化与双层优化，以最大化可区分性并促进嵌入空间多样性，从而实现高效计算。实验表明，该方法在降低计算成本的同时，比现有方法获得更强的隐私泄露估计。
Iterative Refinement Neural Operators are Learned Fixed-Point Solvers: A Principled Approach to Spectral Bias Mitigation
Authors: Xiaotian Liu, Shuyuan Shang, Xiaopeng Wang, Pu Ren, Yaoqing Yang
Venue: ICML 2026 Spotlight
First: 2026-05-21T19:41:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:05:05+00:00
Comments: 47 pages; accepted to ICML 2026 as a Spotlight
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Neural operators serve as fast, data-driven surrogates for scientific modeling but typically rely on a monolithic, single-pass inference procedure that struggles to resolve high-frequency details, a limitation known as spectral bias. We introduce the Iterative Refinement Neural Operator (IRNO), which augments pre-trained operators with a learned refinement module iteratively applied via fixed-point iteration. IRNO decomposes the prediction into a coarse initialization followed by successive residual corrections, paralleling classical numerical solvers. Under local assumptions, we establish contraction of the induced operator, ensuring convergence to a unique fixed point. To explicitly target high-frequency errors, we propose a progressive spectral loss that adaptively increases penalty on high-frequency components over refinement steps during training. Across physical systems, IRNO consistently lowers error, with up to 56.05% improvement on turbulent flow. On Active Matter, spectral analysis reveals that, relative to base operator, the normalized error ratios decrease to 27.72-36.10% in low-, 5.07-6.68% in mid-, and 1.48-2.04% in high-frequencies, remaining stable beyond the trained iteration count. Code is available at https://github.com/xiaotianliu-dartmouth/Iterative_Refinement_Neural_Operator
中文标题/摘要
标题：迭代细化神经算子是学习到的不动点求解器：一种系统性缓解频谱偏置的方法
神经算子作为科学建模的快速数据驱动代理，但通常依赖单次推理的单体结构，难以处理高频细节，这一局限称为频谱偏置。我们提出迭代细化神经算子（IRNO），通过在预训练算子上附加一个学习到的细化模块，利用不动点迭代逐步应用。IRNO将预测分解为粗略初始化后接连续残差修正，与经典数值求解器类似。在局部假设下，我们证明诱导算子的压缩性，确保收敛至唯一不动点。为显式针对高频误差，我们提出渐进频谱损失，在训练过程中随细化步骤自适应增加高频成分的惩罚。在多个物理系统中，IRNO持续降低误差，湍流流动中最高提升达56.05%。在活性物质系统中，频谱分析显示，相较于基础算子，低频、中频、高频的归一化误差比率分别降至27.72-36.10%、5.07-6.68%、1.48-2.04%，且在训练迭代次数后保持稳定。代码见https://github.com/xiaotianliu-dartmouth/Iterative_Refinement_Neural_Operator
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the spectral bias in neural operators that limits their ability to resolve high-frequency details, this work proposes the Iterative Refinement Neural Operator (IRNO), which enhances pre-trained operators with a learned refinement module applied via fixed-point iteration. The method decomposes predictions into a coarse initial estimate followed by iterative residual corrections, mimicking classical numerical solvers, and guarantees convergence under local assumptions. Training incorporates a progressive spectral loss that progressively penalizes high-frequency errors, improving performance across physical systems. Experiments show IRNO reduces error by up to 56.05% in turbulent flow simulations and achieves consistent spectral error reduction across low, mid, and high frequencies in Active Matter, with error ratios dropping to 27.72–36.10%, 5.07–6.68%, and 1.48–2.04% respectively, remaining stable beyond training iterations.
为解决神经算子在高频细节建模上的谱偏置问题，本文提出迭代修正神经算子（IRNO），通过在预训练算子基础上引入可学习的修正模块，采用固定点迭代方式逐步优化预测。该方法将预测分解为粗略初始化与多次残差修正，模拟经典数值求解器，并在局部假设下证明其收敛至唯一固定点。训练中引入渐进谱损失，逐步增强对高频误差的惩罚，提升高频建模能力。实验表明，IRNO在多个物理系统中显著降低误差，湍流场景提升达56.05%，在活性物质系统中谱分析显示，低频误差比降至27.72–36.10%，中频为5.07–6.68%，高频为1.48–2.04%，且在训练迭代后仍保持稳定。
It's Not Always Sycophancy: Measuring LLM Conformity as a Function of Epistemic Uncertainty
Authors: Kevin H. Guo, Chao Yan, Avinash Baidya, Katherine Brown, Xiang Gao, Juming Xiong, Zhijun Yin, Bradley A. Malin
First: 2026-05-26T17:04:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:04:11+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are known to abandon their initial stance to conform to user pushback. While prior research largely attributes this behavior to sycophancy learned during reinforcement learning from human feedback, we hypothesize that conformity is also driven by a model's epistemic uncertainty at inference time. In this paper, we introduce MUSE, a two-stage evaluation framework to disentangle the mechanisms driving LLM conformity. Specifically, MUSE maps a model's epistemic uncertainty in responding to a query against its likelihood to yield to user pushback in a subsequent turn. We demonstrate that the mechanisms driving conformity extend beyond sycophancy alone. Specifically, we characterize two distinct factors that jointly drive conformity: sycophantic conformity, where a model aligns with user pushback even with absolute certainty in its initial response, and uncertainty-driven conformity, where a model's likelihood for conformity increases alongside its uncertainty. Furthermore, we conduct ablation studies to demonstrate that both sycophantic conformity and uncertainty-driven conformity grow with 1) the LLM's perceived expertise of the user and 2) the plausibility of the user's suggestions. More broadly, MUSE informs more targeted intervention strategies by distinguishing alignment-induced sycophancy and training-corpora-driven uncertainty.
中文标题/摘要
标题：并非总是阿谀奉承：测量大语言模型的顺从性作为认知不确定性函数
大语言模型（LLMs）在面对用户反驳时，常会放弃初始立场以迎合用户。尽管先前研究主要将此行为归因于在强化学习中从人类反馈中习得的阿谀奉承，我们假设顺从性也受模型在推理时的认知不确定性驱动。本文提出MUSE，一种两阶段评估框架，用于分离驱动LLM顺从性的机制。具体而言，MUSE将模型对查询回应时的认知不确定性与其在后续回合中顺从用户反驳的可能性进行映射。我们证明，驱动顺从性的机制不仅限于阿谀奉承。具体而言，我们刻画了两种共同驱动顺从性的因素：阿谀奉承型顺从（即使模型对其初始回答绝对确定，仍会迎合用户反驳）和不确定性驱动型顺从（模型顺从可能性随其不确定性增加而上升）。此外，我们通过消融研究证明，无论是阿谀奉承型顺从还是不确定性驱动型顺从，均随1）LLM对用户专业性的感知程度和2）用户建议的合理性而增强。更广泛地说，MUSE通过区分由对齐引发的阿谀奉承与由训练语料驱动的认知不确定性，为更精准的干预策略提供依据。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates whether LLM conformity to user pushback stems solely from sycophantic behavior or is also influenced by epistemic uncertainty. It introduces MUSE, a two-stage framework that correlates a model’s epistemic uncertainty with its tendency to yield to user feedback. Experimental results reveal two distinct conformity mechanisms: sycophantic conformity, where models align with user pushback despite high confidence in their initial stance, and uncertainty-driven conformity, where models are more likely to conform as their uncertainty increases. Ablation studies further show both mechanisms intensify when users are perceived as more expert or when their suggestions are more plausible, suggesting conformity is not purely sycophantic but also epistemic in nature.
该研究探讨大语言模型顺从用户反馈是否仅源于谄媚行为，还是也受认知不确定性影响。论文提出MUSE框架，通过关联模型在回答时的认知不确定性与其后续顺从用户反馈的概率，揭示两种顺从机制：谄媚顺从（即使初始立场绝对确定仍顺从用户）与不确定性驱动顺从（不确定性越高越易顺从）。消融实验表明，这两种机制在用户被感知为更专业或建议更合理时均增强，说明顺从行为同时受对齐激励与模型不确定性驱动。
A Dynamic Programming Framework for Discovering Count and Values of Multilevel Image Thresholding
Authors: Eslam Hegazy, Mohamed Gabr
First: 2026-05-26T17:03:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:03:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multilevel Image thresholding is an important preprocessing algorithm in computer vision applications nowadays. Since most common thresholding methods take the desired count of thresholds as input by the user, thresholding methods that automatically determines a suitable count of thresholds from the input image itself are advantageous. In this article, a novel thresholding method based on a dynamic programming algorithm and a modification of Minimum Error Thresholding (MET) criterion is thoroughly presented. An empirical statistical study is performed to pinpoint why this proposed method is superior. Moreover, an extended comparison between this proposed method and other state-of-the-art methods is performed on a comprehensive set of natural, satellite and medical test images. The numerical results show that the proposed MET-DP method takes much less time than traditional dynamic programming thresholding methods when the number of thresholds is high. The proposed method can detect a suitable count of thresholds for most of tested images of different types. However, traditional methods that take the count of thresholds as input produce thresholded images of higher structural similarity index measure (SSIM) and peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) values than MET-DP. Source code can be found on https://w3id.org/met-dp/article1-code
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于发现多级图像阈值数量与值的动态规划框架

The method employs dynamic programming to efficiently search for threshold configurations that minimize classification error, and is evaluated against state-of-the-art techniques across diverse image types. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed MET-DP method significantly reduces computation time for high-threshold-count scenarios while maintaining competitive segmentation quality, though traditional methods yield slightly higher SSIM and PSNR values in some cases.
为解决多级图像阈值分割中手动设定阈值数量的问题，本文提出一种基于动态规划的改进最小误差阈值法（MET-DP），能自动确定最优阈值数量。该方法通过动态规划高效搜索阈值配置，显著降低高阈值数量时的计算开销。在自然、卫星和医学图像上的实验表明，MET-DP方法计算速度快且能稳定识别合适阈值数量，但其输出图像的结构相似性（SSIM）和峰值信噪比（PSNR）略低于需手动输入阈值数量的传统方法。
Falcon-X: A Time Series Foundation Model for Heterogeneous Multivariate Modeling
Authors: Yiding Liu, Yifan Hu, Hongjie Xia, Peiyuan Liu, Hongzhou Chen, Xilin Dai, Zewei Dong, Jiang-Ming Yang
First: 2026-05-26T17:03:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:03:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Time series foundation models (TSFMs) are transforming the forecasting paradigm through large-scale cross-domain pretraining. However, most existing TSFMs remain univariate, and recent efforts to enable cross-variate modeling still operate directly within the raw variate space. This design introduces fundamental limitations in semantic alignment and relational expressivity. Specifically, raw-space group mixing lacks a dedicated mechanism to align heterogeneous physical quantities, while standard non-negative attention fails to capture the complex synergistic and antagonistic interactions ubiquitous in real-world systems. To address these challenges, we propose Falcon-X, decouples variates from the raw space and maps them into a unified latent prototype space. Falcon-X employs a Unified Prototype Diff-Attention mechanism that explicitly evaluates both positive and negative semantic affinities to explicitly align heterogeneous variates. Cross-variate interactions are then efficiently performed within this shared space via Latent Entity Attention, naturally facilitating zero-shot structural transfer. Finally, a Variate Reassembly Router robustly reconstructs variate-specific trajectories via a request-and-dispatch mechanism. Extensive evaluations on the GIFT-Eval and fev-bench benchmarks demonstrate that Falcon-X achieves state-of-the-art forecasting performance, offering a principled and scalable paradigm for complex multivariate environments. Falcon-X is publicly released to support future research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Falcon-X：面向异构多变量建模的时间序列基础模型
时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）通过大规模跨域预训练正在重塑预测范式。然而，现有大多数TSFMs仍为单变量模型，近期尝试实现跨变量建模的方法仍直接在原始变量空间中操作，这种设计在语义对齐和关系表达能力方面存在根本性局限。具体而言，原始空间中的组混合缺乏专门机制对异构物理量进行对齐，而标准非负注意力无法捕捉现实系统中普遍存在的复杂协同与对抗交互。为解决这些问题，我们提出Falcon-X，将变量从原始空间解耦并映射至统一的潜在原型空间。Falcon-X采用统一原型差分注意力机制，显式评估正负语义亲和度，以显式对齐异构变量。随后，通过潜在实体注意力在共享空间中高效执行跨变量交互，自然支持零样本结构迁移。最后，通过请求-分发机制，变量重组路由器可稳健重建各变量特定轨迹。在GIFT-Eval和fev-bench基准上的广泛评估表明，Falcon-X实现了最先进的预测性能，提供了一种适用于复杂多变量环境的原理清晰且可扩展的范式。Falcon-X已公开发布，以支持未来研究。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing univariate time series foundation models and their inability to effectively model heterogeneous multivariate systems, Falcon-X introduces a novel framework that decouples variates from raw space and maps them into a unified latent prototype space. It employs a Unified Prototype Diff-Attention mechanism to explicitly align heterogeneous variates by evaluating both positive and negative semantic affinities, followed by Latent Entity Attention to capture cross-variate interactions within the shared space. A Variate Reassembly Router then reconstructs individual variate trajectories via request-and-dispatch. Experimental results on GIFT-Eval and fev-bench benchmarks show Falcon-X achieves state-of-the-art forecasting performance, demonstrating its effectiveness in complex multivariate settings.
为解决现有单变量时间序列基础模型在处理异构多变量系统时存在的语义对齐与关系表达不足问题，Falcon-X提出将变量从原始空间解耦并映射至统一的潜在原型空间。其采用统一原型差分注意力机制，显式评估正负语义亲和度以对齐异构变量，再通过潜在实体注意力在共享空间内高效建模跨变量交互，并借助变量重组路由器通过请求-分发机制重构各变量轨迹。在GIFT-Eval与fev-bench基准测试中，Falcon-X实现当前最优的预测性能，验证了其在复杂多变量环境中的有效性与可扩展性。
PHALAR: Phasors for Learned Musical Audio Representations
Authors: Davide Marincione, Michele Mancusi, Giorgio Strano, Luca Cerovaz, Donato Crisostomi, Roberto Ribuoli, Emanuele Rodolà
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-05T16:19:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:01:23+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Stem retrieval, the task of matching missing stems to a given audio submix, is a key challenge currently limited by models that discard temporal information. We introduce PHALAR, a contrastive framework achieving a relative accuracy increase of up to $\approx 70\%$ over the state-of-the-art while requiring $<50\%$ of the parameters and a 7$\times$ training speedup. By utilizing a Learned Spectral Pooling layer and a complex-valued head, PHALAR enforces pitch-equivariant and phase-equivariant biases. PHALAR establishes new retrieval state-of-the-art across MoisesDB, Slakh, and ChocoChorales, correlating significantly higher with human coherence judgment than semantic baselines. Finally, zero-shot beat tracking and linear chord probing confirm that PHALAR captures robust musical structures beyond the retrieval task.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PHALAR：用于学习音乐音频表示的相量方法
音轨检索，即匹配缺失音轨到给定音频子混合的任务，目前受限于丢弃时序信息的模型。我们提出了PHALAR，一种对比框架，在参数量少于50%且训练速度提升7倍的情况下，相较现有最佳方法准确率提升约70%。通过使用学习谱池化层和复数值头，PHALAR强制施加音高等变和相位等变偏置。PHALAR在MoisesDB、Slakh和ChocoChorales数据集上建立了新的检索性能基准，其与人类连贯性判断的相关性显著高于语义基线。最后，零样本节拍跟踪和线性和弦探测证实，PHALAR捕捉了超越检索任务的稳健音乐结构。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of existing stem retrieval models that neglect temporal information, PHALAR introduces a contrastive learning framework that achieves up to a 70% relative accuracy improvement over prior methods while using fewer than half the parameters and training seven times faster. It employs a Learned Spectral Pooling layer and a complex-valued head to enforce pitch- and phase-equivariant biases, enhancing representation robustness. Experiments on MoisesDB, Slakh, and ChocoChorales show PHALAR sets new retrieval state-of-the-art, with results more aligned with human coherence judgments than semantic baselines, and further demonstrates transferability via zero-shot beat tracking and linear chord probing.
为解决现有音频子混合中缺失音轨匹配模型因忽略时序信息而受限的问题，PHALAR提出一种对比学习框架，在参数量减少一半、训练速度提升七倍的前提下，相较当前最优方法准确率提升约70%。该框架通过引入可学习频谱池化层与复值头，强制模型具备音高与相位等变性偏置，增强表征鲁棒性。在MoisesDB、Slakh与ChocoChorales三个数据集上，PHALAR刷新了检索任务的性能纪录，其结果与人类对音频连贯性的判断高度相关，且零样本节拍跟踪与线性和弦探测进一步验证其捕捉音乐结构的能力。
FineVLA: Fine-Grained Instruction Alignment for Steerable Vision-Language-Action Policies
Authors: Xintong Hu, Xuhong Huang, Jinyu Zhang, Yutong Yao, Yuchong Sun, Qiuyue Wang, Mingsheng Li, Sicheng Xie, Yitao Liu, Junhao Chen, Yixuan Chen, Yingming Zheng, Shuai Bai, Tao Yu
First: 2026-05-26T17:01:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T17:01:10+00:00
Comments: 26 pages, 7 figures, 25 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are increasingly expected to not only complete robot tasks, but also follow human instructions about how those tasks should be executed. However, existing robot datasets usually pair trajectories with coarse goal-level language, leaving execution-critical details such as active arm, approach direction, and contact region unspecified. This limits steerable policy learning and robotic video understanding. We introduce FineVLA, an open framework for action-aligned fine-grained VLA supervision. The framework includes: (1) a data construction tool that unifies 972,247 trajectories across 85K tasks from 10 open-source robot datasets and builds FineVLA-Data, a human-verified dataset of 47,159 fine-grained trajectories; (2) a held-out benchmark with 500 videos, 10,816 atomic facts, and 1,030 VQA questions; (3) a robotics-specialized VLM annotator for scalable fine-grained annotation; and (4) a steerable VLA policy trained with controlled mixtures of fine-grained and raw goal-level instructions. Our experiments yield three findings. First, fine-grained supervision does not sacrifice goal-level success: FG-only improves over Raw-only by +1.4 to +8.1 success-rate points across settings. Second, fine-grained and raw instructions are complementary, following a consistent inverted-U trend peaking at FG:Raw = 1:2 to 1:1. The best mixed setting reaches 86.8%/82.5% in RoboTwin simulation and 62.7/100 in real-world dual-arm manipulation (vs. 49.9 Raw-only). Third, fine-grained supervision improves steerable control: the largest real-world gains appear on pose (+23), color (+18), and approach direction (+18)--factors where goal-level instructions provide no guidance. Overall, fine-grained language should augment goal-level instructions: specifying how to execute alongside what to achieve. Project page: https://finevla.xlang.ai/

标题：FineVLA：面向可引导视觉-语言-动作策略的细粒度指令对齐
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型正日益被期望不仅完成机器人任务，还需遵循人类关于任务执行方式的指令。然而，现有机器人数据集通常将轨迹与粗粒度目标级语言配对，未明确执行关键细节如活动手臂、接近方向和接触区域。这限制了可引导策略学习和机器人视频理解。我们提出FineVLA，一个面向动作对齐的细粒度VLA监督的开放框架。该框架包括：（1）一个数据构建工具，统一10个开源机器人数据集中85K任务的972,247条轨迹，构建经人工验证的47,159条细粒度轨迹的FineVLA-Data数据集；（2）一个包含500个视频、10,816个原子事实和1,030个VQA问题的保留基准；（3）一个专为机器人设计的VLM标注器，支持可扩展的细粒度标注；（4）一个通过细粒度与原始目标级指令混合控制训练的可引导VLA策略。实验得出三点发现：第一，细粒度监督不牺牲目标级成功率：FG-only在不同设置下较Raw-only提升+1.4至+8.1个百分点；第二，细粒度与原始指令互补，呈现一致的倒U型趋势，峰值在FG:Raw=1:2至1:1，最佳混合设置在RoboTwin仿真中达86.8%/82.5%，真实双臂操作中达62.7/100（vs. 49.9 Raw-only）；第三，细粒度监督提升可引导控制：在姿态（+23）、颜色（+18）和接近方向（+18）等目标级指令无指导因素上收益最大。总体而言，细粒度语言应补充目标级指令：明确如何执行与实现什么目标。项目页面：https://finevla.xlang.ai/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for precise execution guidance in robot tasks, the paper introduces FineVLA, a framework that aligns vision-language-action policies with fine-grained human instructions. It constructs a dataset of 47,159 human-verified trajectories by unifying and annotating 972,247 trajectories from 10 robot datasets, then trains a steerable policy using mixed fine-grained and goal-level instructions. Experiments show fine-grained supervision boosts success rates by +1.4 to +8.1 points over raw goal-level instructions, achieves peak performance at a 1:2 to 1:1 fine-grained to raw ratio, and significantly improves control over pose, color, and approach direction in real-world tasks, where goal-level instructions offer no guidance.
为使机器人能执行更精细的指令而非仅完成高阶目标，作者提出FineVLA框架，通过标注来自10个机器人数据集的47,159条轨迹，构建细粒度视觉-语言-动作监督数据。该方法结合专用视觉语言模型标注器与混合指令训练策略，用于可操控策略学习。实验表明，细粒度监督可使成功率较原始目标指令提升1.4至8.1个百分点，最佳混合比例为1:2至1:1，且在姿态、颜色、接近方向等原始指令无法指导的领域显著提升控制能力，实测提升分别为+23、+18、+18。
Causal Risk Minimization for High-Dimensional Treatments
Authors: Nikita Dhawan, Arnav Paruthi, Andrew Kim, Lovedeep Gondara, Jekaterina Novikova, Chris J. Maddison
First: 2026-05-26T16:58:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T16:58:39+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Predicting the effect of interventions with many possible variations, e.g., therapeutic content that affects mental health outcomes or an earnings call transcript that drives movement in share price, is useful across several domains. However, classical causal estimators tend to assume that all possible interventions are observed, which is infeasible when interventions vary widely, for instance, in the space of all text strings. We adapt a well-known approach of recasting causal inference as a learning problem, to address high-dimensional treatment spaces. Specifically, under standard assumptions like no unobserved confounding, we show that causal error decomposes into a series of moment-balancing errors of increasing order, and design objectives that directly improve causal estimation. We also show how to project the effect of a high-dimensional treatment onto lower-dimensional treatment attributes, which allows a single model to answer several causal questions without additional attribute-specific training. We empirically evaluate our estimators in settings with high-dimensional continuous, discrete, and text treatments, the last of which used a semi-synthetic dataset of Amazon Reviews. Our experiments demonstrate the benefit of higher-order balance error optimization and competitive performance of projected causal estimates with attribute-specific estimators.
中文标题/摘要
标题：高维治疗的因果风险最小化
预测具有多种可能变化的干预措施的效果（例如，影响心理健康结果的治疗内容或推动股价变动的财报电话会议记录）在多个领域都具有实用价值。然而，经典因果估计器通常假设所有可能的干预措施均被观测到，这在干预措施变化广泛（如所有文本字符串空间）时不可行。我们采用将因果推断重新表述为学习问题的已知方法，以应对高维治疗空间。具体而言，在无未观测混杂因素等标准假设下，我们证明因果误差可分解为一系列递增阶数的矩平衡误差，并设计直接改进因果估计的目标函数。我们还展示了如何将高维治疗效果投影到低维治疗属性上，从而允许单个模型回答多个因果问题，而无需针对每个属性进行额外训练。我们在高维连续、离散和文本治疗的场景中对估计器进行了实证评估，其中文本治疗使用了亚马逊评论的半合成数据集。实验结果表明，高阶平衡误差优化具有优势，且投影因果估计与属性特定估计器相比表现具有竞争力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to estimate causal effects in high-dimensional treatment spaces such as text or continuous variables, the paper reformulates causal inference as a learning problem under standard assumptions like no unobserved confounding. It introduces a method that decomposes causal error into moment-balancing errors of increasing order, enabling direct optimization of higher-order balance to improve estimation. The approach also projects high-dimensional treatments onto lower-dimensional attributes, allowing a single model to answer multiple causal queries without retraining. Experiments across continuous, discrete, and text treatments—including a semi-synthetic Amazon Reviews dataset—show that optimizing higher-order balance improves performance and that projected estimates rival attribute-specific models.
为解决高维治疗空间（如文本干预）下的因果效应估计问题，该研究将因果推断转化为学习问题，通过将因果误差分解为递增阶数的矩平衡误差，直接优化以提升估计精度。方法通过将高维治疗投影到低维属性上，使单一模型可回答多个因果问题而无需额外训练。在连续、离散及文本治疗场景（含半合成亚马逊评论数据集）的实验中，高阶矩平衡误差优化显著提升效果，且投影估计性能与属性特定模型相当。
ReVEL: Multi-Turn Reflective LLM-Guided Heuristic Evolution via Structured Performance Feedback
Authors: Cuong Van Duc, Minh Nguyen Dinh Tuan, Tam Vu Duc, Tung Vu Duy, Son Nguyen Van, Hanh Nguyen Thi, Binh Huynh Thi Thanh
First: 2026-03-05T04:52:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T16:58:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Designing effective heuristics for NP-hard combinatorial optimization problems remains challenging and often requires substantial domain expertise. Recent LLM-guided evolutionary methods have shown promise for automated heuristic generation, but most existing approaches refine heuristics independently or through limited pairwise feedback. We propose ReVEL: Multi-Turn Reflective LLM-Guided Heuristic Evolution via Structured Performance Feedback, a framework for group-wise multi-turn heuristic refinement. ReVEL organizes heuristics into behavior-aware reflective groups, including similarity-driven groups for localized refinement and diversity-driven groups for exploratory search. Within each group, the LLM performs iterative multi-turn refinement using accumulated performance feedback, enabling related heuristics to be jointly analyzed and progressively improved across evolutionary iterations. Experiments on standard combinatorial optimization benchmarks show that ReVEL generally improves optimization performance over existing LLM-guided evolutionary baselines across multiple settings and LLM backbones. Additional analyses suggest that behavior-aware grouping contributes to more consistent refinement trajectories during iterative heuristic evolution.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ReVEL：通过结构化性能反馈实现多轮反思式LLM引导启发式进化
为NP难组合优化问题设计有效的启发式方法仍具挑战性，通常需要大量领域专业知识。近期的LLM引导进化方法在自动化启发式生成方面展现出潜力，但多数现有方法独立或仅通过有限的成对反馈优化启发式。我们提出ReVEL：通过结构化性能反馈实现多轮反思式LLM引导启发式进化，这是一种群体式多轮启发式优化框架。ReVEL将启发式方法组织为行为感知的反思性群体，包括基于相似性的群体用于局部优化和基于多样性的群体用于探索性搜索。在每个群体内，LLM利用累积的性能反馈进行迭代多轮优化，使相关启发式方法能联合分析并逐步跨进化迭代提升。在标准组合优化基准测试中，ReVEL在多种设置和LLM架构下普遍优于现有LLM引导进化基线。进一步分析表明，行为感知分组有助于在启发式迭代进化过程中实现更一致的优化轨迹。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of designing effective heuristics for NP-hard combinatorial optimization problems and the limitations of existing LLM-guided methods that lack structured, multi-turn feedback, ReVEL introduces a framework that groups heuristics into behavior-aware reflective clusters—similarity-driven for local refinement and diversity-driven for exploration. Within each group, an LLM iteratively refines heuristics using accumulated performance feedback, enabling joint analysis and progressive improvement across generations. Experimental results on standard benchmarks demonstrate consistent performance gains over existing LLM-based evolutionary baselines across multiple models and settings, with analyses confirming that behavior-aware grouping stabilizes refinement trajectories.
为应对NP难组合优化问题中设计有效启发式方法的挑战，以及现有LLM引导方法在独立或有限反馈下优化启发式方法的局限性，ReVEL提出一种多轮反思框架，将启发式方法按行为特征分组——相似性驱动组用于局部优化，多样性驱动组用于探索性搜索。每组内LLM利用累积性能反馈进行多轮迭代优化，实现启发式方法的联合分析与渐进改进。实验表明，ReVEL在多个基准和LLM模型上均优于现有方法，分析显示行为分组有助于稳定启发式演化的优化轨迹。
Understanding the Challenges in Iterative Generative Optimization with LLMs
Authors: Allen Nie, Xavier Daull, Zhiyi Kuang, Abhinav Akkiraju, Anish Chaudhuri, Max Piasevoli, Ryan Rong, YuCheng Yuan, Prerit Choudhary, Shannon Xiao, Rasool Fakoor, Adith Swaminathan, Ching-An Cheng
First: 2026-03-25T06:49:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T16:57:44+00:00
Comments: 39 pages, 17 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Generative optimization uses large language models (LLMs) to iteratively improve artifacts (such as code, workflows or prompts) using execution feedback. It is a promising approach to building self-improving agents, yet in practice remains brittle: despite active research, only 9% of surveyed agents used any automated optimization. We argue that this brittleness arises because, to set up a learning loop, an engineer must make ``hidden'' design choices: What can the optimizer edit and what is the "right" learning evidence to provide at each update? We investigate three factors that affect most applications: the starting artifact, the credit horizon for execution traces, and batching trials and errors into learning evidence. Through case studies in MLAgentBench, Atari, and BigBench Extra Hard, we find that these design decisions can determine whether generative optimization succeeds, yet they are rarely made explicit in prior work. Different starting artifacts determine which solutions are reachable in MLAgentBench, truncated traces can still improve Atari agents, and larger minibatches do not monotonically improve generalization on BBEH. We conclude that the lack of a simple, universal way to set up learning loops across domains is a major hurdle for productionization and adoption. We provide practical guidance for making these choices.
中文标题/摘要
标题：理解在大型语言模型中迭代生成优化所面临的挑战
生成式优化利用大型语言模型（LLMs）通过执行反馈迭代改进人工制品（如代码、工作流或提示），是构建自改进代理的一种有前景的方法，但在实践中仍显脆弱：尽管有大量研究，仅9%的调查代理使用了任何自动化优化。我们认为，这种脆弱性源于工程师在建立学习循环时必须做出“隐式”的设计选择：优化器可以编辑什么，以及在每次更新时应提供何种“正确”的学习证据。我们研究了影响大多数应用的三个因素：起始人工制品、执行轨迹的信用时间窗，以及将试验错误批量整合为学习证据。通过在MLAgentBench、Atari和BigBench Extra Hard中的案例研究，我们发现这些设计决策可决定生成式优化是否成功，但此前研究中很少明确说明这些决策。不同的起始人工制品决定了MLAgentBench中可达的解决方案，截断的轨迹仍可改进Atari代理，而更大的小批量在BBEH上并不单调提升泛化能力。我们得出结论，缺乏一种简单、通用的方法来跨领域建立学习循环，是生产化和采用的主要障碍。我们提供了实用的指导，以帮助做出这些选择。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates why iterative generative optimization using large language models remains fragile in practice, despite its potential for self-improving agents. The authors argue that brittleness stems from unaddressed design choices—such as what aspects of an artifact the optimizer can modify and what constitutes valid learning evidence. Through case studies in MLAgentBench, Atari, and BigBench Extra Hard, they show that starting artifact quality, credit horizon for execution traces, and batching strategies significantly impact success. Results reveal that different starting points limit reachable solutions, truncated traces can still improve performance, and larger minibatches do not always enhance generalization. The study concludes that the absence of a universal framework for setting up learning loops hinders real-world adoption and offers practical guidance for engineers.
本文探讨了使用大语言模型进行生成式优化为何在实践中脆弱，尽管其在构建自适应智能体方面潜力巨大。作者认为，其脆弱性源于未被明确处理的设计决策，例如优化器可修改哪些部分以及如何定义有效的学习证据。通过在MLAgentBench、Atari和BigBench Extra Hard上的案例研究，发现起始artifact质量、执行轨迹的信用时间窗和批处理策略显著影响优化效果。结果表明，不同起始点决定可到达的解空间，截断轨迹仍可提升性能，而更大的批处理并不总能改善泛化能力。研究指出，缺乏跨领域的通用学习循环设置框架是实际应用的主要障碍。
SIA: Self Improving AI with Harness & Weight Updates
Authors: Prannay Hebbar, Yogendra Manawat, Samuel Verboomen, Alesia Ivanova, Selvam Palanimalai, Kunal Bhatia, Vignesh Baskaran
First: 2026-05-26T16:55:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T16:55:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Humans are the bottleneck in building and improving AI. Both the models and the agents that wrap them are written, tuned, and corrected by people. The long-horizon goal of an AI that can figure out how to improve itself remains open. Two largely disjoint research lines attack this bottleneck. The harness-update school has a meta-agent rewrite the scaffold of a task-specific agent (its tools, prompts, retry logic, and search procedure) while the model weights are held fixed. The test-time training school uses hand-written RL pipelines to update the model's own weights on task feedback while the harness is held fixed. These two silos operate in isolation. We propose SIA, a self-improving loop in which a language-model agent (the Feedback-Agent) updates both the harness and the weights of a task-specific agent. We evaluate across three contrasting domains: Chinese legal charge classification, low-level GPU kernel optimisation, and single-cell RNA denoising. Combining both levers outperforms scaffold iteration alone on all three benchmarks. The gains are 56.6% on LawBench, 91.9% runtime reduction on GPU kernels, and 502% on denoising over the initial baseline. Harness updates make the model agentic, shaping how it searches and acts, while weight updates build the domain intuition that no prompt or scaffold can instil.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SIA：通过约束与权重更新实现自我改进的人工智能
人类是构建和改进人工智能的瓶颈。无论是模型本身还是包裹它们的代理，均由人类编写、调优和修正。人工智能实现自我改进的长期目标仍未解决。两大主要研究方向分别针对这一瓶颈：约束更新学派通过元代理重写特定任务代理的框架（其工具、提示、重试逻辑和搜索过程），同时保持模型权重不变；测试时训练学派则使用人工编写的强化学习管道，在任务反馈下更新模型自身权重，同时保持约束不变。这两类方法目前各自独立运作。我们提出SIA，一种语言模型代理（反馈代理）同时更新特定任务代理的约束与权重的自我改进循环。我们在三个不同领域进行评估：中文法律指控分类、低级GPU内核优化和单细胞RNA去噪。结合两种方法在所有基准测试中均优于仅使用框架迭代的方法。性能提升分别为：LawBench上提升56.6%，GPU内核运行时间减少91.9%，去噪性能提升502%。约束更新使模型具备代理能力，塑造其搜索与行动方式；权重更新则构建领域直觉，这是任何提示或框架都无法赋予的。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the human bottleneck in AI development, this paper proposes SIA—a self-improving AI system that jointly updates both the task-specific agent’s harness (tools, prompts, search logic) and its model weights using a feedback-agent. Unlike prior approaches that isolate harness or weight updates, SIA integrates both mechanisms in a unified loop. Evaluated across three domains—Chinese legal classification, GPU kernel optimization, and single-cell RNA denoising—SIA outperforms either method alone: achieving 56.6% improvement on LawBench, 91.9% runtime reduction in GPU kernels, and 502% performance gain in denoising over initial baselines. The results show that harness updates enhance agency and search behavior, while weight updates build domain-specific intuition.
为解决AI开发中的人类瓶颈问题，本文提出SIA系统，通过语言模型代理（Feedback-Agent）同时更新任务特定的框架（harness）和模型权重。该方法结合了框架更新与权重更新两种策略，使模型既能优化行为策略，又能增强领域知识。在法律分类、GPU内核优化和单细胞RNA去噪三个任务上，实验结果显著优于单一方法：法律分类提升56.6%，GPU内核运行时间减少91.9%，去噪效果提升502%，验证了联合优化的有效性。
MetaGraph: A Large-Scale Meta-Analysis of GenAI in Financial NLP (2022-2025)
Authors: Paolo Pedinotti, Peter Baumann, Nathan Jessurun, Leslie Barrett, Enrico Santus
Venue: ACL
First: 2025-09-11T15:37:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T16:53:51+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, appendices, GEM, ACL
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Financial NLP has evolved rapidly since late 2022, outpacing narrative surveys. We introduce MetaGraph, a methodology for extracting typed knowledge graphs from scientific corpora using ontology-guided LLM extraction to enable structured, large-scale trend analysis. Applied to 681 papers on GenAI in Finance (2022-2025), MetaGraph reveals three phases: early LLM-driven expansion of tasks and datasets, growing emphasis on limitations and risk, and a shift toward modular, system-oriented methods (e.g., retrieval-augmented designs). We release the resulting resource and artifacts to support reproducible meta-analysis and future monitoring of the field.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MetaGraph：2022-2025年金融NLP中生成式AI的大型元分析
自2022年末以来，金融NLP领域发展迅速，已超越叙事性综述。我们提出了MetaGraph方法，利用本体引导的大语言模型从科学文献中提取类型化知识图谱，以支持结构化、大规模趋势分析。该方法应用于2022-2025年间681篇关于金融领域生成式AI的论文，揭示了三个阶段：早期由大语言模型驱动的任务与数据集扩展、对局限性与风险的关注日益增加，以及向模块化、系统导向方法（如检索增强设计）的转变。我们发布相关资源与成果，以支持可复现的元分析及未来对该领域的持续监测。

Radar-Camera BEV Multi-Task Learning with Cross-Task Attention Bridge for Joint 3D Detection and Segmentation
Authors: Ahmet İnanç, Özgür Erkent
Venue: ICRA
First: 2026-04-14T16:00:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T16:51:17+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 5 figures, 3 Tables, Accepted at Radar in Robotics: New Frontiers workshop, at IEEE International Conference on Robotics & Automation (ICRA), 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Bird's-eye-view (BEV) representations are the dominant paradigm for 3D perception in autonomous driving, providing a unified spatial canvas where detection and segmentation features are geometrically registered to the same physical coordinate system. However, existing radar-camera fusion methods treat these tasks in isolation, missing the opportunity for cross-task feature sharing: object-level geometric cues from detection can sharpen segmentation, while dense road-layout context from segmentation can anchor detection. We propose \textbf{CTAB} (Cross-Task Attention Bridge), a bidirectional module that exchanges features between detection and segmentation branches via multi-scale deformable attention in shared BEV space. CTAB is integrated into a multi-task framework with an Instance Normalization-based segmentation decoder and learnable BEV upsampling to provide a more detailed BEV representation. On nuScenes, CTAB improves segmentation on 7 classes over the joint multi-task baseline at essentially neutral detection. On a 4-class subset (drivable area, pedestrian crossing, walkway, vehicle), our joint multi-task model achieves 51.0 mIoU-4 while simultaneously providing competitive 3D detection.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于跨任务注意力桥接的雷达-相机BEV多任务学习用于联合3D检测与分割
鸟瞰图（BEV）表示是自动驾驶中3D感知的主流范式，提供统一的空间画布，使检测与分割特征在相同物理坐标系中几何对齐。然而，现有雷达-相机融合方法将这些任务孤立处理，错失了跨任务特征共享的机会：检测提供的物体级几何线索可增强分割，而分割提供的密集道路布局上下文可锚定检测。我们提出\textbf{CTAB}（跨任务注意力桥接），一种双向模块，通过共享BEV空间中的多尺度可变形注意力在检测与分割分支间交换特征。CTAB集成于多任务框架中，结合基于实例归一化的分割解码器和可学习的BEV上采样，以提供更精细的BEV表示。在nuScenes数据集上，CTAB在保持检测性能基本不变的前提下，使7类分割性能优于联合多任务基线。在4类子集（可行驶区域、人行横道、人行道、车辆）上，我们的联合多任务模型实现51.0 mIoU-4，同时提供具有竞争力的3D检测性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to better integrate 3D detection and segmentation in autonomous driving, this paper proposes CTAB, a bidirectional cross-task attention module that enables feature exchange between detection and segmentation branches in a shared BEV space using multi-scale deformable attention. Integrated into a multi-task framework with instance normalization and learnable BEV upsampling, CTAB enhances feature sharing: detection geometry refines segmentation, while segmentation context anchors detection. Experiments on nuScenes show CTAB improves segmentation mIoU by 7 classes without harming detection performance, and on a 4-class subset achieves 51.0 mIoU-4 while maintaining competitive 3D detection accuracy.
为提升自动驾驶中雷达-相机融合下的3D检测与分割联合性能，本文提出CTAB模块，通过多尺度可变形注意力在共享BEV空间中双向交换检测与分割分支特征。该模块集成于多任务框架，结合实例归一化与可学习BEV上采样，实现检测几何信息增强分割、分割语义引导检测的协同优化。在nuScenes数据集上，CTAB在不降低检测性能的前提下使7类分割mIoU优于联合基线，4类子集（可行驶区域、人行横道、人行道、车辆）达到51.0 mIoU-4的分割精度并保持检测竞争力。
Stop Listening to Me! How Multi-turn Conversations Can Degrade LLM Reliability
Authors: Kevin H. Guo, Chao Yan, Avinash Baidya, Katherine Brown, Xiang Gao, Juming Xiong, Zhijun Yin, Bradley A. Malin
First: 2026-03-12T00:14:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T16:50:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) excel on static benchmarks, but their performance across multi-turn conversations, which better reflect real-world usage, remains understudied. Addressing this gap is critical in high-stakes settings like healthcare, where patients and clinicians are turning to LLM chatbots to address their medical inquiries. Here, we introduce the "stick-or-switch" (SoS) framework, which partitions a question-answer space into multiple sequential presentations to model two safety-centric behaviors: conviction (i.e., sticking to a correct answer selection or abstention against incorrect suggestions) and flexibility (i.e., switching to a correct suggestion when it is introduced). Evaluating 17 LLMs across three clinical benchmarks, we observe a pervasive conversation tax, where partitioning an answer-space into sequential presentations reduces end-to-end accuracy and abstention against incorrect suggestions by an average of up to 30%, reaching 65% in certain models. We also observe blind switching, where models transition an initial abstention to incorrect and correct suggestions at near-identical rates reaching 50%. Finally, we show that increasing model scale mitigates some of these conversational inefficacies while exacerbating others, such as a higher propensity to adopt an incorrect suggestion from an initial abstention. Together our findings demonstrate that the general proficiency captured by static benchmarks do not translate over multi-turn dialogues.
中文标题/摘要
标题：别听我的！多轮对话如何降低大语言模型的可靠性
大语言模型（LLMs）在静态基准测试中表现优异，但在更贴近真实场景的多轮对话中的表现却鲜有研究。在医疗等高风险领域，患者和临床医生正依赖LLM聊天机器人解答医疗问题，填补这一研究空白至关重要。本文提出“坚持或切换”（SoS）框架，将问答空间划分为多个顺序呈现部分，以建模两种安全导向行为：坚持（即坚持正确答案或拒绝错误建议）和灵活切换（即当正确建议出现时切换至该建议）。在三个临床基准上评估17个LLM模型，我们发现普遍存在“对话税”现象：将答案空间划分为顺序呈现会降低端到端准确率和拒绝错误建议的能力，平均降幅达30%，某些模型高达65%。我们还观察到“盲目切换”现象：模型从初始拒绝状态切换至错误和正确建议的概率几乎相同，高达50%。最后，我们发现增大模型规模虽能缓解部分对话效率问题，却会加剧其他问题，如更倾向于从初始拒绝状态采纳错误建议。综合来看，静态基准所体现的通用能力无法直接迁移到多轮对话中。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how multi-turn conversations degrade the reliability of large language models (LLMs), especially in high-stakes domains like healthcare. The authors introduce the "stick-or-switch" (SoS) framework to model two safety behaviors—conviction (sticking to correct answers or abstaining) and flexibility (switching to correct suggestions)—by sequentially presenting answer options. Evaluating 17 LLMs across three clinical benchmarks, they find a "conversation tax": sequential presentation reduces accuracy and abstention by up to 30% on average, with some models dropping 65%. They also observe "blind switching," where models switch from abstention to both correct and incorrect suggestions at nearly equal rates (up to 50%). While larger models show some improvement in reliability, they also exhibit increased susceptibility to adopting incorrect suggestions after initial abstention, indicating that static benchmark performance does not reliably transfer to conversational settings.
该研究探讨了多轮对话如何降低大语言模型（LLM）在高风险领域（如医疗）中的可靠性。作者提出了“坚持或切换”（SoS）框架，通过分步呈现答案选项，模拟模型的两种安全行为——坚持（坚持正确答案或拒绝错误建议）和灵活切换（当正确建议出现时切换）。在三个临床基准上评估17个LLM模型，发现存在“对话税”：准确率和拒绝错误建议的比例平均下降30%，部分模型下降达65%。同时观察到“盲目切换”现象，即模型从拒绝状态切换到正确或错误建议的概率几乎相等（最高达50%）。虽然更大规模模型部分缓解了这些问题，但也加剧了从拒绝状态采纳错误建议的倾向，表明静态基准表现无法可靠迁移到对话场景。
Transfer Learning using 66 Diseases for Disease Forecasting Applications
Authors: Lauren J Beesley, Alexander C Murph, Dave Osthus, Lauren A Castro
First: 2026-05-26T16:45:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T16:45:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Disease forecasting models typically rely on a single data stream, making models brittle when histories are short or noisy. Recent top-performing models have shown that synthesizing multiple reporting systems for the same disease improves performance. Other recent work takes this idea a step further, using transfer learning to train a forecasting model for one disease using data from a different disease. We expand upon each of these approaches greatly, training machine learning models on data that span 66 infectious diseases and several data streams. We investigate the value of incorporating different data streams for forecasting 20 different disease data streams. We find that incorporating other data streams improves forecasting in the vast majority (84.9%) of time series and model structures considered. However, our work highlights that the quality of the added data matters, where adding data extremely different from the target data stream can sometimes degrade forecast performance. A major contribution of this work is in compiling a publicly-available database of data for use by the infectious disease forecasting community.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用66种疾病进行转移学习以应用于疾病预测

为解决单一数据流导致的疾病预测模型脆弱性问题，本研究通过跨66种传染病的迁移学习提升预测鲁棒性，训练模型使用多源数据并评估其对20种目标疾病时间序列的预测效果。结果显示，84.9%的情况下引入其他数据流可提升预测性能，但若添加的数据与目标数据差异过大，反而可能降低性能。研究同时贡献了一个公开可用的传染病数据集，供预测社区使用。
Lost in Sampling: Assessing Lexical Reachability in LLMs via the Word Coverage Score (WCS)
Authors: Samer Awad, Javier Conde, Carlos Arriaga, Tairan Fu, Javier Coronado-Blázquez, Pedro Reviriego
First: 2026-05-26T16:44:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T16:44:25+00:00
Comments: 15 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern Large Language Models (LLMs) are often criticized for producing repetitive and homogeneous text, despite possessing vast latent vocabularies. While previous research has focused on model knowledge and training data, we investigate the role of decoding mechanics in suppressing linguistic diversity. We introduce the Word Coverage Score (WCS), a metric that quantifies the extent to which contextually appropriate human vocabulary is mathematically pruned by standard sampling filters (e.g., Top-$p$, Top-$k$, and Min-$p$). Rather than assessing static knowledge, the WCS measures the lexical survival rate of low-frequency, high-information human words as a function of sampling parameters. By auditing open-weight models on human-authored corpus fragments, we identify which logical lexical choices are rendered unreachable by the decoder, even when they reside within the probability space. Our results provide quantitative evidence that industry-standard sampling defaults act as unintended censorship mechanisms, smoothing the unique textures of human expression into a homogenized discourse. The WCS offers a rigorous framework for optimizing the trade-off between text coherence and lexical richness, providing a diagnostic tool for preserving the diversity of human language in generative models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：迷失于采样之中：通过词覆盖得分（WCS）评估大语言模型中的词汇可达性
尽管现代大语言模型（LLMs）拥有庞大的潜在词汇库，但仍常因生成重复且同质化的文本而受到批评。以往研究多关注模型知识与训练数据，而我们则探究解码机制如何抑制语言多样性。我们引入词覆盖得分（WCS），该指标量化了标准采样过滤器（如Top-p、Top-k和Min-p）如何数学上剔除语境合适的高频人类词汇。WCS不评估静态知识，而是衡量低频高信息量人类词汇在不同采样参数下的词汇存活率。通过对开源模型在人类作者语料片段上的审计，我们识别出即使这些词汇存在于概率空间内，解码器仍使其不可达的逻辑词汇选择。我们的结果提供了定量证据，表明业界标准采样默认设置充当了无意的审查机制，将人类表达的独特性平滑为同质化话语。WCS为优化文本连贯性与词汇丰富度之间的权衡提供了严谨框架，并为在生成模型中保持人类语言多样性提供诊断工具。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that large language models produce repetitive text despite having large vocabularies, this study investigates how decoding mechanisms suppress linguistic diversity. It introduces the Word Coverage Score (WCS), a metric that measures how many low-frequency, contextually appropriate human words survive standard sampling filters like Top-p and Top-k. By auditing open-weight models on human-authored text fragments, the study finds that common sampling defaults systematically exclude diverse lexical choices even when they are probabilistically available, effectively acting as censorship mechanisms. The WCS quantifies this suppression and provides a tool to optimize between coherence and lexical richness in model outputs.
鉴于大语言模型虽拥有庞大词汇量却常输出重复性文本，本研究探讨解码机制如何抑制语言多样性。提出词覆盖分数（WCS）这一指标，用于衡量低频但语境恰当的人类词汇在Top-p、Top-k等标准采样策略下被保留的比例。通过对开源模型在人类写作片段上的审计发现，标准采样默认设置会系统性排除多样化的词汇选择，即使这些词在概率空间中存在。结果表明，这些默认设置实质上构成无意审查，牺牲语言多样性以换取连贯性，WCS可作为优化生成模型词汇丰富度与流畅性平衡的诊断工具。
Atari Games Challenge: A Pilot Study on Multimodal Player Experience Assessment
Authors: Oleg Jarma Montoya, Erica Manca, Thomas Vase Schultz Volden, Paolo Burelli
First: 2026-05-26T16:39:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T16:39:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present a pilot study on the collection and synchronisation of multimodal data for player experience investigation. We collected game telemetry, self-reported surveys, biometrics, and cued-retrospective think-aloud (C-RTA) data from 19 participants playing three Atari 2600 games. The study then uses the data to investigate difficulty in PX, showcasing a protocol for future multimodal research. The dataset obtained from the experiment, which is publicly available, shows potential as a rich, transformative source that can be used to investigate dynamic difficulty adjustment algorithms, game balancing strategies or broader explorations of games user research. The study findings suggest that the experimental approach holds strong potential for generalisation in future player experience studies.
