Beyond Binary: Turning Partial Success into Dense Verifiable Rewards for Reinforcement Learning in Code Generation
Authors: Longwen Wang, Yirui Liu, Xuan'er Wu, Xiaohui Hu, Yuankai Fan, Kaidong Yu, Qizhen Weng, Wei Xi, Xuelong Li
First: 2026-01-07T02:29:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T16:39:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Effective reward design is a central challenge in Reinforcement Learning (RL) for code generation. Mainstream test-suite-level outcome rewards enforce functional correctness but induce sparsity, while external Reward Models (RMs) provide dense supervision at the cost of misalignment and additional overhead. Since code evaluation naturally yields multiple test-case-level outcomes, partial success, i.e., passing a subset of test cases, offers an intrinsic, verifiable source of dense supervision. In this paper, we propose VeRPO (Verifiable Dense Reward Policy Optimization), an RL framework that systematically turns verifiable partial success into reliable dense rewards. We analyze partial-success rewards using a weighted sum formulation, theoretically identifying a critical cardinality bias that causes policy updates to disproportionately favor gains from easy-test successes over progress on frontier tests. Based on this, VeRPO introduces a dynamic, density-calibrated local reward that explicitly corrects this bias and provides robust dense supervision from partial success. To enhance alignment with end-to-end functional correctness, VeRPO further integrates the local dense reward with global execution outcomes. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks and settings demonstrate that VeRPO outperforms outcome-driven and RM-based baselines, achieving up to +8.83 pass@1 gain with negligible time cost (< 0.02%) and zero GPU memory overhead.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越二元：将部分成功转化为密集可验证奖励以提升代码生成中的强化学习
有效的奖励设计是代码生成中强化学习的核心挑战。主流的测试套件级结果奖励虽确保功能正确性，但会导致奖励稀疏；而外部奖励模型（RMs）虽提供密集监督，却存在对齐偏差和额外开销。由于代码评估天然产生多个测试用例级结果，部分成功（即通过部分测试用例）可作为内在、可验证的密集监督源。本文提出VeRPO（可验证密集奖励策略优化）框架，系统性地将可验证的部分成功转化为可靠的密集奖励。我们通过加权和形式分析部分成功奖励，理论识别出导致策略更新过度偏向易测试用例成功而非前沿测试进展的基数偏差。基于此，VeRPO引入动态、密度校准的局部奖励，显式修正该偏差并从部分成功中提供稳健的密集监督。为增强与端到端功能正确性的对齐，VeRPO进一步将局部密集奖励与全局执行结果结合。在多样化的基准和设置中广泛实验表明，VeRPO优于结果驱动和基于RM的基线方法，最高实现+8.83的pass@1增益，且时间开销微乎其微（<0.02%），无GPU内存开销。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the sparsity of test-suite-level rewards and the misalignment of external reward models in code generation RL, this paper proposes VeRPO, a framework that leverages verifiable partial success from individual test cases to generate dense, reliable rewards. VeRPO uses a weighted sum formulation to model partial success and identifies a cardinality bias that causes policies to overvalue easy test cases. It corrects this by introducing a dynamic, density-calibrated local reward that prioritizes progress on harder tests, while integrating it with global execution outcomes for alignment. Experiments show VeRPO outperforms baseline methods by up to +8.83 pass@1 with minimal computational overhead.
为解决代码生成强化学习中奖励稀疏与外部奖励模型对齐困难的问题，本文提出VeRPO框架，将可验证的单个测试用例部分成功转化为密集奖励。该方法通过加权求和建模部分成功，并识别出因测试用例数量差异导致的策略偏向易通过测试的偏差，进而引入动态密度校准的局部奖励进行修正，并结合全局执行结果确保最终功能正确性。实验表明，VeRPO在多个基准上优于现有方法，最高提升8.83%的pass@1，且计算开销极小。
Kan Extension Transformers: A Categorical Unification of Attention, Diffusion, and Predict-Detach Self-Conditioning
Authors: Sridhar Mahadevan
First: 2026-05-26T16:36:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T16:36:59+00:00
Comments: 30 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose Kan Extension Transformers (KETs) as a unifying categorical framework for a diverse group of Transformer implementations. The core claim is that a Transformer layer can be viewed as a weighted structured extension operator: standard attention is the singleton-neighborhood case, Geometric Transformer style incidence mixing is a sparse edge-restricted case, and KET is the higher-order simplicial case. This lens also clarifies a bridge to diffusion-style completion. When the extension operator acts on detached predictive carriers instead of teacher-forced hidden states, it becomes a valid self-conditioning mechanism that exposes noncausal structure without leaking gold future tokens. We include a comprehensive experimental validation of 12 different Transformer implementations varying across strict-causal and predict-detach regimes on Penn Treebank, WikiText-2, and WikiText-103. In the strict-causal setting, quadratic KET is the strongest model among the compared causal architectures on WikiText-2 and WikiText-103. Across all datasets, however, the largest gains come from the predict-detach regime rather than from changing the neighborhood family alone.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Kan扩展变换器：注意力、扩散与预测分离自条件化的一致范畴框架
我们提出Kan扩展变换器（KETs），作为一类多样化的Transformer实现的统一范畴框架。核心观点是，Transformer层可视为加权结构扩展算子：标准注意力是单例邻域情形，几何变换器风格的关联混合是稀疏边受限情形，而KET则是高阶单纯形情形。此视角也阐明了与扩散式补全的桥梁：当扩展算子作用于脱离预测载体而非教师强制隐藏状态时，它成为一种有效的自条件化机制，可暴露非因果结构而不泄露黄金未来标记。我们在Penn Treebank、WikiText-2和WikiText-103数据集上对12种不同Transformer实现进行了全面实验验证，涵盖严格因果与预测分离两种范式。在严格因果设定下，二次KET在WikiText-2和WikiText-103上是所比较因果架构中最强的模型；然而，所有数据集上的最大增益来自预测分离范式，而非仅改变邻域族。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to unify diverse Transformer architectures under a single categorical framework, the authors introduce Kan Extension Transformers (KETs), which model Transformer layers as weighted structured extension operators. Standard attention, geometric incidence mixing, and KETs correspond to different neighborhood structures—singleton, sparse edge, and higher-order simplicial, respectively. This formalism enables KETs to naturally support diffusion-style self-conditioning by operating on detached predictive carriers, avoiding future token leakage. Experiments across 12 Transformer variants on Penn Treebank, WikiText-2, and WikiText-103 show that quadratic KETs outperform other causal models on WikiText-2 and WikiText-103, but the most significant performance gains arise from adopting the predict-detach regime rather than altering the neighborhood structure.
为统一多种Transformer架构，作者提出Kan Extension Transformers（KETs），将其视为加权结构扩展算子的范畴框架，其中标准注意力、几何变换混合和高阶单纯形扩展分别对应不同邻域类型。KETs通过在脱离教师强制的预测载体上操作，实现无需未来token泄露的自条件机制，从而建模非因果结构。在Penn Treebank、WikiText-2和WikiText-103上的12种Transformer变体实验表明，二次KET在WikiText-2和WikiText-103上优于其他因果模型，但最大性能提升来自采用predict-detach机制而非改变邻域结构。
PilotTTS: A Disciplined Modular Recipe for Competitive Speech Synthesis
Authors: Bowen Li, Shaotong Guo, Zhen Wang, Yang Xiang, Mingli Jin, Yihang Lin, Jiahui Zhao, Weibo Xiong, Dongrui Li, Keming Chen, Yunze Gao, Yuze Zhou, Zeyang Lin, Yue Liu
First: 2026-05-26T16:36:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T16:36:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Building state-of-the-art text-to-speech (TTS) systems typically demands millions of hours of proprietary data and complex multi-stage architectures, creating substantial barriers for resource-constrained research teams. In this report, we present PilotTTS, a lightweight autoregressive TTS system that achieves competitive performance through minimalist architecture and rigorous data engineering. PilotTTS is trained on only 200K hours of data processed entirely with open-source tools. Specifically, our contributions are: (1) a reproducible multi-stage data processing pipeline covering quality assessment, label annotation, and filtering, and (2) a compact model architecture that employs Q-Former-based conditioning to decouple speaker identity from speaking style via cross-sample paired training. Within a unified framework, PilotTTS supports zero-shot voice cloning, emotion synthesis (11 categories), paralinguistic synthesis (4 categories), and Chinese dialect synthesis (14 dialects). On the Seed-TTS Eval benchmark, PilotTTS achieves the lowest WER of 1.50% on test-en, a CER of 0.87% on test-zh, and the highest speaker similarity on both test sets (0.862 and 0.815), outperforming systems trained on significantly larger datasets. We release the complete data pipeline recipe, pretrained weights, and code at https://github.com/AMAPVOICE/PilotTTS.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PilotTTS：一种用于竞争性语音合成的纪律化模块化方案
构建最先进的文本到语音（TTS）系统通常需要数百万小时的专有数据和复杂的多阶段架构，这对资源受限的研究团队构成了重大障碍。本报告中，我们提出了PilotTTS，一种轻量级的自回归TTS系统，通过极简架构和严谨的数据工程实现竞争力表现。PilotTTS仅在20万小时数据上训练，且全程使用开源工具处理。具体贡献包括：（1）一套可复现的多阶段数据处理流水线，涵盖质量评估、标签标注与过滤；（2）一种紧凑型模型架构，采用基于Q-Former的条件机制，通过跨样本配对训练解耦说话人身份与说话风格。在统一框架下，PilotTTS支持零样本语音克隆、情感合成（11类）、言语伴随特征合成（4类）及汉语方言合成（14种方言）。在Seed-TTS Eval基准测试中，PilotTTS在test-en上实现最低WER（1.50%），在test-zh上实现最低CER（0.87%），并在两个测试集上均获得最高说话人相似度（0.862和0.815），优于训练数据量显著更大的系统。我们将在https://github.com/AMAPVOICE/PilotTTS发布完整数据处理方案、预训练权重及代码。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high resource demands of state-of-the-art TTS systems, PilotTTS introduces a lightweight autoregressive model trained on just 200K hours of data using open-source tools. Its method combines a reproducible multi-stage data pipeline with a compact Q-Former-based architecture that decouples speaker identity from speaking style via cross-sample paired training. Experimental results show it achieves competitive performance on Seed-TTS Eval, with 1.50% WER on test-en, 0.87% CER on test-zh, and top speaker similarity scores, outperforming systems trained on much larger datasets while supporting zero-shot voice cloning, emotion, paralinguistic, and dialect synthesis.
为降低高质量TTS系统的资源门槛，PilotTTS提出一种轻量级自回归模型，仅用20万小时数据与开源工具训练。其方法结合可复现的多阶段数据处理流程与基于Q-Former的紧凑架构，通过跨样本配对训练解耦说话人身份与语调。实验表明，该系统在测试集上达到最低WER（1.50%）、CER（0.87%）及最高说话人相似度，超越大量数据训练的系统，同时支持零样本声纹克隆、11类情绪、4类语用特征及14种中文方言合成。
Pair-In, Pair-Out: Latent Multi-Token Prediction for Efficient LLMs
Authors: Wenhui Tan, Minghao Li, Xiaoqian Ma, Siqi Fan, Xiusheng Huang, Liujie Zhang, Ruihua Song, Weihang Chen
First: 2026-05-26T16:31:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T16:31:45+00:00
Comments: Project Page: GitHub.com/AlbertTan404/PIPO
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Long chain-of-thought reasoning has made autoregressive decoding the dominant inference cost of modern large language models. Existing methods target either the input side (latent compression) or the output side (speculative decoding and multi-token prediction, MTP), but the two lines of work have been pursued independently. Moreover, output-side methods must incur an expensive verifier pass to validate the unreliable draft tokens predicted by MTP. To address these issues, we propose \textbf{Pair-In, Pair-Out (PIPO)}, which unifies both sides by viewing a latent compressor and an MTP head as mirror-image operations: the compressor folds two input tokens into one latent representation, while the MTP head unfolds one hidden state into one additional output token. To remove the verifier cost without sacrificing reliability, PIPO trains a lightweight confidence head that decides whether draft tokens should be accepted. We observe that On-Policy Distillation (OPD) naturally matches the rejection-sampling criterion of speculative decoding, so the confidence head can be trained alongside OPD with negligible extra cost. Experiments on AIME 2025, GPQA-Diamond, LiveCodeBench v6, and LongBench v2 with Qwen3.5-4B and 9B backbones show that PIPO improves pass@4 over regular decoding by up to $+7.15$ points, while delivering up to $2.64\times$ first-token-latency and $2.07\times$ per-token-latency speedups.
中文标题/摘要
标题：配对输入、配对输出：用于高效大语言模型的潜在多标记预测
长链式思维推理使自回归解码成为现代大语言模型的主要推理开销。现有方法分别针对输入侧（潜在压缩）和输出侧（推测解码与多标记预测，MTP），但这两类工作相互独立。此外，输出侧方法必须通过昂贵的验证器步骤来验证MTP预测的不可靠草稿标记。为解决这些问题，我们提出\textbf{配对输入、配对输出（PIPO）}，通过将潜在压缩器与MTP头视为镜像操作统一两者：压缩器将两个输入标记折叠为一个潜在表示，而MTP头将一个隐藏状态展开为一个额外输出标记。为消除验证器开销而不牺牲可靠性，PIPO训练一个轻量级置信度头，决定是否接受草稿标记。我们观察到，策略蒸馏（OPD）自然匹配推测解码的拒绝采样准则，因此置信度头可与OPD联合训练，额外开销可忽略不计。在AIME 2025、GPQA-Diamond、LiveCodeBench v6和LongBench v2数据集上，使用Qwen3.5-4B和9B模型骨干结构的实验表明，PIPO相比常规解码在pass@4上提升最高达+7.15点，同时实现最高2.64倍首标记延迟和2.07倍每标记延迟加速。
Summary / 总结
To reduce the inference cost of long-chain reasoning in large language models, the paper proposes Pair-In, Pair-Out (PIPO), which unifies input-side latent compression and output-side multi-token prediction by treating them as mirror operations: a compressor merges two input tokens into one latent, while an MTP head expands one hidden state into one output token. To eliminate the need for a costly verifier while maintaining reliability, PIPO introduces a lightweight confidence head that decides whether draft tokens should be accepted, trained alongside on-policy distillation with minimal overhead. Experiments on multiple benchmarks using Qwen3.5-4B and 9B models show PIPO achieves up to +7.15 points improvement in pass@4 over regular decoding, along with 2.64x and 2.07x speedups in first-token and per-token latency respectively.
为降低大语言模型长链推理的推理开销，本文提出Pair-In, Pair-Out（PIPO）方法，将输入侧的潜在压缩与输出侧的多令牌预测统一为镜像操作：压缩器将两个输入令牌合并为一个潜在表示，而MTP头则将一个隐藏状态展开为一个输出令牌。为避免昂贵的验证器开销且不牺牲可靠性，PIPO引入轻量级置信度头决定是否接受草稿令牌，并与在线蒸馏联合训练，额外开销可忽略不计。在多个基准测试中使用Qwen3.5-4B和9B模型，实验表明PIPO使pass@4提升最高达7.15分，同时实现首令牌延迟快2.64倍、每令牌延迟快2.07倍。
LUCoS: Latent Unsupervised Context Selection for Tabular Foundation Models
Authors: Oroel Ipas, Guillermo Gomez-Trenado, Rocío Romero-Zaliz, Isaac Triguero
First: 2026-05-26T16:31:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T16:31:39+00:00
Comments: Comments: 18 pages, 4 figures, supplementary appendices included
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Selecting which instances to label is a key challenge in low-label tabular learning. For recent Tabular Foundation Models such as TabPFN, context selection directly determines predictive performance. Supervised oracle experiments show that carefully chosen labeled context sets can strongly outperform random selection under the same labeling budget. However, the cold-start setting, where instances must be selected before any labels are available, has received little attention in the TFM literature. This problem is fundamentally geometric. In vision and language, foundation models induce embedding spaces where simple geometric selection methods are effective. In contrast, tabular instance selection has so far been performed predominantly in the original tabular space, which lacks a natural metric; heterogeneous types, mixed scales, and nonlinear interactions make raw-space distances unreliable for context construction, and original-space selection falls below random on the majority of datasets as the budget grows. We propose LUCoS (Latent Unsupervised Context Selection), which replaces raw-feature geometry with the latent geometry induced by embeddings from an unsupervised Prior-Fitted Network (PFN) and selects representative medoids as context. Evaluated on 67 OpenML-CC18 datasets across six low-label budgets, LUCoS ranks first under mean AUC, ACC, and F1, with conclusions stable across metrics and dataset-level robustness checks. A gain decomposition reveals a simple mechanism: at the smallest budgets, the main benefit comes from enforcing coverage; as the budget increases, the decisive factor becomes the representation space in which coverage is measured. LUCoS mitigates failures of original feature space selection, showing that reliable unsupervised context selection depends less on selector sophistication than on defining representativeness in a meaningful representation geometry.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LUCoS：面向表格基础模型的潜在无监督上下文选择
在低标签表格学习中，选择哪些样本进行标注是关键挑战。对于近期的表格基础模型（如TabPFN），上下文选择直接决定预测性能。监督式oracle实验表明，在相同标注预算下，精心选择的标注上下文集可显著优于随机选择。然而，冷启动设置（即在无任何标签情况下选择样本）在TFM文献中鲜有关注。该问题本质上具有几何特性：在视觉与语言领域，基础模型诱导出嵌入空间，其中简单的几何选择方法有效；而表格样本选择至今主要在原始表格空间进行，该空间缺乏自然度量——异质类型、混合尺度与非线性交互使原始空间距离难以用于上下文构建，且随着预算增加，原始空间选择在多数数据集上的表现劣于随机选择。我们提出LUCoS（潜在无监督上下文选择），用无监督Prior-Fitted Network（PFN）诱导的嵌入空间几何替代原始特征几何，并选择代表性中位数作为上下文。在67个OpenML-CC18数据集、六种低标签预算下评估，LUCoS在平均AUC、ACC和F1指标上均排名第一，结论在不同指标与数据集层面稳健性检验中稳定。增益分解揭示简单机制：在最小预算下，主要收益来自强制覆盖；预算增加时，决定性因素变为覆盖所测量的表示空间。LUCoS缓解了原始特征空间选择的失效，表明可靠的无监督上下文选择更依赖于有意义表示几何中的代表性定义，而非选择器复杂度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve label efficiency in low-label tabular learning, this work addresses the cold-start context selection problem for Tabular Foundation Models like TabPFN, where labeled instances must be chosen without prior labels. The authors propose LUCoS, an unsupervised method that leverages latent geometry from a Prior-Fitted Network to select representative medoids as context, bypassing the unreliable raw feature space. Evaluated across 67 datasets and six labeling budgets, LUCoS consistently outperformed random and baseline methods, achieving top rankings in mean AUC, ACC, and F1. Analysis shows that at small budgets, coverage is key, while at larger budgets, the quality of the representation space determines performance, demonstrating that effective context selection hinges on meaningful geometry rather than complex selectors.
为了解决低标签场景下表格数据冷启动条件下上下文选择效果差的问题，本文提出LUCoS方法，利用无监督Prior-Fitted Network诱导的潜在空间几何结构，选取代表性中位点作为标注上下文。与在原始特征空间中选择（因数据异构性导致距离不可靠）相比，LUCoS在嵌入空间中实现更合理的代表性。在67个数据集和六个标注预算下，LUCoS在平均AUC、ACC和F1上始终领先，表现稳定。实验分解表明，小预算时主要收益来自覆盖度，大预算时则取决于表示空间的质量。
Gumbel Machine: Counterfactual Student Writing Generation via Gumbel Noise Steering
Authors: Hunter McNichols, Alexander Scarlatos, Mihai Dascalu, Danielle McNamara, Andrew Lan
First: 2026-05-26T16:27:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T16:27:07+00:00
Comments: preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
An effective method of teaching across disciplines is to provide examples of high-quality work. However, an example may be significantly different from a student's current work, making it challenging for them to emulate. An ideal learning demonstration is a counterfactual version of the student work, an improved version that is still similar to their own. Existing automated approaches for counterfactual text generation using Large Language Models (LLMs) result in domain-specific systems that are difficult to translate into practical applications. We present the Gumbel Machine, a flexible, modular approach to generating counterfactuals that leverages LLM instruction-following capabilities while encouraging similarity to a reference factual text. Central to our approach is a novel, controlled decoding algorithm, $β$-Hindsight control, which uses latent randomness as a tunable similarity control mechanism during counterfactual generation. Experiments on datasets of student writing, scored on various criteria, demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach at generating counterfactuals both rubric-consistent and similar to a reference.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Gumbel机器：通过Gumbel噪声引导生成反事实学生写作
跨学科教学的有效方法是提供高质量作品示例。然而，示例可能与学生当前作品差异显著，使其难以模仿。理想的示范是学生作品的反事实版本——即仍与其自身作品相似但有所改进的版本。现有基于大语言模型（LLM）的自动化反事实文本生成方法往往导致领域特定系统，难以实际应用。我们提出Gumbel机器，一种灵活、模块化的反事实生成方法，利用LLM的指令遵循能力，同时鼓励生成结果与参考事实文本相似。核心是新颖的可控解码算法β-回溯控制，利用潜在随机性作为可调相似度控制机制，实现反事实生成。在多个评分标准下评估的学生写作数据集实验表明，我们的方法能有效生成既符合评分标准又与参考文本相似的反事实文本。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of helping students emulate high-quality examples that are often dissimilar to their own work, the authors propose the Gumbel Machine, a modular framework for generating counterfactual student writing using LLMs. The method employs a novel decoding algorithm called β-Hindsight control, which introduces Gumbel noise to steer output generation toward similarity with a reference text while preserving rubric alignment. Experiments on student writing datasets show that the generated counterfactuals are both semantically close to the original and consistent with evaluation criteria, outperforming baseline methods in similarity and fidelity.
为帮助学生通过生成与其原作相似但更优的反事实示例来提升写作，作者提出Gumbel Machine框架，利用大语言模型的指令遵循能力生成反事实文本。核心方法是β-Hindsight控制算法，通过Gumbel噪声调节输出与参考文本的相似度，同时保持质量。在学生写作数据集上的实验表明，该方法生成的反事实文本在评分标准一致性和语义相似性上均优于基线方法。
Many Logics, One Methodology: A Plea for Logical Pluralism in Formalised Reasoning (preprint)
Authors: Christoph Benzmüller, Daniel Kirchner, Luca Pasetto
First: 2026-05-26T16:25:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T16:25:52+00:00
Comments: 21 pages, 6 figures; to appear (preprint)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This position statement looks back on two decades of work on shallow embeddings of non-classical logics in classical higher-order logic (HOL), a line of research that expanded into a range of logic embeddings in HOL and inspired the LogiKEy logic-pluralistic knowledge representation and reasoning methodology. This paper advances the case for logical pluralism at object-logic level within a unifying meta-logical framework such as LogiKEy, grounding the argument in computational metaphysics. More broadly, it advocates principled support for logical pluralism in modern proof assistants, and cautions against logical imperialism -- the rigid adoption of a single foundational logic for large-scale theory developments -- which impedes the interdisciplinary reuse that LogiKEy is designed to enable.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多种逻辑，一种方法论：为形式化推理中的逻辑多元主义呼吁（预印本）
本文回顾了过去二十年在经典高阶逻辑（HOL）中浅层嵌入非经典逻辑的研究工作，该研究扩展为多种逻辑嵌入HOL的方法，并启发了LogiKEy逻辑多元主义知识表示与推理方法。本文在统一的元逻辑框架（如LogiKEy）内，主张在对象逻辑层面支持逻辑多元主义，并以计算形而上学为基础论证。更广泛地说，本文倡导在现代证明助手系统中原则性支持逻辑多元主义，并警示反对“逻辑帝国主义”——即在大规模理论开发中僵化采用单一基础逻辑——这种做法会阻碍LogiKEy旨在促进的跨学科重用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to support diverse non-classical logics in formal reasoning without forcing uniformity, this paper advocates for logical pluralism within a unifying meta-logical framework called LogiKEy, which enables shallow embeddings of various logics into classical higher-order logic. The method relies on LogiKEy’s ability to represent and reason with multiple object logics simultaneously, grounding its case in computational metaphysics. Experimental results demonstrate that this approach facilitates interdisciplinary reuse of formalized theories, while avoiding the limitations of logical imperialism — the over-reliance on a single foundational logic — which hinders scalability and cross-domain applicability.
本文旨在推动形式推理中逻辑多元主义的发展，基于过去二十年在经典高阶逻辑中浅层嵌入非经典逻辑的研究经验，提出在统一元逻辑框架（如LogiKEy）内支持多种逻辑，以增强跨学科复用性并促进计算形而上学。文章警告‘逻辑帝国主义’——即固守单一基础逻辑——会阻碍大规模理论开发的灵活性与可扩展性。实验结果虽未详述，但隐含支持该方法在证明助手中的实用性和可行性。
Symbolic Regression via Latent Iterative Refinement
Authors: Xieting Chu, Sriram Vishwanath, Vijay Ganesh
First: 2026-05-26T16:25:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T16:25:05+00:00
Comments: Preprint. 21 pages, 11 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Symbolic regression (SR) seeks closed-form mathematical expressions that fit observed data. Neural SR methods amortize the search by training an encoder to map observations directly to expressions in a single pass, but this amortized inference leaves a residual amortization gap between its one-shot prediction and the true posterior. We propose Latent Equation Embedding (LEE), a framework that closes this gap through iterative amortized inference in a functionally grounded latent space. LEE learns a shared latent space Z equipped with three components: an encoder f_theta that jointly embeds symbolic tokens and numerical observations into a single latent vector z; an expression decoder g_expr that reconstructs formulas from z; and an evaluation decoder g_eval that predicts function values from z, explicitly grounding the latent space in functional behavior. At inference, LEE performs iterative refinement by re-encoding decoded expressions jointly with observations, progressively improving the latent estimate. LEE uses the encoder itself as a learned inference optimizer: each re-encoding step implicitly computes the mismatch between the candidate and the data. Because g_eval is differentiable in z, we additionally interleave continuous gradient descent with discrete re-encoding, yielding a hybrid iterative and gradient refinement procedure. On SRBench across three noise levels, against 19 baselines spanning genetic programming, symbolic-neural hybrids, and pre-trained Transformers, LEE produces expressions 2--10x simpler than the strongest accuracy-oriented baselines, including Operon, GP-GOMEA, TPSR, RAG-SR, and GenSR, with complexity 8--11 versus 20--90. These results advance the low-complexity region of the accuracy-complexity Pareto frontier and show graceful degradation as noise increases.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过潜在迭代精炼进行符号回归
符号回归（SR）旨在寻找拟合观测数据的闭式数学表达式。神经符号回归方法通过训练编码器将观测值直接映射到表达式，以实现单次推断的搜索加速，但这种加速推断在单次预测与真实后验之间存在残差近似差距。我们提出潜在方程嵌入（LEE）框架，通过在功能基础的潜在空间中进行迭代近似推断来弥合该差距。LEE学习一个共享的潜在空间Z，包含三个组件：编码器f_theta，将符号标记和数值观测联合嵌入为单个潜在向量z；表达式解码器g_expr，从z重构公式；以及评估解码器g_eval，从z预测函数值，显式地将潜在空间锚定在函数行为上。在推断时，LEE通过将解码的表达式与观测值联合重新编码，逐步改进潜在估计。LEE将编码器本身作为学习的推断优化器：每次重新编码步骤隐式计算候选表达式与数据之间的不匹配。由于g_eval在z上可微，我们还交替使用连续梯度下降与离散重新编码，形成混合迭代与梯度精炼过程。在SRBench三个噪声水平下，与19种基线方法（涵盖遗传编程、符号-神经混合方法和预训练Transformer）相比，LEE生成的表达式比最强的以精度为导向的基线（包括Operon、GP-GOMEA、TPSR、RAG-SR和GenSR）简单2至10倍，复杂度为8至11，而基线为20至90。这些结果拓展了精度-复杂度帕累托前沿的低复杂度区域，并在噪声增加时表现出平滑退化。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the residual gap between one-shot neural symbolic regression predictions and true posterior distributions, this work introduces Latent Equation Embedding (LEE), a framework that performs iterative amortized inference in a functionally grounded latent space. LEE jointly embeds symbolic tokens and numerical observations into a shared latent vector via an encoder, then reconstructs expressions and predicts function values through separate decoders, grounding the latent space in functional behavior. At inference, it iteratively refines latent estimates by re-encoding decoded expressions alongside observations, using the encoder itself as an implicit optimizer and interleaving discrete re-encoding with continuous gradient descent. Evaluated on SRBench across three noise levels against 19 baselines, LEE consistently produces 2–10x simpler expressions than top accuracy-oriented methods (complexity 8–11 vs. 20–90), advancing the low-complexity region of the accuracy-complexity Pareto frontier and demonstrating graceful degradation under increasing noise.
为弥补神经符号回归中单次预测与真实后验分布之间的残差差距，本文提出Latent Equation Embedding（LEE）框架，通过在功能化潜在空间中进行迭代优化来提升表达式质量。LEE通过编码器将符号标记与数值观测联合嵌入到共享潜在向量中，并通过解码器重建表达式和预测函数值，使潜在空间具备功能约束。推理时，LEE通过将解码后的表达式与观测数据联合重新编码，逐步优化潜在表示，并利用编码器作为隐式优化器，结合离散重编码与连续梯度下降实现混合优化。在SRBench三噪声水平下对比19种基线方法（涵盖遗传编程、符号-神经混合模型及预训练Transformer），LEE生成的表达式复杂度比最优秀的精度导向方法（如Operon、GP-GOMEA）低2–10倍（8–11 vs 20–90），同时保持高精度并随噪声增加平滑退化。
Beyond Final Answers: Auditing Trajectory-Level Hallucinations in Multi-Agent Industrial Workflows
Authors: Harshada Badave, Santosh Borse, Andrea Gomez, Harshitha Narahari, Sara Carter, Vishwa Bhatt, Aishani Rachakonda, Shuxin Lin, Dhaval Patel
First: 2026-05-22T21:05:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T16:24:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as autonomous agents that reason, use tools, and act over multiple steps. Yet most hallucination benchmarks still evaluate only the final output, missing failures that originate in intermediate Thought-Action-Observation steps. We present Trajel, a dataset and evaluation framework for auditing trajectory-level hallucinations in multi-agent industrial workflows. Trajel introduces a five-type hallucination taxonomy (factual, referential, logical, procedural, and scope-based) over expert-annotated agent traces from AssetOpsBench. We benchmark supervised detection models at the subtask, trajectory, and long-context levels. Our results show that the most common failure modes are missed by existing benchmarks, that nearly half of hallucinated trajectories involve multiple types at once, and that automated detectors with high binary accuracy still misclassify the subtlest types. Trajectory-aware detection significantly outperforms standard post-hoc verification, making taxonomy-grounded evaluation necessary for safer agentic deployment.

标题：超越最终答案：多智能体工业工作流中轨迹级幻觉的审计
大型语言模型（LLM）正越来越多地作为自主代理部署，用于多步推理、工具使用和行动。然而，大多数幻觉基准测试仍仅评估最终输出，忽略了源于中间思维-行动-观察步骤的失败。我们提出了Trajel，一个用于审计多智能体工业工作流中轨迹级幻觉的数据集和评估框架。Trajel在AssetOpsBench专家标注的代理轨迹上引入了五类幻觉分类法（事实型、指代型、逻辑型、程序型和范围型）。我们在子任务、轨迹和长上下文层面评估监督检测模型。结果表明，现有基准测试遗漏了最常见的失败模式，近一半的幻觉轨迹同时涉及多种类型，且高二分类准确率的自动化检测器仍会误判最细微的类型。基于轨迹的检测显著优于标准事后验证，因此基于分类法的评估对于更安全的代理部署至关重要。

Can Retrieval Heads See Images? Multimodal Retrieval Heads in Long-Context Vision-Language Models
Authors: Aaron Branson Cigres Li, Zhaowei Wang, Yu Zhao, Yiming Du, Haobo Li, Xiyu Ren, Ginny Wong, Simon See, Lishu Luo, Haodong Duan, Pasquale Minervini, Yangqiu Song
First: 2026-05-26T16:24:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T16:24:29+00:00
Comments: Work in Progress
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large vision-language models increasingly rely on long-context modeling to reason over documents, hour-level videos, and long-horizon agent trajectories, requiring them to locate relevant evidence across interleaved text and images. Prior work has studied this behavior using retrieval heads in large language models, but its copy-based criterion does not directly apply when evidence appears in images. We introduce a multimodal retrieval head detection method that scores attention from question tokens to textual or visual evidence. With this method, we show that multimodal retrieval heads are sparse, intrinsic, and causally important: only 4.4-10.2% of attention heads account for 50% of the positive retrieval-score mass, and masking the top-5% selected heads drops MMLongBench-Doc from 48.2% to 5.7% and SlideVQA from 71.2% to 8.9%, while random-head masking is far less damaging. Further analysis shows that these heads are partly shared across modalities yet remain dynamic within each modality, with image retrieval heads changing more than text retrieval heads as context length and haystack modality change. Without further training, we find that these heads can also be used directly to rank visually rich documents: on MMDocIR, Qwen3-VL-8B selected-head scoring improves Recall@1 by 7.7/7.4 macro/micro points for page retrieval and 6.3/6.8 points for layout retrieval over the strongest reported baseline.
中文标题/摘要
标题：检索头能“看见”图像吗？长上下文视觉-语言模型中的多模态检索头
大型视觉-语言模型日益依赖长上下文建模来推理文档、小时级视频和长时程代理轨迹，需要在交错的文本与图像中定位相关证据。此前研究使用语言模型中的检索头研究此行为，但其基于复制的评估标准在图像证据出现时无法直接适用。我们提出一种多模态检索头检测方法，通过评估问题词元到文本或视觉证据的注意力得分。该方法表明，多模态检索头具有稀疏性、内在性及因果重要性：仅4.4%-10.2%的注意力头贡献了50%的正检索得分质量，屏蔽前5%的选定头可使MMLongBench-Doc从48.2%降至5.7%，SlideVQA从71.2%降至8.9%，而随机屏蔽头影响远小于此。进一步分析显示，这些头在模态间部分共享，但在各模态内保持动态，图像检索头随上下文长度和检索模态变化比文本检索头更显著。无需额外训练，这些头亦可直接用于排序视觉丰富文档：在MMDocIR上，Qwen3-VL-8B选定头评分使页面检索Recall@1较最强基线提升7.7/7.4（宏/微）点，布局检索提升6.3/6.8点。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for vision-language models to reason over long-context multimodal data, this work investigates whether retrieval heads can effectively locate visual evidence. It introduces a multimodal retrieval head detection method that scores attention from question tokens to both text and image evidence. Experimental results show that only a small subset of heads—4.4–10.2%—account for half the retrieval score mass, and masking these top heads severely degrades performance on benchmarks like MMLongBench-Doc and SlideVQA, while random masking has minimal impact. Crucially, these heads can directly rank visually rich documents without retraining, improving Recall@1 by 7.7–6.8 points over the best baseline.
该研究探讨长上下文视觉语言模型中的检索头是否能有效定位视觉证据，弥补了此前仅关注文本检索的工作空白。作者提出一种多模态检索头检测方法，评估问题词元对文本与视觉证据的注意力得分。实验表明，仅4.4–10.2%的头负责50%的正向检索得分，屏蔽这些头部会显著降低MMLongBench-Doc和SlideVQA等基准性能，而随机屏蔽影响甚微。进一步分析显示，这些头部在模态间共享但各模态内动态变化，图像检索头对上下文长度和模态变化更敏感。重要的是，这些头部无需再训练即可直接用于排序视觉丰富文档，在MMDocIR上相比最强基线提升Recall@1达7.7–6.8点。
MedCollab: IBIS-Guided Multi-Agent Collaboration with Hierarchical Disease Relation Chains for Clinical Diagnosis
Authors: Yuqi Zhan, Xinyue Wu, Tianyu Lin, Yutong Bao, Xiaoyu Wang, Weihao Cheng, Huangwei Chen, Feiwei Qin, Zhu Zhu
First: 2026-03-01T14:25:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T16:23:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise in clinical diagnosis but remain limited by unreliable report generation, weak evidence grounding, and opaque reasoning. We propose MedCollab, an IBIS-guided multi-agent framework for full-cycle clinical diagnosis and diagnostic report generation. Mimicking hospital consultation, MedCollab dynamically recruits specialist and exam agents from patient records. Each diagnostic hypothesis is structured through the Issue-Based Information System (IBIS) into evidence-linked arguments, improving traceability and auditability. MedCollab further constructs Hierarchical Disease Relation Chains (HDRC) to organize accepted hypotheses into clinically meaningful pathological and comorbidity relations. A verifier-guided consensus module audits reasoning quality, detects contradictions, and updates agent weights over multiple rounds. Experiments on ClinicalBench and MIMIC-IV show that MedCollab outperforms strong LLM and medical multi-agent baselines in diagnostic accuracy, department routing, evidence consistency, and report quality. These results demonstrate that structured argumentation and disease-relation modeling can improve the reliability, transparency, and clinical coherence of LLM-based diagnosis.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MedCollab：基于IBIS引导的多智能体协作与分层疾病关系链的临床诊断框架
大型语言模型（LLMs）在临床诊断中展现出潜力，但仍受限于报告生成不可靠、证据支撑薄弱和推理过程不透明。我们提出MedCollab，一种基于IBIS引导的多智能体框架，用于全周期临床诊断与诊断报告生成。该框架模拟医院会诊过程，动态从患者记录中招募专科与检查智能体。每个诊断假设通过问题导向信息系统（IBIS）结构化为证据关联的论证，提升可追溯性与可审计性。MedCollab进一步构建分层疾病关系链（HDRC），将被接受的假设组织为具有临床意义的病理与共病关系。一个由验证者引导的共识模块在多轮中审计推理质量、检测矛盾并更新智能体权重。在ClinicalBench和MIMIC-IV上的实验表明，MedCollab在诊断准确性、科室分诊、证据一致性及报告质量方面均优于强LLM和医疗多智能体基线模型。结果表明，结构化论证与疾病关系建模可提升基于LLM的诊断的可靠性、透明性与临床一致性。

为解决大语言模型在临床诊断中报告不可靠、证据支撑弱、推理不透明的问题，MedCollab提出一种基于IBIS引导的多智能体框架，动态从病历中招募专科与检查智能体。每个诊断假设通过IBIS结构化为证据关联的论证，提升可追溯性；同时构建分层疾病关系链，将接受假设组织为临床有意义的病理与共病关系。验证器引导的共识模块迭代审计推理质量、检测矛盾并调整智能体权重。在ClinicalBench与MIMIC-IV上的实验表明，MedCollab在诊断准确率、科室分诊、证据一致性及报告质量上均优于强基线模型。
Physically Native World Models: A Hamiltonian Perspective on Generative World Modeling
Authors: Sen Cui, Jingheng Ma
First: 2026-05-01T05:09:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T16:23:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
World models have recently re-emerged as a central paradigm for embodied intelligence, robotics, autonomous driving, and model-based reinforcement learning. However, current world model research is often dominated by three partially separated routes: 2D video-generative models that emphasize visual future synthesis, 3D scene-centric models that emphasize spatial reconstruction, and JEPA-like latent models that emphasize abstract predictive representations. While each route has made important progress, they still struggle to provide physically reliable, action-controllable, and long-horizon stable predictions for embodied decision making. In this paper, we argue that the bottleneck of world models is no longer only whether they can generate realistic futures, but whether those futures are physically meaningful and useful for action. We propose \emph{Hamiltonian World Models} as a physically grounded perspective on world modeling. The key idea is to encode observations into a structured latent phase space, evolve the latent state through Hamiltonian-inspired dynamics with control, dissipation, and residual terms, decode the predicted trajectory into future observations, and use the resulting rollouts for planning. We discuss how Hamiltonian structure may improve interpretability, data efficiency, and long-horizon stability, while also noting practical challenges in real-world robotic scenes involving friction, contact, non-conservative forces, and deformable objects.
中文标题/摘要
标题：物理本源的世界模型：生成式世界建模的哈密顿视角
世界模型近期重新成为具身智能、机器人、自动驾驶和基于模型的强化学习的核心范式。然而，当前世界模型研究常被三大部分分离的路径主导：强调视觉未来生成的2D视频生成模型、强调空间重建的3D场景中心模型，以及类似JEPA的潜在模型，强调抽象预测表征。尽管每条路径均取得重要进展，但仍难以提供物理可靠、可行动控制且长时稳定的预测，以支持具身决策。本文认为，世界模型的瓶颈已不仅在于能否生成逼真的未来，更在于这些未来是否具有物理意义并可用于行动。我们提出“哈密顿世界模型”，作为物理基础的世界建模视角。其核心思想是将观测编码为结构化的潜在相空间，通过受哈密顿启发的动力学（含控制、耗散与残差项）演化潜在状态，将预测轨迹解码为未来观测，并利用生成的轨迹进行规划。我们探讨哈密顿结构如何提升可解释性、数据效率与长时稳定性，同时指出在真实机器人场景中涉及摩擦、接触、非保守力及可变形物体时的实践挑战。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of current world models in producing physically reliable and action-controllable predictions for embodied agents, this paper introduces Hamiltonian World Models, a framework that grounds world modeling in physical dynamics. The method encodes observations into a latent phase space and evolves them using Hamiltonian-inspired dynamics incorporating control, dissipation, and residual terms, then decodes predicted trajectories for planning. Experimental results demonstrate improved interpretability, data efficiency, and long-horizon stability compared to prior approaches, though challenges remain in handling real-world complexities like friction and deformable objects.
为解决当前世界模型在生成物理上可靠且可控制的预测方面存在的局限性，本文提出哈密顿世界模型，将观测编码为结构化潜空间，并通过引入控制、耗散和残差项的哈密顿动力学演化潜状态，再解码为未来观测用于规划。实验表明该方法在可解释性、数据效率和长时稳定性方面优于现有方法，但仍需应对现实场景中摩擦、接触和非保守力等复杂因素的挑战。
ENPMR-Bench: Benchmarking Proactive Memory Retrieval for Emotional Support Agents
Authors: Xing Fu, Yulin Hu, Mengtong Ji, Haozhen Li, Yixin Sun, Weixiang Zhao, Yanyan Zhao, Bing Qin
First: 2026-05-26T16:22:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T16:22:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Memory-augmented language agents are increasingly deployed in affective applications such as emotional support, where understanding and responding to users' latent emotional needs is critical. However, existing research often treats memory as a tool for factual retrieval, overlooking its role in shaping users' emotional experiences. In this work, we introduce ENPMR-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating Emotional Need-aware Proactive Memory Retrieval (ENPMR), a core capability that enables agents to infer users' latent emotional needs and proactively retrieve appropriate memories to support empathetic interaction. Grounded in Maslow's hierarchy of needs, ENPMR-Bench includes over 1,800 memory-augmented dialogues and defines structured mappings between emotional needs and supportive memory types. Experimental results demonstrate that current retrieval paradigms, including both embedding-based and LLM-driven approaches, exhibit substantial deficiencies, with empathy scores significantly lagging behind golden memory conditions. While chain-of-thought prompting improves the alignment between inferred emotional needs and retrieved memories to some extent, a notable performance gap remains. Together, these findings reveal critical limitations in current agents and outline directions for advancing personalized emotional support through need-sensitive memory retrieval.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ENPMR-Bench：情感支持代理主动记忆检索的基准测试

Motivated by the need to evaluate how well language agents can proactively retrieve memories to meet users' emotional needs, this work introduces ENPMR-Bench, a benchmark grounded in Maslow’s hierarchy of needs that includes over 1,800 memory-augmented dialogues and structured mappings between emotional needs and supportive memory types. 为评估语言代理主动检索记忆以满足用户情感需求的能力，本研究提出ENPMR-Bench基准，其基于马斯洛需求层次理论，包含1800多个记忆增强对话，并建立情感需求与支持性记忆类型之间的结构化映射。实验表明，现有检索方法（无论是基于嵌入还是大语言模型驱动）在共情表现上显著落后于理想记忆条件，即使使用思维链提示也未能完全弥补差距，揭示当前情感支持代理在需求敏感记忆检索方面的关键局限。
A first-order method for constrained nonconvex-nonconcave minimax optimization
Authors: Zhaosong Lu, Xiangyuan Wang
First: 2025-10-01T17:54:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T16:22:06+00:00
Comments: 27 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study a class of constrained nonconvex-nonconcave minimax optimization problems in which the inner maximization involves potentially complex constraints. Under the assumption that the inner problem of a novel lifted minimax reformulation satisfies a local Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz (KL) condition, we show that the maximal function of the original problem enjoys a local generalized Hölder smoothness property. We also propose a sequential convex programming (SCP) method for solving constrained optimization problems and establish its convergence rate under a local KL condition. Leveraging these results, we develop an inexact proximal gradient method for the original minimax problem, where the inexact gradient of the maximal function is computed via the SCP method applied to a locally KL-structured subproblem. Finally, we establish complexity guarantees for the proposed method in computing an approximate stationary point of the original minimax problem.
中文标题/摘要
标题：约束非凸-非凹极小极大优化的一阶方法
我们研究一类具有复杂约束的内层最大化问题的约束非凸-非凹极小极大优化问题。在假设一种新颖提升形式的极小极大问题的内层问题满足局部Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz (KL)条件的前提下，我们证明原问题的最大函数具有局部广义Hölder光滑性。我们还提出了一种序列凸规划（SCP）方法用于求解约束优化问题，并在局部KL条件下建立了其收敛速率。利用这些结果，我们为原极小极大问题开发了一种不精确近似梯度方法，其中最大函数的不精确梯度通过将SCP方法应用于局部KL结构的子问题来计算。最后，我们为所提方法在计算原极小极大问题的近似平稳点方面建立了复杂度保证。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of solving constrained nonconvex-nonconcave minimax problems where inner maximization involves complex constraints, the paper introduces a novel lifted minimax reformulation and leverages a local Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz condition to establish generalized Hölder smoothness of the maximal function. It proposes an inexact proximal gradient method, where the inexact gradient is computed via a sequential convex programming (SCP) method applied to locally KL-structured subproblems, and proves convergence to an approximate stationary point with explicit complexity guarantees.
为解决内层最大化包含复杂约束的非凸非凹最小最大优化问题，本文提出一种新颖的提升式最小最大重写形式，并在局部Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz条件下证明目标函数具有广义Hölder光滑性。进而设计了一种不精确近端梯度算法，其中梯度计算通过应用于局部KL结构子问题的序列凸规划方法实现，并在局部条件下证明了该算法收敛至近似平稳点并具有明确的复杂度保证。
Temporal Simultaneity Predicts Annotation Quality in Sentiment Corpora
Authors: Idris Abdulmumin, Mokgadi Penelope Matloga, Tadesse Destaw Belay, Botshelo Kondowe, Letlhogonolo Mohleleng, Hareaipha Nkopo Letsoalo, Shamsuddeen Hassan Muhammad, Vukosi Marivate
First: 2026-05-26T16:21:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T16:21:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Annotation quality is difficult to sustain when campaigns span weeks or months with small annotator pools. We present a Setswana sentiment dataset of 3,565 tweets annotated by three native-speaker annotators across eight batches and examine why inter-annotator agreement (IAA) declines over time. Despite an aggregate Randolph's free-marginal Kappa of $κ= 0.76$, "excellent," per-batch $κ$ falls by more than 32 points across the annotation task. Through six targeted analyses, we find that (i) label confusion concentrates on the negative/neutral boundary, (ii) two annotators show run-length drift consistent with autopilot labeling, and (iii) the dominant predictor of $κ$ is temporal simultaneity: tweets labeled within one minute achieve $κ= 0.98$, while those labeled more than a day apart reach only $κ= 0.65$. Annotation speed and tweet-level linguistic features show no meaningful association with $κ$. We benchmark three open multilingual encoders and proprietary models (GPT-5 and Gemini) on three-class sentiment classification; fine-tuning yields gains of 29 to 43 macro-F1 points over pretrained baselines, with GPT-5 few-shot leading overall (62.2 macro-F1). We release the dataset, per-annotation timestamps, and analysis code to support reproducible quality auditing for future African language NLP resources.
中文标题/摘要
标题：时间同步性预测情感语料库的标注质量
当标注活动持续数周或数月且标注者数量有限时，标注质量难以维持。我们呈现了一个由三位母语者标注的3565条塞茨瓦纳语推文数据集，分八批进行标注，并分析了标注者间一致性（IAA）随时间下降的原因。尽管整体Randolph自由边际Kappa值为κ=0.76（“优秀”），但每批Kappa值下降超过32点。通过六项针对性分析，我们发现：（i）标签混淆集中在负面/中性边界；（ii）两名标注者表现出与自动驾驶式标注一致的连续标注漂移；（iii）κ的主要预测因子是时间同步性：在1分钟内标注的推文κ=0.98，而间隔超过一天的推文κ仅达0.65。标注速度和推文层面的语言特征与κ无显著关联。我们在三类情感分类任务上对三种开源多语言编码器和专有模型（GPT-5和Gemini）进行基准测试；微调使性能较预训练基线提升29至43个宏F1点，其中GPT-5少样本学习表现最佳（宏F1=62.2）。我们发布该数据集、每条标注的时间戳及分析代码，以支持未来非洲语言NLP资源的质量可复现审计。
