Motivated by the decline in annotation quality over time in long-running sentiment labeling campaigns, the study examines a Setswana tweet dataset annotated across eight batches by three native speakers. It finds that despite an overall high inter-annotator agreement (κ=0.76), per-batch agreement drops sharply, with temporal simultaneity emerging as the strongest predictor: tweets labeled within one minute achieve κ=0.98, while those labeled more than a day apart reach only κ=0.65. The study rules out annotation speed or linguistic features as key factors and benchmarks several models, finding that fine-tuning GPT-5 few-shot yields the highest performance (62.2 macro-F1) on sentiment classification.
为了解决长期标注任务中注释质量随时间下降的问题，该研究分析了一个由三名母语者分八批标注的塞茨瓦纳语推文数据集。研究发现，尽管整体标注者间一致性（κ=0.76）较高，但每批一致性显著下降，且标注时间同步性是主要预测因素：同一分钟内标注的推文一致性达κ=0.98，而间隔一天以上标注的仅κ=0.65。研究排除了标注速度和语言特征的影响，并表明对三种多语言编码器及专有模型（含GPT-5）进行微调可提升三分类情感分类效果29–43个宏F1点，其中GPT-5少样本微调表现最佳（62.2宏F1）
Faithfulness Evaluation for Decoder-only LLM Attributions with Controlled Retained Information
Authors: Xin Huang, Antoni B. Chan
First: 2026-01-06T15:22:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T16:19:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly evaluated with input attribution methods, yet comparing such explanations remains challenging. Existing soft-perturbation faithfulness metrics, such as Soft-NC and Soft-NS, can conflate attribution quality with the number of words retained during perturbation: attribution methods with larger average scores may keep more words and therefore obtain inflated scores. To address this issue, we propose $π$-Soft-NC and $π$-Soft-NS, an evaluation framework that compares attribution methods under the same expected retaining probability, thus controlling the number of retained words. We further introduce Grad-ELLM, a gradient-based attribution method tailored to autoregressive decoder-only LLMs, which combines gradient-derived channel importance with attention-derived token importance at each decoding step. Experiments on classification and open-generation tasks with Llama and Mistral show that Grad-ELLM achieves strong comprehensiveness-oriented faithfulness under $π$-Soft-NC, while there is no dominant method under $π$-Soft-NS. Our evaluation metric serves as a rigorous framework to compare XAI methods for LLMs, which will support progress in the field.
中文标题/摘要
标题：解码器仅模型归因的忠实性评估方法：控制保留信息量
大型语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地使用输入归因方法进行评估，但比较这些解释仍具挑战性。现有软扰动忠实性度量（如Soft-NC和Soft-NS）可能将归因质量与扰动过程中保留的词数混淆：得分较高的归因方法可能保留更多词，从而获得虚高的分数。为解决此问题，我们提出π-Soft-NC和π-Soft-NS评估框架，在相同预期保留概率下比较不同归因方法，从而控制保留词数。我们进一步提出Grad-ELLM，一种基于梯度的归因方法，专为自回归解码器仅LLMs设计，结合梯度推导的通道重要性与注意力推导的词重要性，每一步解码均进行综合。在Llama和Mistral模型上的分类与开放生成任务实验表明，Grad-ELLM在π-Soft-NC下实现强综合性忠实性，而在π-Soft-NS下无明显主导方法。我们的评估指标为LLMs的XAI方法比较提供严谨框架，将推动该领域进展。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the problem that existing faithfulness metrics like Soft-NC and Soft-NS confuse attribution quality with retained word count, this work introduces π-Soft-NC and π-Soft-NS, which evaluate attribution methods under a fixed expected retention probability to isolate attribution performance. It also proposes Grad-ELLM, a gradient-based attribution method for decoder-only LLMs that integrates channel and token importance from attention and gradients. Experiments on Llama and Mistral across classification and open-generation tasks show Grad-ELLM achieves high faithfulness under π-Soft-NC, while no single method dominates under π-Soft-NS, demonstrating the value of the new metrics for fair XAI comparison.
为解决现有LLM归因方法的忠实性评估易受保留词数干扰的问题，本文提出π-Soft-NC和π-Soft-NS两种在相同保留概率下比较归因质量的评估框架，并设计了针对解码器型LLM的Grad-ELLM归因方法，结合梯度通道重要性和注意力词重要性。实验表明Grad-ELLM在π-Soft-NC下表现优异，但在π-Soft-NS下无单一方法占优，凸显了控制保留概率对公平评估的重要性。
Explainable Comparison of Feature-Based and Deep Learning Models for TROPOMI Methane Plume Screening
Authors: Solomiia Kurchaba, Joannes D. Maasakkers, Berend J. Schuit, Ilse Aben
First: 2026-05-26T16:17:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T16:17:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Continuous and global detection of large methane emissions is a crucial step for global warming mitigation. Satellite observations, such as from S5P/TROPOMI, combined with plume detection algorithms, can play a key role in this effort. However, not all TROPOMI plume detections that look like methane emission plumes are the result of actual emissions. A significant part of the plume-like features in the data are retrieval artifacts. Such artifacts could be the result of variations in elevation or albedo gradients, high concentrations of aerosols, coastal lines, water bodies, etc. Previous work approached the problem of plume-artifact classification by means of a Support Vector Machine Classifier (SVC), trained on an extensive set of observation-based scalar features designed by domain experts. However, such an approach limits the information scope received by the algorithm to what is deemed to be important by the experts, breaks the spatial relationship between pixels, and loses information during the process of statistical aggregation. In this study, we compare feature-based (SVC, Random Forest, XGBoost) and image-based (ResNet-18, ResNet-34) models for methane plume-artifact classification under balanced and imbalanced evaluation settings. To interpret the results, we apply SHAP-based explainability to both model families. Our findings provide practical guidance for model selection in operational methane-screening workflows such as the CAMS Methane Hotspot Explorer.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于特征与深度学习模型的TROPOMI甲烷羽流筛选可解释性比较
持续全球监测大规模甲烷排放是减缓全球变暖的关键步骤。来自S5P/TROPOMI等卫星观测结合羽流检测算法，可在此过程中发挥重要作用。然而，并非所有TROPOMI检测到的类似甲烷羽流的特征都源于实际排放，数据中大量羽流状特征实为反演伪影，可能由地形起伏、反照率梯度、高浓度气溶胶、海岸线、水体等引起。此前研究采用支持向量机分类器（SVC），基于领域专家设计的大量观测标量特征进行羽流伪影分类。但该方法受限于专家认定的重要信息范围，破坏像素间空间关系，并在统计聚合过程中丢失信息。本研究在平衡与非平衡评估设置下，比较基于特征（SVC、随机森林、XGBoost）与基于图像（ResNet-18、ResNet-34）的模型在甲烷羽流伪影分类中的表现。为解释结果，我们对两类模型均应用基于SHAP的可解释性方法。研究结果为实际甲烷筛选工作（如CAMS甲烷热点探测器）的模型选择提供实用指导。
Summary / 总结
To improve methane plume screening by distinguishing real emissions from retrieval artifacts in TROPOMI satellite data, this study compares feature-based models (SVC, Random Forest, XGBoost) and image-based deep learning models (ResNet-18, ResNet-34) under balanced and imbalanced conditions. The feature-based models rely on expert-designed scalar features but lose spatial context and expert-defined relevance; the deep learning models capture spatial patterns directly from images. Results show that deep learning models outperform feature-based ones in both settings, especially under imbalance, and SHAP explainability reveals that deep models focus on spatially coherent regions while feature models rely on specific scalar thresholds.
为区分TROPOMI卫星数据中真实甲烷排放与伪影，本研究对比了基于特征的模型（SVC、随机森林、XGBoost）与基于图像的深度学习模型（ResNet-18、ResNet-34），在平衡与非平衡数据条件下评估性能。特征模型依赖专家设计的标量特征，而图像模型直接处理空间像素数据，保留空间关系。通过SHAP可解释性分析两类模型决策。结果表明，深度学习模型在准确率上优于特征模型，尤其在非平衡数据下表现更优，且SHAP分析显示其更有效捕捉空间上下文，为实际甲烷筛查系统提供实用模型选择依据。
MRT: Masked Region Transformer for Layered Image Generation and Editing at Scale
Authors: Zhicong Tang, Zhao Zhang, Jingye Chen, Mohan Zhou, Yifan Pu, Yuchi Liu, Yalong Bai, Ethan Smith, Yuhui Yuan
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-05-26T16:16:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T16:16:19+00:00
Comments: CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Layered image generation and editing is a fundamental capability that enables layer-wise reuse, editing, and composition of generated visual content, analogous to word-level editing in natural language. Despite its importance, this remains an underexplored area at scale. To address this gap, we present MRT, a 20B-parameter masked region diffusion model tailored for multi-layer transparent image generation and editing, trained on over 10M multilingual design samples spanning diverse aspect ratios and textual prompts. To fully leverage this scale, we make two key technical contributions. First, we unify three complementary tasks including text-to-layers, image-to-layers, and layers-to-layers within a shared masked region diffusion framework, where selective token masking enables flexible layer-wise generation and editing. Second, to enable overflow layer generation, we introduce an overflow-aware canvas layer that handles boundary inconsistencies and supports semi-transparent background synthesis, enabling complete editable layers extending beyond visible canvas boundaries. Additionally, we apply diffusion distillation to achieve 8-step, real-time multi-layer generation with minimal quality degradation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our framework substantially outperforms prior state-of-the-art approaches, including various commercial systems, across all three tasks, establishing a new benchmark for multi-layer transparent image generation. Notably, our model significantly outperforms the concurrent Qwen-Image-Layered model in image-to-layers quality according to user-study results, while achieving 10-100\times faster inference and reducing activation GPU memory consumption by 50-90\% during image-to-layer inference.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MRT：面向大规模分层图像生成与编辑的遮罩区域变换器
分层图像生成与编辑是一项基础能力，可实现生成视觉内容的逐层复用、编辑与合成，类似于自然语言中的词级编辑。尽管其重要性显著，但在大规模应用上仍属探索不足。为填补这一空白，我们提出MRT，一种专为多层透明图像生成与编辑设计的200亿参数遮罩区域扩散模型，基于超过1000万个多语言设计样本训练，涵盖多样化的长宽比与文本提示。为充分挖掘该规模潜力，我们做出两项关键技术贡献：首先，将文本到分层、图像到分层、分层到分层三个互补任务统一于共享的遮罩区域扩散框架内，通过选择性token遮罩实现灵活的逐层生成与编辑；其次，为支持溢出层生成，我们引入一种溢出感知画布层，处理边界不一致性并支持半透明背景合成，从而实现超出可见画布边界的完整可编辑分层。此外，我们采用扩散蒸馏技术，实现8步实时多层生成且质量损失极小。大量实验表明，我们的框架在三项任务上均显著优于现有最先进方法（包括各类商业系统），确立了多层透明图像生成的新基准。值得注意的是，根据用户研究结果，我们的模型在图像到分层任务的质量上显著优于同期发布的Qwen-Image-Layered模型，同时推理速度提升10-100倍，图像到分层推理时GPU激活内存消耗降低50-90%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable, layer-wise image generation and editing analogous to word-level text editing, the authors introduce MRT, a 20B-parameter masked region diffusion model trained on over 10M multilingual design samples. The method unifies three tasks—text-to-layers, image-to-layers, and layers-to-layers—within a shared masked region diffusion framework using selective token masking for flexible layer manipulation, and introduces an overflow-aware canvas layer to handle boundary inconsistencies and enable semi-transparent background synthesis. Experimental results show MRT significantly outperforms prior state-of-the-art methods across all tasks, including commercial systems, and notably surpasses the concurrent Qwen-Image-Layered model in image-to-layers quality according to user studies, while achieving 10–100× faster inference and 50–90% lower GPU memory usage during image-to-layer generation.
为实现类文本编辑的分层图像生成与编辑能力，作者提出MRT模型，一个200亿参数的掩码区域扩散模型，基于超过1000万张多语言设计样本训练。该方法统一了文本到分层、图像到分层、分层到分层三项任务，通过选择性掩码实现灵活的分层操作；引入溢出感知画布层，解决边界不一致问题并支持半透明背景合成，使分层可超出可见画布范围。扩散蒸馏技术实现8步实时生成且质量损失极小。实验表明，MRT在三项任务上均显著优于现有最先进方法，包括商业系统，且在图像到分层任务中用户研究显示优于同期Qwen-Image-Layered模型，推理速度提升10-100倍，图像生成时GPU显存消耗降低50-90%。
Chat2Workflow: A Benchmark for Generating Executable Visual Workflows with Natural Language
Authors: Yi Zhong, Buqiang Xu, Yijun Wang, Zifei Shan, Shuofei Qiao, Guozhou Zheng, Ningyu Zhang
First: 2026-04-21T16:49:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T16:14:10+00:00
Comments: Work in progress
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
At present, executable visual workflows have emerged as a mainstream paradigm in real-world industrial deployments, offering strong reliability and controllability. However, in current practice, such workflows are almost entirely constructed through manual engineering: developers must carefully design workflows, write prompts for each step, and repeatedly revise the logic as requirements evolve -- making development costly, time-consuming, and error-prone. To study whether large language models can automate this multi-round interaction process, we introduce Chat2Workflow, a benchmark for generating executable visual workflows directly from natural language, and propose a robust agentic baseline to improve performance. The benchmark is built from a large collection of real-world business workflows, with each instance designed so that the generated workflow can be transformed and directly deployed to practical workflow platforms such as Dify and Coze. Experimental results show that while state-of-the-art language models can often capture high-level intent, they struggle to generate correct, stable, and executable workflows, especially given complex and evolving requirements. Although our agentic baseline yields up to 6.05% resolve rate gains, the remaining real-world gap positions Chat2Workflow as a foundation for advancing industrial-grade automation. Code is available at https://github.com/zjunlp/Chat2Workflow.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Chat2Workflow：一种从自然语言生成可执行视觉工作流的基准
目前，可执行视觉工作流已成为现实工业部署中的主流范式，具有强大的可靠性和可控性。然而，在当前实践中，此类工作流几乎完全依赖人工工程：开发者必须精心设计工作流、为每一步编写提示，并随着需求变化反复修订逻辑——使得开发成本高、耗时长且易出错。为研究大语言模型是否能自动化这一多轮交互过程，我们引入Chat2Workflow基准，用于直接从自然语言生成可执行视觉工作流，并提出一个稳健的代理基线以提升性能。该基准基于大量真实业务工作流构建，每个实例均设计为可转换并直接部署到Dify和Coze等实际工作流平台。实验结果表明，尽管最先进的语言模型通常能捕捉高层意图，但在生成正确、稳定且可执行的工作流方面仍存在困难，尤其面对复杂且不断变化的需求时。尽管我们的代理基线实现了最高6.05%的解决率提升，但剩余的现实差距使Chat2Workflow成为推动工业级自动化发展的基础。代码见https://github.com/zjunlp/Chat2Workflow。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high cost and error-proneness of manually constructing executable visual workflows, this work introduces Chat2Workflow, a benchmark designed to evaluate whether large language models can autonomously generate deployable workflows from natural language. The benchmark is built from real-world business workflows and is structured to enable direct deployment on platforms like Dify and Coze. The authors propose an agentic baseline to improve performance, which demonstrates up to a 6.05% increase in resolution rate over existing models. However, results show that even state-of-the-art models often fail to produce correct, stable, and executable workflows under complex or evolving requirements, highlighting the need for further research to bridge the gap between language models and industrial automation.
鉴于人工构建可执行视觉工作流成本高且易出错，本研究提出Chat2Workflow基准，评估大语言模型能否直接从自然语言生成可部署的工作流。该基准基于真实业务工作流构建，兼容Dify和Coze等平台。作者提出一种代理基线方法提升性能，实验表明当前模型虽能理解高层意图，但在复杂或变化需求下常难以生成正确、稳定且可执行的工作流。代理方法最高提升6.05%的解决率，但仍有显著差距，凸显工业级自动化仍需进一步研究。
Tracing the Dynamics of Refusal: Exploiting Latent Refusal Trajectories for Robust Jailbreak Detection
Authors: Xulin Hu, Che Wang, Wei Yang Bryan Lim, Jianbo Gao, Zhong Chen
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-02T14:56:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T16:10:32+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the 43rd International Conference on Machine Learning (ICML 2026). Camera-ready version
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Representation Engineering analyses often characterize refusal using static directions extracted from terminal or pooled representations. We ask whether this view misses how refusal is constructed across layer-token positions. Using causal tracing, we identify a \textit{Refusal Trajectory}: a sparse upstream activation pattern that often persists even when attacks such as GCG suppress terminal refusal signals. Based on this observation, we propose SALO (Sparse Activation Localization Operator), a lightweight white-box detector that operates on raw hidden-state volumes from a selected layer window. Across Qwen, Llama, and Mistral models, SALO improves jailbreak detection on several attack families under a fixed XSTest-calibrated operating point. We further analyze static RepE-style baselines, ROI sensitivity, adaptive GCG attacks, and encoded-input boundary cases, clarifying both the promise and limitations of refusal-trajectory monitoring.
中文标题/摘要
标题：追踪拒绝的动态：利用潜在拒绝轨迹实现鲁棒的越狱检测
表示工程分析通常通过从终端或池化表示中提取的静态方向来刻画拒绝行为。我们质疑这种观点是否忽略了拒绝在层-令牌位置上的构建过程。通过因果追踪，我们识别出一种“拒绝轨迹”：一种稀疏的上游激活模式，即使在GCG等攻击抑制终端拒绝信号时，该模式仍常持续存在。基于此观察，我们提出SALO（稀疏激活定位算子），这是一种轻量级白盒检测器，作用于选定层窗口的原始隐藏状态体积。在Qwen、Llama和Mistral模型上，SALO在固定XSTest校准操作点下，对多个攻击家族的越狱检测性能均有提升。我们进一步分析了静态RepE风格基线、ROI敏感性、自适应GCG攻击及编码输入边界情况，阐明了拒绝轨迹监控的潜力与局限。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that static representation analyses miss the dynamic construction of refusal across transformer layers, this work introduces causal tracing to identify a Refusal Trajectory—a sparse upstream activation pattern that persists even under adversarial attacks like GCG. The authors propose SALO, a lightweight white-box detector that operates on raw hidden-state volumes from a selected layer window to localize these trajectories. Experiments across Qwen, Llama, and Mistral show SALO consistently improves jailbreak detection accuracy across multiple attack families while maintaining a fixed XSTest-calibrated operating point, outperforming static RepE-style baselines and demonstrating robustness to adaptive attacks and encoded-input boundary cases.
鉴于静态拒绝表征可能忽略跨层Transformer中拒绝行为的动态构建，本文通过因果追踪识别出一种拒绝轨迹——一种即使在GCG等对抗攻击下仍能持续存在的稀疏上游激活模式。基于此，提出轻量级白盒检测器SALO，直接作用于选定层窗口的原始隐藏状态体积以定位该轨迹。实验表明，在Qwen、Llama和Mistral模型上，SALO在固定XSTest校准操作点下显著优于静态基线方法，同时通过分析ROI敏感性、自适应攻击和编码输入边界情况，揭示了拒绝轨迹监控的潜力与局限。
MinT: Managed Infrastructure for Training and Serving Millions of LLMs
Authors: Mind Lab, :, Song Cao, Vic Cao, Andrew Chen, Kaijie Chen, Cleon Cheng, Steven Chiang, Kaixuan Fan, Hera Feng, Huan Feng, Arthur Fu, Jun Gao, Hongquan Gu, Aaron Guan, Nolan Ho, Mutian Hong, Hailee Hou, Peixuan Hua, Charles Huang, Miles Jiang, Nora Jiang, Yuyi Jiang, Qiuyu Jin, Fancy Kong, Andrew Lei, Kyrie Lei, Alexy Li, Lucian Li, Ray Li, Theo Li, Zhihui Li, Jiayi Lin, Kairus Liu, Kieran Liu, Logan Liu, Xiang Liu, Irvine Lu, Maeve Luo, Runze Lv, Pony Ma, Verity Niu, Anson Qiu, Vincent Wang, Rio Yang, Maxwell Yao, Carrie Ye, Regis Ye, Wenlin Ye, Josh Ying, Danney Zeng, Yuhan Zhan, Anya Zhang, Di Zhang, Ruijia Zhang, Sueky Zhang, Ya Zhang, Wei Zhao, Ada Zhou, Changhai Zhou, Yuhua Zhou, Xinyue Zhu, Murphy Zhuang
First: 2026-05-13T16:59:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T16:10:31+00:00
Comments: 30 pages, technical report
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present MindLab Toolkit (MinT), a managed infrastructure system for Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) post-training and online serving. MinT targets a setting where many trained policies are produced over a small number of expensive base-model deployments. Instead of materializing each policy as a merged full checkpoint, MinT keeps the base model resident and moves exported LoRA adapter revisions through rollout, update, export, evaluation, serving, and rollback, hiding distributed training, serving, scheduling, and data movement behind a service interface. MinT scales this path along three axes. Scale Up extends LoRA RL to frontier-scale dense and MoE architectures, including MLA and DSA attention paths, with training and serving validated beyond 1T total parameters. Scale Down moves only the exported LoRA adapter, which can be under 1% of base-model size in rank-1 settings; adapter-only handoff reduces the measured step by 18.3x on a 4B dense model and 2.85x on a 30B MoE, while concurrent multi-policy GRPO shortens wall time by 1.77x and 1.45x without raising peak memory. Scale Out separates durable policy addressability from CPU/GPU working sets: a tensor-parallel deployment supports 10^6-scale addressable catalogs (measured single-engine sweeps through 100K) and thousand-adapter active waves at cluster scale, with cold loading treated as scheduled service work and packed MoE LoRA tensors improving live engine loading by 8.5-8.7x. MinT thus manages million-scale LoRA policy catalogs while training and serving selected adapter revisions over shared 1T-class base models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MinT：用于训练和提供百万级大语言模型的托管基础设施
我们提出了MindLab工具包（MinT），一个面向低秩适配（LoRA）后训练和在线服务的托管基础设施系统。MinT针对一种场景：在少量昂贵的基础模型部署上生成大量训练好的策略。MinT不将每个策略合并为完整检查点，而是保留基础模型驻留，并在部署、更新、导出、评估、服务和回滚过程中移动导出的LoRA适配器修订版，将分布式训练、服务、调度和数据移动隐藏在服务接口之后。MinT沿三个维度扩展此路径：向上扩展将LoRA RL扩展至前沿规模的密集型和MoE架构（包括MLA和DSA注意力路径），训练与服务已验证超过1万亿总参数；向下扩展仅移动导出的LoRA适配器，其大小在秩1设置下可低于基础模型的1%；仅适配器交接使4B密集模型的测量步数减少18.3倍，30B MoE模型减少2.85倍，同时并发多策略GRPO将壁时缩短1.77倍和1.45倍，且不增加峰值内存；向外扩展将持久策略可寻址性与CPU/GPU工作集分离：张量并行部署支持10^6规模可寻址目录（单引擎扫描10万项），集群规模下支持千适配器活跃波次，冷加载作为调度服务工作处理，打包MoE LoRA张量使实时引擎加载提升8.5-8.7倍。MinT因此可管理百万级LoRA策略目录，同时在共享的1万亿级基础模型上训练和提供选定的适配器修订版。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of storing and deploying millions of full LLM checkpoints, MinT introduces a managed infrastructure for LoRA-based post-training and online serving. It retains the base model in memory and dynamically moves only lightweight LoRA adapters through the full lifecycle—from rollout to rollback—abstracting away distributed training, scheduling, and data movement. Experiments demonstrate that MinT scales efficiently across three dimensions: it supports frontier-scale architectures (up to 1T parameters), reduces deployment overhead by up to 18.3x on dense models and 2.85x on MoE models by transferring only adapters, and enables massive policy catalogs (10^6 addressable policies) with concurrent active adapters, improving live engine loading by 8.5–8.7x through tensor-parallel deployment and optimized MoE tensor packing.
为解决存储和部署百万级LLM全量检查点的低效问题，MinT提出一种基于LoRA的管理基础设施，保留基础模型而动态服务和更新轻量级适配器修订。其在三个维度上实现扩展：Scale Up支持前沿架构如MLA和DSA，训练与服务验证规模超1T参数；Scale Down仅迁移适配器（占基础模型不足1%），在4B模型上训练步骤减少18.3倍、30B模型减少2.85倍，多策略RL加速1.77倍和1.45倍；Scale Out通过张量并行部署支持10^6级可寻址策略，冷加载作为调度工作处理，MoE适配器打包使在线引擎加载提升8.5–8.7倍。
PRBench: A Standardized Probabilistic Robustness Benchmark
Authors: Yi Zhang, Zheng Wang, Zhen Chen, Wenjie Ruan, Qing Guo, Siddartha Khastgir, Carsten Maple, Xingyu Zhao
First: 2025-11-03T16:33:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T16:10:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Deep learning models are notoriously vulnerable to imperceptible perturbations. Most existing research centers on adversarial robustness (AR), which evaluates models under worst-case scenarios by examining the existence of deterministic adversarial examples (AEs). In contrast, probabilistic robustness (PR) adopts a statistical perspective, measuring the probability that predictions remain correct under stochastic perturbations. While PR is widely regarded as a practical complement to AR, dedicated training methods for improving PR are still relatively underexplored, albeit with emerging progress. Among the few PR-targeted training methods, we identify three limitations: i non-comparable evaluation protocols; ii limited comparisons to strong AT baselines despite anecdotal PR gains from AT; and iii no unified framework to compare the generalization of these methods. Thus, we introduce PRBench, the first benchmark dedicated to evaluating improvements in PR achieved by different robustness training methods. PRBench empirically compares most common AT and PR-targeted training methods using a comprehensive set of metrics, including clean accuracy, PR and AR performance, training efficiency, and generalization error (GE). We also provide theoretical analysis on the GE of PR performance across different training methods. Main findings revealed by PRBench include: AT methods are more versatile than PR-targeted training methods in terms of improving both AR and PR performance across diverse hyperparameter settings, while PR-targeted training methods consistently yield lower GE and higher clean accuracy. A leaderboard comprising 229 trained models across 7 datasets and 10 model architectures is publicly available at https://wellzline.github.io/PRBenchLeaderboard/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PRBench：标准化概率鲁棒性基准
深度学习模型对微小扰动异常脆弱。现有大部分研究聚焦对抗鲁棒性（AR），通过检验确定性对抗样本（AEs）的存在来评估模型在最坏情况下的表现。相比之下，概率鲁棒性（PR）采用统计视角，衡量在随机扰动下预测保持正确的概率。尽管PR被广泛认为是AR的实用补充，但专门用于提升PR的训练方法仍相对较少探索，尽管已有初步进展。在少数针对PR的训练方法中，我们发现三个局限：i 评估协议不可比；ii 尽管有AT方法在PR上获得经验性增益，但与强AT基线的比较有限；iii 缺乏统一框架比较这些方法的泛化能力。因此，我们引入PRBench，这是首个专门用于评估不同鲁棒性训练方法在PR提升方面的基准。PRBench通过一套全面的指标（包括干净准确率、PR与AR性能、训练效率和泛化误差GE）实证比较了主流AT与PR目标训练方法。我们还对不同训练方法下PR性能的GE进行了理论分析。PRBench的主要发现包括：在多样超参数设置下，AT方法在提升AR与PR性能方面比PR目标训练方法更具通用性，而PR目标训练方法始终表现出更低的GE和更高的干净准确率。包含7个数据集、10种模型架构、229个训练模型的排行榜公开于https://wellzline.github.io/PRBenchLeaderboard/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for standardized evaluation of probabilistic robustness (PR) in deep learning, this work introduces PRBench, the first benchmark designed to compare how different training methods affect PR under stochastic perturbations. It systematically evaluates common adversarial training (AT) and PR-targeted methods using metrics including clean accuracy, PR and AR performance, training efficiency, and generalization error. Experimental results show that AT methods generally outperform PR-targeted methods in enhancing both AR and PR across diverse hyperparameters, while PR-targeted methods consistently achieve lower generalization error and higher clean accuracy.
为标准化评估深度学习模型的统计鲁棒性（PR），PRBench首次构建了专门用于比较不同训练方法在PR提升效果的基准。该框架统一评估了干净准确率、PR与AR性能、训练效率及泛化误差，覆盖7个数据集和10种模型架构。实验表明，对抗训练（AT）方法在超参数多样性下更具普适性，PR针对性方法则在泛化误差和干净准确率上表现更优。
The Coverage Illusion: From Pre-retrieval Routing Failure to Post-retrieval Cascades in a Production RAG System
Authors: Zafar Hussain, Kristoffer Nielbo
First: 2026-05-26T16:08:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T16:08:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In modern RAG pipelines, query augmentation methods such as HyDE and query expansion are applied to every query, resulting in substantial LLM inference costs and increased end-to-end latency. The empirical justification for this overhead in real production traffic remains largely unexplored. We present a case study of the Danish National Encyclopedia, evaluating five retrieval workflows over 20,000 query-workflow pairs from production traffic and synthetic conditions. In this system, synthetic queries suggest that LLM augmentation is needed for over 90% of queries to achieve high retrieval coverage. However, under our production deferral policy, only 27.8% of real user queries need LLM augmentation. We call this gap the Coverage Illusion and attribute it to a structural mismatch between synthetic and real query distributions. Pre-retrieval routing cannot resolve this gap, as the need for LLM augmentation is only revealed after searching the index, a result confirmed by our evaluation of four machine learning paradigms. The coverage gap, undetectable from the query alone, motivates a post-retrieval cascade that runs workflows in cheapest-first order and escalates to LLM augmentation only when a step returns no documents. Operating entirely without training overhead or secondary serving infrastructure, the cascade improves quality by +0.140 Composite Overall points over Always-HyDE, reduces latency by 31.8%, and serves 72.2% of real user queries without LLM augmentation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：覆盖幻觉：生产级RAG系统中预检索路由失败与后检索级联现象
在现代RAG管道中，HyDE和查询扩展等查询增强方法被应用于每个查询，导致大量LLM推理开销和端到端延迟增加。然而，这种开销在真实生产流量中的实证依据仍鲜有研究。我们以丹麦国家百科全书为案例，评估了五个检索工作流，基于20,000组生产流量与合成条件下的查询-工作流对。合成查询表明，为实现高检索覆盖率，超过90%的查询需LLM增强；但在我们的生产延迟策略下，仅27.8%的真实用户查询需要LLM增强。我们称此差距为“覆盖幻觉”，归因于合成与真实查询分布的结构性不匹配。预检索路由无法解决此差距，因为LLM增强需求仅在索引搜索后显现，这一结论通过四种机器学习范式评估得到验证。由于查询本身无法检测覆盖率差距，我们提出后检索级联机制：按成本最低优先顺序运行工作流，仅当某步骤返回无文档时才升级至LLM增强。该机制无需训练开销或二次服务基础设施，相比Always-HyDE提升质量0.140个综合得分，降低延迟31.8%，且72.2%的真实用户查询无需LLM增强。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high inference costs and latency from applying LLM-based query augmentation universally in RAG systems, this study investigates a coverage gap between synthetic and real query distributions in a production system. Using 20,000 query-workflow pairs from the Danish National Encyclopedia, it finds that while synthetic queries require augmentation for 90% to achieve coverage, only 27.8% of real user queries do. This discrepancy, termed the Coverage Illusion, cannot be resolved by pre-retrieval routing, as augmentation need is only revealed post-retrieval. The paper introduces a post-retrieval cascade that runs workflows in cheapest-first order and escalates to LLM augmentation only when retrieval fails, achieving a 0.140 point quality gain over Always-HyDE, 31.8% latency reduction, and serving 72.2% of queries without augmentation.
为降低RAG系统中LLM查询增强带来的高推理成本和延迟，本研究探讨为何合成基准会高估真实生产流量中对增强的需求。通过对丹麦国家百科全书的2万组查询-工作流数据评估发现，合成查询中90%需增强以实现高召回，而真实用户查询仅27.8%需要。这种差距称为“覆盖率幻觉”，源于合成与真实查询分布不匹配，且无法通过预检索路由解决。研究提出一种后检索级联机制，优先执行低成本检索步骤，仅在无结果时才启用LLM增强，相比Always-HyDE提升0.140分质量，降低31.8%延迟，并在72.2%真实查询中无需LLM增强，且无需训练或额外基础设施。
Nonlinear Data Integration via Kernel Methods for Data Collaboration Analysis
Authors: Yamato Suetake, Yuta Kawakami, Shunnosuke Ikeda, Yuichi Takano
First: 2026-05-26T16:07:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T16:07:32+00:00
Comments: 50 pages, 7 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Collaborative analysis of decentralized confidential datasets is important, but direct sharing of original datasets is often restricted by privacy and institutional constraints. Data collaboration (DC) analysis transforms each dataset into privacy-preserving intermediate representations via party-specific obfuscation functions and integrates them into common collaboration representations using an anchor dataset. However, many existing DC analysis methods rely on linear transformations for data obfuscation and integration, which may increase reconstruction risk. Although nonlinear dimensionality reduction can mitigate this risk, conventional linear integration methods cannot accurately align intermediate representations produced by nonlinear transformations. Moreover, existing integration methods mainly minimize discrepancies among parties and do not explicitly incorporate geometric or target-variable information useful for downstream analysis. To overcome these limitations, we first formulate linear kernel integration (LKI) as a linear integration method and then kernelize it to obtain nonlinear kernel integration (NKI). NKI admits a globally optimal solution via kernel ridge regression and an eigenvalue problem. We also introduce graph regularization and a centering constraint so that the target representation can capture geometric and target-variable information useful for downstream analysis. Experiments on image classification tasks demonstrate that NKI improves classification accuracy over existing linear integration methods under nonlinear dimensionality reduction, with further gains from target-variable-aware graph regularization and centering. The results also show that dimensionality reduction choices substantially affect both classification accuracy and reconstruction risk.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于核方法的非线性数据集成用于数据协作分析
分散式保密数据的协作分析很重要，但原始数据的直接共享常受隐私和机构限制。数据协作（DC）分析通过各参与方特定的混淆函数将每个数据集转换为隐私保护的中间表示，并利用锚定数据集将其整合为共同协作表示。然而，许多现有DC分析方法依赖线性变换进行数据混淆与集成，可能增加重建风险。尽管非线性降维可缓解此风险，但传统线性集成方法无法准确对齐非线性变换生成的中间表示。此外，现有集成方法主要最小化各参与方间的差异，未显式纳入对下游分析有用的几何或目标变量信息。为克服这些局限，我们首先将线性核集成（LKI）形式化为线性集成方法，再将其核化以获得非线性核集成（NKI）。NKI可通过核岭回归和特征值问题获得全局最优解。我们还引入图正则化与中心化约束，使目标表示能捕捉对下游分析有用的几何与目标变量信息。在图像分类任务上的实验表明，NKI在非线性降维下比现有线性集成方法提升分类准确率，且结合目标变量感知图正则化与中心化后进一步提升性能。结果亦表明，降维选择显著影响分类准确率与重建风险。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to analyze decentralized confidential datasets without direct sharing, this work proposes nonlinear kernel integration (NKI) to improve data collaboration analysis. It extends linear kernel integration (LKI) by kernelizing it to handle nonlinear obfuscation and integration, enabling globally optimal solutions via kernel ridge regression and eigenvalue decomposition. To enhance downstream performance, it incorporates graph regularization and centering constraints to preserve geometric and target-variable information. Experiments on image classification show NKI outperforms linear methods under nonlinear dimensionality reduction, with further gains from target-aware regularization, while also revealing that dimensionality reduction choices significantly impact both accuracy and reconstruction risk.
为在不直接共享原始数据的前提下分析分散的保密数据集，本文提出非线性核集成（NKI）方法以提升隐私保护与下游任务性能。该方法在线性核集成（LKI）基础上进行核化，支持非线性数据变换与集成，通过核岭回归与特征值问题实现全局最优解。为增强下游分析能力，引入图正则化与中心化约束以保留几何结构与目标变量信息。图像分类实验表明，NKI在非线性降维下优于线性方法，结合目标变量感知正则化可进一步提升准确率，同时发现降维方式对准确率与重构风险均有显著影响。
Jacobian-Velocity Bounds for Deployment Risk Under Covariate Drift
Authors: Jonathan R. Landers
First: 2026-05-06T13:57:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T16:04:20+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 4 figures, 4 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study long-horizon deployment of a frozen predictor under dynamic covariate shift. A time-domain Poincare inequality first reduces temporal risk volatility to derivative energy. A Jacobian-velocity theorem then supplies the corresponding pathwise control. Given explicit regularity and domination assumptions, the theorem identifies directional tangent energy along the deployment path as the governing quantity. Under low-rank drift, that quantity reduces to directional Jacobian energy in the drift subspace, motivating drift-aligned tangent regularization (DTR) and a matched monitoring proxy. Rather than smoothing the network isotropically, DTR penalizes sensitivity only along estimated drift directions. We validate the theorem-to-method pipeline in four experiments: a synthetic benchmark for the time-domain inequality, a controlled synthetic comparison against isotropic Jacobian regularization, and two frozen-deployment studies on the UCI Air Quality and Tetouan power-consumption datasets. DTR reduces risk volatility and directional gain in the controlled low-rank regime and beats isotropic smoothing there. It also gives validation-selected deployment gains on both real datasets, with the Air Quality subspace estimated from target-orthogonal sensor motion. Moderate drift-subspace misspecification is tolerable while orthogonal misspecification largely removes the benefit.
中文标题/摘要
标题：协变量漂移下部署风险的雅可比-速度界限
我们研究在动态协变量偏移下冻结预测器的长期部署问题。首先，时间域庞加莱不等式将时间风险波动性降低为导数能量；随后，雅可比-速度定理提供相应的路径控制。在明确的正则性和支配假设下，该定理将部署路径上的方向切线能量识别为主导量。在低秩漂移情形下，该量简化为漂移子空间中的方向雅可比能量，从而启发了漂移对齐切线正则化（DTR）及匹配的监控代理。与各向同性平滑不同，DTR仅对估计漂移方向上的敏感性施加惩罚。我们在四个实验中验证了定理到方法的转化流程：一个用于时间域不等式的合成基准、一个与各向同性雅可比正则化对比的受控合成实验，以及两个在UCI空气质量与Tetouan电力消耗数据集上的冻结部署实验。在受控低秩情形下，DTR降低了风险波动性和方向增益，并优于各向同性平滑；在真实数据集上，DTR也实现了验证选择的部署增益，其中空气质量子空间由目标正交的传感器运动估计得到。适度的漂移子空间误设可容忍，而正交误设则基本消除了该优势。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of deploying frozen predictors under dynamic covariate drift, this work derives a Jacobian-velocity theorem that links temporal risk volatility to directional tangent energy along deployment paths. Under low-rank drift assumptions, this energy simplifies to directional Jacobian energy in the drift subspace, leading to drift-aligned tangent regularization (DTR) that penalizes sensitivity only along estimated drift directions. Experimental validation across four settings—including synthetic benchmarks and real-world UCI datasets—shows DTR reduces risk volatility and directional gain more effectively than isotropic Jacobian regularization in controlled low-rank regimes, and achieves deployment gains on real data even with moderate misspecification of drift subspaces.
为了解决在输入分布动态变化下部署冻结机器学习模型的风险问题，本文构建了一个理论框架以界定协变量漂移下的部署风险。通过时域Poincare不等式将时间风险波动转化为导数能量，再结合Jacobian-velocity定理推导出路径级控制，其核心是方向切线能量。在低秩漂移条件下，该能量简化为方向Jacobian能量，由此提出漂移对齐切线正则化（DTR），仅对估计漂移方向上的敏感度进行惩罚。实验表明，DTR在可控低秩场景下有效降低风险波动和方向增益，优于各向同性Jacobian正则化，并在真实数据集上实现部署增益，即使在漂移子空间存在中等误估时仍具鲁棒性。
AI Agent for Reverse-Engineering Legacy Finite-Difference Code and Translating to Devito
Authors: Yinghan Hou, Zongyou Yang
First: 2026-01-26T11:31:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T16:03:59+00:00
Comments: 14 pages, 7 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
To facilitate the transformation of legacy finite difference implementations into the Devito environment, this study develops an integrated AI agent framework. Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) and open-source Large Language Models are combined through multi-stage iterative workflows in the system's hybrid LangGraph architecture. The agent constructs an extensive Devito knowledge graph through document parsing, structure-aware segmentation, extraction of entity relationships, and Leiden-based community detection. GraphRAG optimisation enhances query performance across semantic communities that include seismic wave simulation, computational fluid dynamics, and performance tuning libraries. A reverse engineering component derives three-level query strategies for RAG retrieval through static analysis of Fortran source code. To deliver precise contextual information for language model guidance, the multi-stage retrieval pipeline performs parallel searching, concept expansion, community-scale retrieval, and semantic similarity analysis. Code synthesis is governed by Pydantic-based constraints to guarantee structured outputs and reliability. A comprehensive validation framework integrates conventional static analysis with the G-Eval approach, covering execution correctness, structural soundness, mathematical consistency, and API compliance. The overall agent workflow is implemented on the LangGraph framework and adopts concurrent processing to support quality-based iterative refinement and state-aware dynamic routing. The principal contribution lies in the incorporation of feedback mechanisms motivated by reinforcement learning, enabling a transition from static code translation toward dynamic and adaptive analytical behavior.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于逆向工程遗留有限差分代码并翻译为Devito的AI代理
为促进遗留有限差分实现向Devito环境的转换，本研究开发了一种集成式AI代理框架。该系统采用混合LangGraph架构，结合检索增强生成（RAG）与开源大语言模型，通过多阶段迭代工作流实现。代理通过文档解析、结构感知分割、实体关系提取及基于Leiden的社区检测，构建了广泛的Devito知识图谱。GraphRAG优化提升了在包含地震波模拟、计算流体力学及性能调优库等语义社区中的查询性能。逆向工程组件通过Fortran源代码的静态分析，推导出三级查询策略以支持RAG检索。为提供语言模型所需的精确上下文信息，多阶段检索管道执行并行搜索、概念扩展、社区级检索及语义相似性分析。代码合成受Pydantic约束控制，以确保结构化输出与可靠性。综合验证框架结合传统静态分析与G-Eval方法，覆盖执行正确性、结构合理性、数学一致性及API合规性。整体代理工作流基于LangGraph框架实现，并采用并发处理支持基于质量的迭代优化与状态感知动态路由。主要贡献在于引入强化学习驱动的反馈机制，实现从静态代码翻译向动态自适应分析行为的转变。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to automate the conversion of legacy finite-difference code into the Devito framework, this study introduces an AI agent that combines Retrieval-Augmented Generation with open-source LLMs within a LangGraph architecture. The agent builds a Devito knowledge graph via document parsing, structural segmentation, entity relationship extraction, and Leiden community detection, then optimizes queries using GraphRAG across domains like seismic simulation and CFD. A reverse-engineering module extracts multi-level query strategies from Fortran code, while a multi-stage retrieval pipeline performs parallel searches, concept expansion, and semantic similarity analysis to guide synthesis. Output is constrained via Pydantic to ensure structural correctness, and validation combines static analysis with G-Eval to verify correctness, structure, math, and API compliance. The agent’s iterative, state-aware workflow, enhanced by reinforcement learning-inspired feedback, enables dynamic adaptation during translation.
为实现将遗留有限差分代码自动转换为Devito框架，本研究提出一个结合检索增强生成与开源大语言模型的AI代理系统，基于LangGraph架构构建。代理通过文档解析、结构感知分割、实体关系抽取及Leiden社区检测构建Devito知识图谱，并利用GraphRAG优化跨领域（如地震波模拟、流体力学、性能调优）的语义查询。逆向工程模块从Fortran源码中提取三级查询策略，多阶段检索管道并行搜索、概念扩展、社区级检索与语义相似度分析以引导模型。代码合成通过Pydantic约束确保结构正确性，验证结合静态分析与G-Eval评估执行正确性、结构合理性、数学一致性与API合规性。代理采用基于强化学习反馈的迭代式、状态感知工作流，支持动态自适应翻译。
Qiskit QuantumKatas: Adapting Microsoft's Quantum Computing exercises for LLM evaluation
Authors: Juan Cruz-Benito, Ismael Faro
First: 2026-05-26T16:02:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T16:02:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We adapt Microsoft's QuantumKatas -- a well-established quantum computing curriculum -- from Q# to Qiskit, the most widely-adopted quantum computing framework, and package it with an evaluation framework for systematic LLM assessment. The resulting benchmark comprises 350 tasks across 26 categories, spanning fundamental gates through advanced algorithms (Grover's, Simon's, Deutsch-Jozsa), error correction, key distribution, and quantum games. Each task includes a natural language prompt, canonical solution, and deterministic test verification via classical circuit simulation. By building on the QuantumKatas' proven pedagogical design rather than creating tasks from scratch, we inherit a principled difficulty progression and comprehensive concept coverage while contributing the framework adaptation, evaluation infrastructure, and empirical analysis. We evaluate 16 LLMs across 7 prompting configurations -- a total of 39,200 model runs -- to demonstrate the benchmark's utility. Three key findings emerge: (1) the benchmark effectively differentiates model capabilities, with best-configuration pass rates ranging from 32.3% to 83.1% and a 26.1 pp average gap between frontier and open-source models; (2) models perform well at implementing known algorithms (SimonsAlgorithm 82.1%, BasicGates 81.6%) but struggle with problem encoding (SolveSATWithGrover 34.4%, DistinguishUnitaries 40.0%); and (3) chain-of-thought prompting shows a modestly bimodal effect -- it is the best strategy for three models (two of them explicitly reasoning-tuned per vendor documentation) but degrades performance for the rest, leaving it mid-pack in aggregate (56.3% mean) behind few-shot-5 (57.8%). We release the benchmark, evaluation framework, and baseline results to support research on LLM capabilities in quantum computing.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Qiskit量子练习题：将微软量子计算练习题适配用于大语言模型评估
我们将微软广受认可的量子计算课程QuantumKatas从Q#语言迁移至Qiskit（当前最广泛采用的量子计算框架），并配套构建评估框架，以实现对大语言模型（LLM）的系统性评估。该基准包含350个任务，覆盖26个类别，从基础门电路到高级算法（Grover算法、Simon算法、Deutsch-Jozsa算法）、纠错、密钥分发及量子博弈。每个任务均包含自然语言提示、标准解法及通过经典电路模拟进行的确定性测试验证。我们基于QuantumKatas已验证的教学设计而非从零创建任务，从而继承其严谨的难度递进与全面的概念覆盖，同时贡献框架适配、评估基础设施及实证分析。我们对16个LLM在7种提示配置下进行评估——共计39,200次模型运行——以验证基准的有效性。主要发现如下：（1）该基准能有效区分模型能力，最佳配置通过率介于32.3%至83.1%之间，前沿模型与开源模型平均差距达26.1个百分点；（2）模型在实现已知算法方面表现良好（Simon算法82.1%，基础门电路81.6%），但在问题编码方面表现不佳（Grover求解SAT问题34.4%，区分酉算子40.0%）；（3）思维链提示策略呈现中等双峰效应——对三个模型（其中两个明确为推理调优模型）效果最佳，但对其他模型表现下降，整体平均得分（56.3%）低于五样本少样本提示（57.8%）。我们公开发布该基准、评估框架及基线结果，以支持大语言模型在量子计算领域能力的研究。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for a standardized, pedagogically grounded benchmark to evaluate LLMs on quantum computing tasks, the authors adapted Microsoft's QuantumKatas from Q# to Qiskit and integrated an evaluation framework, creating a 350-task benchmark across 26 categories. Each task includes a natural language prompt, canonical solution, and classical simulation verification. Evaluated across 16 LLMs and 7 prompting strategies (39,200 runs), results show the benchmark effectively distinguishes model capabilities, with pass rates from 32.3% to 83.1% and a 26.1 percentage point gap between frontier and open-source models. Models excel at implementing known algorithms (e.g., 82.1% for Simon’s algorithm) but struggle with problem encoding (e.g., 34.4% for Grover-based SAT solving). Chain-of-thought prompting helps only three models, yielding mid-pack aggregate performance (56.3%) compared to few-shot-5 (57.8%).
为评估大语言模型在量子计算领域的表现，研究者将微软的QuantumKatas从Q#迁移到主流框架Qiskit，并配套评估系统，构建包含350道题、覆盖26个主题的基准测试集。每道题含自然语言描述、标准解答及经典电路模拟验证。在16个模型与7种提示策略（共39,200次运行）的测试中，前沿模型平均比开源模型高26.1个百分点，通过率介于32.3%至83.1%。模型在实现已知算法（如Simon算法82.1%）表现优异，但在问题编码（如Grover求解SAT仅34.4%）上困难重重。思维链提示仅对三个模型有效，整体平均通过率56.3%，低于少样本提示（57.8%）
Learning to Act under Noise: Enhancing Agent Robustness via Noisy Environments
Authors: Yuxin Chen, Xiaodong Cai, Junfeng Fang, Zhuowen Han, Yu Wang, Yaorui Shi, Yi Zhang, Qi Gu, Xunliang Cai, Xiang Wang, An Zhang, Tat-Seng Chua
First: 2026-05-26T16:02:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-26T16:02:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have facilitated the widespread deployment of LLMs as interactive agents capable of reasoning, planning, and tool use. Despite strong performance on existing benchmarks, such agents often exhibit notable degradation when deployed in real-world settings, where environments are inherently stochastic and imperfect. We argue that this discrepancy arises from a fundamental mismatch between idealized training settings and real-world interaction dynamics, where current paradigms rely on carefully curated task instructions and stable, well-controlled environments. To address this gap, we propose NoisyAgent, an agentic training framework that explicitly incorporates environmental imperfections into the agent learning process. We identify two major sources of interaction noise in real-world scenarios: user noise, which captures ambiguity and variability in user interaction, and tool noise, which reflects failures and anomalies in tool execution. We introduce such perturbations into the training pipeline by modifying user interaction patterns and simulating tool execution results within the training environment. To stabilize training while encouraging agents to handle increasingly challenging imperfections, noise is applied to only a subset of rollouts and progressively increased in difficulty as the model adapts to the current noise level. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach consistently improves agent robustness under noisy and dynamic environments. Our analysis reveals that training under noise conditions also yields performance gains on idealized benchmarks, suggesting that controlled exposure to environmental noise promotes more generalizable reasoning and decision-making behaviors. Our findings highlight the importance of modeling interaction imperfections for bridging the gap between agent training and real-world deployment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：在噪声环境下学习行动：通过嘈杂环境增强智能体鲁棒性
大型语言模型（LLMs）的最新进展推动了其作为可进行推理、规划和工具使用的交互式智能体的广泛应用。尽管在现有基准测试中表现优异，但这些智能体在真实世界部署时往往出现显著性能下降，因为现实环境本质上具有随机性和不完美性。我们认为，这一差异源于理想化训练环境与真实世界交互动态之间的根本性不匹配，当前范式依赖于精心设计的任务指令和稳定可控的环境。为解决这一差距，我们提出NoisyAgent框架，该框架在智能体学习过程中显式纳入环境不完美性。我们识别出真实场景中两种主要的交互噪声来源：用户噪声，捕捉用户交互中的模糊性和变异性；工具噪声，反映工具执行中的失败与异常。我们通过修改用户交互模式并在训练环境中模拟工具执行结果，将这些扰动引入训练流程。为在稳定训练的同时鼓励智能体应对日益复杂的不完美性，我们仅对部分rollout施加噪声，并随着模型适应当前噪声水平逐步增加难度。大量实验表明，我们的方法在嘈杂和动态环境中持续提升智能体的鲁棒性。分析显示，噪声训练条件下智能体在理想化基准测试中也获得性能提升，表明受控的环境噪声暴露有助于培养更具泛化能力的推理与决策行为。我们的研究强调，建模交互不完美性对于弥合智能体训练与真实世界部署之间的差距至关重要。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observed performance drop of large language model agents in real-world stochastic environments, this paper proposes NoisyAgent, a training framework that intentionally introduces environmental noise to enhance robustness. It models two key noise sources—user noise (ambiguity in interaction) and tool noise (tool execution failures)—by perturbing user inputs and simulating tool errors during training. Noise is applied selectively and incrementally to stabilize learning while progressively increasing difficulty as the agent adapts. Experiments show that agents trained under such noisy conditions perform better in dynamic, imperfect environments and also achieve improved results on standard benchmarks, indicating that controlled exposure to noise fosters more generalizable reasoning.
为弥合大语言模型代理在理想化训练与现实噪声环境中的性能差距，本文提出NoisyAgent框架，通过在训练中引入环境噪声提升鲁棒性。该框架模拟两类噪声——用户噪声（交互歧义）与工具噪声（工具执行失败）——通过扰动用户输入和模拟工具异常来实现。噪声以渐进、选择性方式施加，随模型适应当前噪声水平逐步增加难度。实验表明，该方法在噪声环境中显著提升代理性能，同时在干净基准上也获得意外提升，说明适度暴露于环境噪声有助于形成更泛化的推理能力。
